Motivation: 现有目标识别方法通常要么依赖高层符号信息、忽略连续运动细节，要么依赖难以获取且难以迁移的显式动力学模型。它们也往往没有学习代理如何朝不同候选目标移动，因此在轨迹前缀含糊、最需要运动先验的阶段容易误判或过度确信。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的航点识别方法。该方法学习轨迹先验，并针对每个候选目标运行一条经热启动的去噪链；通过闭式对数似然梯度，使生成轨迹与已观测轨迹相匹配；最后依据贝叶斯规则重新加权样本，得到经过校准的目标后验分布。

Result: 在模拟机器人、行人轨迹和车辆轨迹三类场景中，研究使用针对不同失效模式的四项指标进行评估。结果显示，该方法在目标难以区分的轨迹前缀阶段，能够维持经过校准的多模态信念；相比之下，基于滤波器或分类器的观察方法更容易坍缩为单一模式或产生过度自信。

Conclusion: 学习到的连续轨迹先验可以有效辅助目标识别，无须依赖显式动力学模型。该方法的核心优势是在观测信息不足时保留多个合理目标及其不确定性，从而提升部分轨迹条件下的推断可靠性。

Abstract: Goal recognition is the task of inferring an agent's intended goal from its observed behavior. Most existing work focuses on high-level symbolic recognition, abstracting away the continuous motion through which agents reach their goal. Methods that do reason over continuous trajectories rely on an explicit dynamics model that is hard to acquire and rarely transfers across settings. Neither approach explicitly learns how agents move toward each candidate goal, limiting accuracy on ambiguous trajectory prefixes where such knowledge is most needed.
  We present WoRD (Waypoint Recognition with Diffusion), which uses a learned trajectory prior to infer the agent's goal from partial observations. At each step, WoRD runs one warm-started denoising chain per candidate goal, steers each chain toward the observed trajectory through a closed-form log-likelihood gradient, and reweights the resulting samples by Bayes' rule into a calibrated posterior over goals. We evaluate WoRD on simulated robot settings, pedestrian trajectories, and vehicle trajectories using four metrics targeting distinct failure modes. Across all three settings, the same inference procedure preserves calibrated multimodal beliefs through ambiguous prefixes where filter- and classifier-based observers collapse to a single mode or grow overconfident.

</details>


### [169] [Agent Trust Infrastructure (ATI): A Federated Identity, Trust, Attestation, and Accountability Architecture for Cross-Organization Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23202)
*Viet K. Nguyen,Mohammad I. Husain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨组织智能体信任基础设施，通过证书、远程证明和授权策略验证智能体身份、执行完整性及权限；实验显示攻击遏制率显著提升，单次请求增加约一百七十四微秒开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体日益跨越组织边界协作并调用外部工具，但现有框架缺乏统一机制来核验智能体身份、执行环境是否可信以及请求是否获准。若组织间没有可靠的信任基础，恶意或受篡改的智能体可能借协作关系实施攻击。

Method: 提出一种与智能体框架无关的外部安全架构，结合公钥基础设施、零信任架构与远程证明。各组织通过彼此认可的证书颁发机构建立联邦信任，无须共享根证书机构。处理请求前，系统验证智能体证书及经批准的模型、工具和系统提示配置，并执行三项授权策略检查。该架构不修改现有框架或底层模型；身份与请求验证采用工作负载令牌格式，完整性验证采用实体证明令牌。

Result: 在由八个组织、六种智能体框架构成的模拟联邦中，研究者针对两个组织评估了十四种攻击情景和六项正常任务，并使用五种模型、五种攻击变体及每种变体三次重复实验。启用全部防护机制后，攻击遏制率从百分之四十三升至百分之八十四；在所有配置均一致评估的十一个攻击情景中，则从百分之四十升至百分之八十。每次请求的额外开销约为一百七十四微秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在不改动现有智能体框架的情况下，外置式信任基础设施能够显著增强跨组织智能体协作的安全性，同时保持较低的请求开销。不过，评估基于模拟联邦和裁判模型判断；其在真实部署中的效果、规模扩展能力及策略配置可靠性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly collaborate across organizational boundaries, invoking tools and interacting with agents operated by independent entities. Although existing agent frameworks support planning and coordination, they lack a common mechanism for verifying cross-organizational agent identity, execution integrity, and authorization. We propose the Agent Trust Infrastructure (ATI), a framework-independent security architecture built on Public Key Infrastructure (PKI), Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA), and remote attestation. ATI establishes federated trust through peer organizational certificate authorities (AgentCAs), eliminating the need for a shared root authority. Before executing requests, ATI validates agent certificates, verifies approved model, tool, and system-prompt configurations, and enforces authorization through three policy checks. We implement ATI as an external security enforcement layer that integrates with existing agent frameworks without modifying their underlying models. We evaluate ATI in a simulated federation of eight organizations using six agent frameworks, each deploying a five-agent team. Security experiments involve two organizations and assess 14 attack scenarios and six benign tasks across five agent models, using five attack variations and three repetitions per variation. A panel of judge models evaluates the experimental outcomes. With all enforcement mechanisms enabled, attack containment increases from 43% to 84% (40% to 80% for the 11 attack scenarios evaluated consistently across all configurations), while enforcement introduces approximately 174 microseconds of overhead per request. ATI is released as an open-source, framework-independent package that uses IETF WIMSE workload token formats for identity and request verification and Entity Attestation Tokens for model, tool, and system-prompt integrity verification.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Beyond Bitwidth: Deployment-Constrained Mixed-Precision Quantization for Multilingual LLM Inference on NVIDIA Blackwell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21974)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究在英伟达数据中心图形处理器上评估通义千问三代八十亿参数模型的低比特量化。最终混合精度方案通过预设质量、显存和延迟门槛，但实验也显示，极低比特量化可能导致生成退化，且配置效果难以简单归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化只有在模型保留实际服务所需的准确率、能够通过受支持的图形处理器内核运行，并满足实测显存与延迟限制时才有价值。论文旨在系统检验量化方案是否同时满足这些实际部署条件，而非仅关注理论压缩率或单项精度。

Method: 以通义千问三代八十亿参数模型为对象，在英伟达数据中心图形处理器上使用推理框架进行评估，测试二位、三位、四位及八位量化和混合精度配置。研究核验了全部一百四十四个融合线性模块的打包执行与精度，并按预先设定的门槛评估韩语、英语、数值和推理能力。另通过模块恢复实验考察各模块的影响，并在未参与选择的最终评测集上独立验证候选配置。

Result: 所有测试的二位量化网格虽能执行，但七种配置在生成时均出现退化。单独恢复模块的效果并非简单相加；韩语与推理任务对模块的敏感度排序也不一致。一个表现有希望的四位／八位候选方案未通过独立最终评测。最终通过门槛的方案采用十六位激活值、九十八个四位浮点权重模块、四十六个八位整数模块及八位整数输出头。在包含五千一百六十八个项目的最终集上，其权重加载显存为五点九一七吉比字节，低于五点九八吉比字节上限；韩语教师判定正确样本的保留率为零点九零四，高于零点九零门槛；每输出令牌时间为基准的零点九三九倍。相较于四位权重与四位激活的方案，配对准确率提高一点四个百分点，校正后的区间为正零点二至正二点六个百分点；选择集上的提升为三点四个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，经过任务导向评估和混合精度配置，低比特量化可以在显存与延迟约束下保留服务所需的质量；但二位量化在本实验中不适用于生成，且局部模块分析不能可靠地预测整体效果。最终方案与四位权重、四位激活基线的比较同时改变了激活精度和八位保护模块，因此不能确定提升分别来自何处；探索性分析认为激活精度变化可能贡献较大，但模块分配的独立作用尚未证实。此外，最终检查点未保留，逐位一致的重建可复现性仍未验证。

Abstract: Low-bit LLM quantization is useful only if a model preserves service-relevant accuracy, executes through supported GPU kernels, and meets measured memory and latency limits. We study Qwen3-8B on NVIDIA B200 with vLLM, including INT2/INT3, GPTQ, NVFP4, and mixed-precision configurations. We verify native packed execution and precision on all 144 fused linear modules and evaluate Korean, English, numerical, and reasoning quality under preregistered gates. Packed INT2 and INT3 execute, but all seven tested INT2 grids degenerate on generation. Isolated module-restoration effects are strongly non-additive; Korean and reasoning sensitivity rankings disagree, and a promising INT4/INT8 development candidate fails independent final evaluation. A configuration with 16-bit activations, 98 NVFP4 weight-only modules, 46 INT8 modules, and an INT8 output head passes all gates on the untouched FINAL-81 set (5,168 items): 5.917 GiB of weight-loading memory (limit 5.98), Korean teacher-correct retention 0.904 (gate 0.90), and TPOT ratio 0.939 relative to BF16. It improves paired accuracy over NVFP4 W4A4 by +1.4 percentage points (Bonferroni-adjusted interval [+0.2, +2.6]), compared with +3.4 points on the selection set. This comparison changes both activation precision and INT8 protection; exploratory analyses suggest that the activation change contributes substantially, but the isolated allocation effect is not established. Final checkpoints were not retained, and bitwise rebuild reproducibility remains unverified.

</details>


### [171] [SIMQ: A Single Graph-based Index for Multi-Vector Query](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22079)
*Yifan Lu,Jiabao Jin,Xiaoyao Zhong,Peng Cheng,Xiangyu Wang,Haotian Li,Hua Chen,Junqing Chen,Wei Xi,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多向量检索的单图索引框架，通过数据感知聚类与逐查询词图搜索近似最大相似度评分，降低计算开销，并以计数评分和动态候选集增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索将文档表示为多个词元级向量，检索效果较强，但计算成本高，难以扩展到大规模语料。现有近似方法要么需要为每个查询计算完整的质心交互矩阵并依赖图形处理器聚类，要么采用与数据无关的压缩，导致召回率不稳定且需反复补偿。

Method: 该框架通过数据感知采样，将词元向量划分为细粒度簇；随后在簇质心上建立单一图索引，不依赖既有方法的残差表示。查询时，对每个查询词元分别进行图搜索，以近似最大相似度聚合评分，避免构造交互矩阵，也无需图形处理器加速。其扩展版本进一步采用基于计数的评分和动态候选集。理论分析在流形假设及成功检索到相应簇的条件下，证明数据感知划分的期望近似误差小于与数据无关的划分。

Result: 论文在五个基准数据集上，将该方法与多种基线方法进行召回率、吞吐量和构建时间比较，实验主要在中央处理器上进行；大规模基线质心的聚类则在独立的图形处理器机器上完成。摘要未提供具体数值，因此无法判断各项指标的提升幅度。该框架已集成到蚂蚁集团开源的向量检索库中。

Conclusion: 该工作尝试以数据感知的簇划分和逐词元图导航，将单向量图索引扩展到多向量检索，在避免完整交互矩阵和图形处理器依赖的同时，提供理论误差分析与面向不同查询条件的鲁棒性增强。其实际优势仍需结合论文中的具体实验数值评估。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness by representing documents as sets of token-level vectors and scoring via MaxSim, but their high computational cost limits scalability to large corpora. Existing approximate methods face fundamental bottlenecks: centroid interaction matrix approaches require computing a full matrix at every query and rely on GPU-accelerated clustering, while data-oblivious compression methods yield unstable recall that requires repeated compensation. We propose SIMQ (Single graph Index for Multi-vector Query), a framework that bridges single-vector graph indices and multi-vector retrieval through data-aware sampling-based clustering and per-token graph navigation, simultaneously bypassing all aforementioned bottlenecks. SIMQ organizes all token vectors into fine-grained clusters via data-aware sampling, builds a single graph index over cluster centroids independently of PLAID's residual representation, and approximates the MaxSim score through per-token graph search without materializing any interaction matrix or relying on GPU acceleration. We provide theoretical analysis showing that, under a manifold assumption and successful cell retrieval, data-aware partitioning has a smaller expected approximation error than a data-oblivious partition. Building on this foundation, SIMQ+ further introduces count-based scoring and a dynamic candidate set to improve robustness across varying query conditions. Experiments on five benchmarks compare recall, throughput, and construction time against several baselines, primarily on CPU; large-scale PLAID k-means centroids are built on a separate GPU machine. SIMQ has been integrated into VSAG, an Ant Group's open-source vector search library.

</details>


### [172] [Cluster-Guided Decoding with Trust-Region Constraints for Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22163)
*Gezheng Wen,Chao Shen,Raviteja Anantha,Tan Shen Cliff Chung,Layne C. Price*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用语义聚类先验直接引导个性化物品排序的方法，在多个数据集上提升了推荐效果，并能以毫秒级延迟提供推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数推荐系统独立评估目录中的每个物品，无法明确控制推荐结果所属的语义区域；能够提供这种控制的高表达能力模型，往往难以在完整物品目录上快速进行在线推理。此外，仅用聚类筛选候选物品，并不能让聚类结构直接影响最终排序。

Method: 该方法学习分层语义簇，并构造簇级先验，将其直接融入物品评分规则，使聚类结构参与排序，而非仅用于候选集预筛选。先验由簇中心构成，不依赖用户逐物品交互统计，因此即使冷启动用户没有历史记录，仍可定义并用于推荐。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，该方法相较于传统、神经网络及大语言模型基线，在召回率和归一化折损累计增益方面表现更好；随着物品目录变得更加稀疏，性能优势进一步扩大。在线服务延迟为毫秒级。

Conclusion: 将语义簇先验直接纳入物品排序，可以兼顾语义区域引导、冷启动适用性与低延迟推理。实验结果表明，该方法尤其适合目录稀疏场景，并有望用于低延迟的第一阶段召回。

Abstract: Most recommenders score each item in the catalog separately, and do not explicitly model which semantic region of the catalog a recommendation belongs to. Models expressive enough to offer that control are often too slow to run over a full catalog for online inference. In this paper, we present Cluster-Guided Generative Reconstruction (CG2R), which learns a hierarchy of semantic clusters and uses a cluster-level prior to steer item ranking for personalized recommendations. Unlike existing systems that only use clusters to pre-select candidates, CG2R adds the cluster prior directly to the item scoring rule, so that the learned cluster structure shapes the ranking itself. Because the prior is assembled from cluster centroids rather than per-item statistics, it remains defined even for cold-start users when no history is available. Across the three benchmark datasets evaluated, CG2R outperforms classical, neural, and LLM-based baselines in terms of Recall and nDCG, with its performance gains widening as the catalog grows sparser. In addition, CG2R serves recommendations in milliseconds, suggesting potential as low-latency first-stage retrieval.

</details>


### [173] [Teaching Language Models to Narrow Boolean Patent Searches from Search Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22270)
*Seiya Kawano,Koji Marusaki,Hirofumi Nonaka,Koichiro Yoshino*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究利用日本专利厅的检索报告训练模型生成布尔检索策略：逐行奖励有助于缩小含引文的候选集，策略级偏好学习则更擅长提高引文召回率，两者各有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利现有技术检索需要在尽量找到相关文献的同时，将候选文献集缩小到人工能够筛查的规模。提示模型虽能生成有效且召回率较高的检索策略，却常留下过大的候选集，难以实际筛查。

Method: 研究将日本专利厅的三万八千三百九十七份检索报告提炼为可执行的专家检索策略，并重新运行这些策略。模型生成的策略以引文召回率及每条引文所在的最小候选集规模进行评估。随后，研究采用两种离线适配方式：一是依据兼顾筛查效率的策略整体评分进行学习；二是依据逐行奖励进行学习，对能保留已找到引文、同时缩小候选集的查询行给予更高奖励。

Result: 在相同的采样结果上，逐行奖励在候选集筛查指标上优于策略整体评分。经过迭代优化，含引文候选集规模的中位数从七百六十八篇降至五十篇以下；与专家策略相比，更多引文落在少于一百篇文献的候选集中，但召回率较低。策略级偏好学习则能达到接近完整的引文召回率。

Conclusion: 逐行奖励更能推动检索策略缩小含相关引文的候选集，策略级偏好学习则更适合追求高召回率。两种方法优化目标不同、彼此互补，可结合使用以平衡检索完整性与人工筛查成本。

Abstract: Patent prior-art searchers issue Boolean queries line by line, broadening to find relevant documents and narrowing until candidates can be screened. We distill 38,397 Japan Patent Office search reports into executable expert strategies, re-execute them, and evaluate generated strategies by citation recall and the smallest set containing each citation. Prompted models write valid, high-recall strategies but leave citations in sets too large to screen. We adapt a generator offline from scored rollouts in two forms: strategy-level learning from a screening-aware strategy score, and line-level learning from citation-preserving narrowing credit, which upweights the query lines keeping a found citation in a smaller set. On shared rollouts, line-level credit outperforms strategy-level scores on screening measures; iterated, it cuts the median citation-containing set from 768 to under 50 and places more citations in sets under 100 documents than experts, at lower recall. Strategy-level preference learning reaches near-complete recall; the two forms are complementary.

</details>


### [174] [GuardAgent: A Memory-Augmented Agentic Framework for Adaptive Detection of Adversarial Promotions in LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22274)
*Shiyi Yang,Xinshu Li,Chen Wang,Xiwei Xu,Liming Zhu,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向大语言模型推荐系统的促销驱动攻击检测框架，通过持续感知、行动、反思与记忆来适应新型威胁，并提供可解释的检测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统主要将文本作为辅助信号，而大语言模型推荐系统则依赖文本进行语义理解、推理和推荐生成。这一变化扩大了攻击面，使促销驱动的操纵更容易实施、更有效且更难发现。针对这类攻击的检测研究仍不充分，因此论文旨在提升新一代推荐平台的安全性。

Method: 提出名为“守卫智能体”的大语言模型驱动检测框架。该框架按感知、行动、反思和记忆的循环运行，不采用静态、一次性且不透明的检测方式，而是通过持续学习和自我演进来适应尚未见过的威胁模式，并生成可解释的推理过程与检测结果。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，该框架取得了当前领先的泛化性能。摘要未提供具体数据集名称、评价指标或数值结果。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统探讨了大语言模型推荐系统中的促销驱动攻击检测，并验证了智能体式、持续演进且可解释的检测思路具有较强泛化能力，为构建更稳健、可信的实际推荐系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: In traditional recommender systems (RSs), textual information typically serves as auxiliary signals for representation learning. In contrast, in LLM-based RSs, textual information becomes the primary medium for recommendation generation through semantic understanding and reasoning. This shift fundamentally changes the attack surface, making promotion-driven manipulations more feasible, effective, and harder to detect. However, effectively detecting such attacks remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic study on detecting promotion-driven attacks against LLM-based RSs, aiming to safeguard next-generation recommendation platforms. Specifically, we propose GuardAgent, a novel LLM-powered agentic detection framework that operates through a loop of Perception, Action, Reflection and Memory. In contrast to conventional LLM-based detectors that function in a static, one-off, and opaque fashion, GuardAgent continually learns and self-evolves to adapt to unseen threat patterns while generating explainable reasoning traces and outcomes. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that GuardAgent achieves state-of-the-art generalization performance, laying the foundation for robust and trustworthy recommendation in practical settings.

</details>


### [175] [NONSENSE: Non-Semantic Strings Expose Semanticity Gaps in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22353)
*Ankit Gupta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现，密集检索器可能把明显缺乏语义的字符串评为高相关，甚至排在语料库首位，说明相似度排名与语义证据并不总是一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索广泛用于搜索、问答和检索增强生成，通常将高相似度视为语义相关的证据。本文旨在检验这一假设在多大程度上成立，并考察检索器的相似度分数是否可靠地反映语义。

Method: 提出一种黑盒稀疏编辑探针：针对给定查询生成明显不具语义的字符串，禁止复制查询词，并仅优化字符串与查询之间的密集相似度。随后在五个基准风格的数据集和三种常用单向量检索器上进行评估。

Result: 生成的无意义字符串经常获得高于检索器自身最佳人工判定相关段落的分数，并且常在完整语料库中排名第一。这表明在受测检索器中，最近邻排名可以与段落所表达的语义相分离。

Conclusion: 评估密集检索器时，不应只关注其能否找回相关文档，还应检验相似度分数是否稳定、可靠地对应语义证据。

Abstract: Dense retrieval is central to modern search, question answering, and retrieval-augmented generation, where high similarity is often treated as evidence of semantic relevance. We examine how far this assumption can be stressed. NONSENSE is a black-box sparse-edit probe that constructs visibly non-semantic strings while forbidding query-term copying, optimizing only the dense similarity between a query and the string. Across five BEIR-style datasets and three popular single-vector retrievers, these strings frequently outscore the retriever's own best judged query-relevant passage (QRP), and often rank first in the full corpus. The result indicates a semanticity gap in the evaluated dense retrievers: nearest-neighbor status can be separated from passage-like meaning. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating dense retrievers not only by whether they retrieve relevant documents, but also by how reliably their similarity scores reflect semantic evidence.

</details>


### [176] [Deal With It: On Better Schedules for Listwise Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22392)
*Benjamin Clavié,Herumb Shandilya,Hanna Lichtenberg,Aamir Shakir,Makoto P. Kato*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究如何将候选文档分组供大语言模型重排，提出按检索排名轮流分发文档的分组方式，可减少优质文档被配额截断及后续修复调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表式大语言模型重排器难以在单次调用中可靠处理数百篇文档，因此需要分轮、分组调度。现有并行调度为每组设置固定晋级配额；若某组集中包含较多高排名文档，配额便会截掉其中一部分，迫使系统进行昂贵的修复调用。此前，候选文档如何划分尚未得到充分分析。

Method: 论文分析配额可能成为限制的场景，并在简化的检索器模型下证明：当各组配额相同时，按检索排名轮流将候选文档分入各组（类似发牌）在期望意义上不会比其他划分截掉更多真实前十文档；连续切块则会随着检索器变强而截掉更多优质文档。基于此，作者提出一种简单的发牌式调度方案，并将其用于既有调度中的分组环节。

Result: 在六种大语言模型和两轮调度实验中，发牌式方案的前十名归一化折损累计增益与最佳调度平均相差不超过0.1分。首次调用并发、候选规模最高达一千篇时，该方案通常比闪电排序快1.1至1.4倍；在该场景下需要三轮，速度约为滑动窗口方案的11倍。在既有调度中采用发牌式分组后，触发修复调用的查询比例从59%降至14%，质量没有显著变化。配额为四时，随机划分表现与理论预测相近；但它依赖随机种子，并且在稠密检索器、配额为一时，前十名指标低1.3分。

Conclusion: 候选文档的分组方式会显著影响固定配额下的截断与修复成本。按检索排名轮流分组是一种简单、有效且理论上有依据的策略，能在基本维持排序质量的同时减少修复调用并提升速度。

Abstract: Listwise LLM rerankers cannot reliably rank hundreds of documents in a single call, and rely on schedules to orchestrate their calls. Parallel schedules split candidates into groups and advance a fixed quota from each. When a group holds more of the top passages than its quota, some are cut, and expensive repair calls become required. We show that the way candidates are split matters where such a quota can bind, and that, in expected cuts, the best split there deals them round-robin by retriever rank, like cards. Although not conceptually new, dealing has remained unanalyzed. We prove that, under a simple retriever model, no split with equal quotas cuts fewer of the true top ten on average, and that contiguous chunks cut more as the retriever improves. DealRank, the simplest schedule our analysis allows, is on average within 0.1 nDCG@10 points of the best schedules over six LLMs, in two rounds. It is consistently 1.1 to 1.4 times faster than BlitzRank with concurrent first calls up to a thousand candidates. There, it needs three rounds and is eleven times faster than the sliding window. In existing schedules such as BlitzRank, dealing reduces repair calls from 59% of queries to 14%, improving latency with no significant change in quality. Random partitioning appears equivalent at a quota of four, as predicted, but ties results to the seed and trails by 1.3 nDCG@10 points at a quota of one over a dense retriever.

</details>


### [177] [More Than a Score: Reinforcement Learning with Verbalized Feedback for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22410)
*Zipeng Chen,Xinyu Lin,Yuliang Sun,Sheng Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Xiaowen Shi,Junwei Pan,Chengguo Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用个性化文字反馈指导强化学习的生成式推荐框架，可提升推荐准确性、排序质量与候选多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习推荐方法通常依赖标量奖励引导探索，但奖励无法指出候选项目与用户偏好具体冲突之处，也难以说明下一步应如何调整候选。因此，模型难以获得明确、可操作的改进方向。

Method: 提出文字反馈引导的生成式推荐框架。训练时，用户模拟器根据用户历史偏好和候选项目特征生成个性化文字反馈，策略模型据此生成改进后的推荐。原始候选与改进候选组成扩展候选组，并通过组相对策略优化进行联合训练。用户模拟器仅参与训练，推理阶段将其移除。

Result: 在三个亚马逊评论数据集和两种生成式推荐基础模型上的实验表明，该方法提升了推荐准确性与排序质量。进一步分析显示，文字指导促进了对更多样化候选的探索，并帮助策略模型在训练过程中发现奖励更高的推荐结果。摘要未提供具体提升幅度。

Conclusion: 将针对候选内容的自然语言反馈纳入强化学习，能够为生成式推荐提供比单一标量奖励更具体的优化信号，并改善推荐效果与探索过程；该反馈机制无需在实际推理阶段保留。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for generative recommendation, where recommended items are generated directly from users' interaction histories. To further improve recommendation quality, reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to align these models with recommendation objectives. However, existing RL methods rely on scalar rewards to implicitly guide exploration. These rewards do not specify which aspects of a candidate conflict with the user's preferences or how the next candidate should differ. To address this gap, we propose VerRec, a verbalized feedback-guided RL framework for generative recommendation. Specifically, a user simulator generates personalized verbalized feedback based on the user's historical preferences and the candidate item profile. The policy uses this feedback to produce refined recommendations. Original and refined candidates form an expanded group and are jointly optimized with group relative policy optimization. The user simulator is only required during training and is removed at inference time. Experiments on three Amazon review datasets with two generative recommendation backbones demonstrate that VerRec improves recommendation accuracy and ranking quality. Further analyses show that verbalized guidance encourages exploration of more diverse candidates and helps the policy discover more rewarding recommendations during training.

</details>


### [178] [From Relevance to Utility: Rubric Alignment in Yuanbao Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22503)
*Yu Lu,Lixin Zou,Yangqi Chen,Hui Xu,Qiaolin Xia,Hao Wang,Qianwen Ma,Reed Li,Shuang Zeng,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出“准则对齐搜索”，以面向推理效用的统一监督协调检索、排序与文档集选择；在元宝搜索部署后，端到端回答质量较线上基线提升百分之八点五二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体搜索的目标不只是为单篇文档排序，还要为后续大语言模型推理组织出高效的文档集合。既有方法往往只优化搜索流程中的个别环节，或在上游相关性模型之上用强化学习选择文档，因而整个流程未能围绕推理效用进行一致优化。

Method: 针对每个查询，先构建由粗到细的评估准则，明确文档集合应满足的加权正向条件以及应避免的负向条件。随后生成两类训练信号：一是由轻量标注器将基于准则的判断转化为单篇文档效用标签，用于训练对比式双编码器和带有四个准则评分分支的多任务排序器；二是由准则条件化评估器计算文档集合层面的奖励，并据此训练双分支强化学习选择器，结合组相对策略优化与优选轨迹蒸馏，缓解子集优化中的信用分配问题。

Result: 该方法已部署于元宝搜索，端到端回答质量较线上生产基线提升百分之八点五二。摘要未提供更多实验设置、分项指标或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将查询准则作为贯穿检索、排序和集合选择的统一监督依据，使搜索系统从优化单篇文档相关性转向优化文档集合对下游推理的整体效用。线上结果显示这一端到端协同优化具有实际收益；其效果在其他场景中的可迁移性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Agentic search demands that a search engine move beyond per document relevance ranking to assemble document sets optimized for downstream LLM reasoning. Prior work addresses this either by improving individual pipeline stages in isolation or by applying RL based selection on top of relevance trained upstream modules, leaving the full stack unaligned to utility for reasoning. We propose rubric aligned search, a framework that unifies the traditional search pipeline under a single supervision surface. For each query, we derive a rubric from coarse to fine, specifying weighted positive criteria that the document set must satisfy and negative criteria that it must avoid. From this rubric we derive two training signals. First, a lightweight labeler distills rubric grounded judgments into per document utility labels, which supervise a contrastive dual encoder for retrieval and a multi task ranker trained with four rubric aligned scoring heads. Second, a rubric conditioned evaluator produces a set level reward that drives a dual branch RL selector, combining GRPO with best rollout distillation to resolve credit assignment in subset optimization. Deployed in Yuanbao Search, the pipeline improves end to end answer quality by 8.52% over the online production baseline.

</details>


### [179] [MIPR: A Benchmark for Reasoning-Intensive Multimodal Intent-to-Product Retrieval with Multi-Category Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22509)
*Qiang Li,Xuan Lu,Jian Ruan,Manxi Lin,Jiacheng Chen,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei,Xiaoyu Shen,Dan Ou,Xiangheng Kong,Junjun Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出多模态意图到商品检索基准 MIPR，评测用户未明确指定商品、且需求可由多个品类满足的检索场景；实验表明现有方法仍有明显提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实电商查询常表达的是用户意图，而非具体商品名称；同一意图也可能由多个商品品类满足。现有多模态检索基准通常假设目标明确，因此对这类复杂意图及多品类解决方案的评测不足。

Method: 构建 MIPR 基准，包含 3,522 条多模态查询和一个含 30,731 件商品的共享商品库。该基准强调需要推理的检索任务、可跨多个品类满足的查询，以及细粒度相关性标注，并评估向量嵌入检索与重排序方法。

Result: 在完整查询集上，表现最佳的向量嵌入检索器的前十项归一化折损累计增益为 49.23%；对具有多个解决方案品类的查询，其前十项解决方案品类召回率为 55.86%。加入重排序后，两项指标分别提升至 60.11% 和 67.00%，但与候选池预言机结果仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: MIPR 为多模态意图到商品检索提供了具有挑战性且具诊断价值的评测基准。实验显示，重排序能够改善检索效果，但现有方法仍未充分覆盖用户意图对应的多品类商品，后续研究仍有较大空间。

Abstract: Real-world e-commerce queries often express user intent without naming a target product, and a single intent may be fulfilled by products from multiple categories. However, existing multimodal retrieval benchmarks typically assume a relatively well-defined target, leaving retrieval for intents with multi-category solutions less explored. To fill this gap, we introduce MIPR, a benchmark for Multimodal Intent-to-Product Retrieval comprising 3,522 multimodal queries and a shared corpus of 30,731 products. MIPR features reasoning-intensive retrieval, multi-category solutions, and fine-grained relevance labels. Experiments show that the best embedding-based retriever achieves 49.23% nDCG@10 on the full query set, and 55.86% solution-category recall@10 on queries with multiple solution categories. Reranking improves these scores to 60.11% and 67.00%, respectively, yet substantial gaps to the candidate-pool oracle remain. These results establish MIPR as a challenging and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating multimodal intent-to-product retrieval. The data and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/leequeue/MIPR.

</details>


### [180] [Learning Cross-Domain Media Similarity from Amazon Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22756)
*Gabrielly Xavier dos Santos,Lucas Dias Cler,Luciana Fujii Pontello,Olga Goussevskaia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究利用公开评论中的语义与时间特征预测商品相似度，在书籍和电影领域验证了方法，并发现少量目标领域数据可显著改善跨领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同过滤依赖用户历史行为来计算物品相似度，但这类数据对独立系统和研究者而言往往稀疏或难以获取，跨媒体领域时尤为明显。研究旨在利用公开可得的信息，学习并推广物品间的相似关系。

Method: 从亚马逊商品评论中提取语义特征和时间特征作为模型输入，以用户评论历史中的商品共现关系作为监督目标，通过监督学习预测商品相似度。研究在书籍和电影两个领域评估模型，并分析不同特征组在领域内及跨领域设置中的作用。

Result: 实验显示，该方法具有较好的稳健性，在零样本跨领域迁移中也能取得积极效果。特征归因分析表明，领域内模型主要依赖语言语义信息，而跨领域模型则更多利用反映行为流行度的线索。训练时加入少量目标领域上下文，能够弥补领域数据稀疏带来的差距，并显著提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: 公开评论数据可用于学习可迁移的商品相似度，为缺少用户行为数据的推荐研究提供了可行途径。跨领域迁移时，模型所依赖的特征会发生变化；适量引入目标领域信息，有助于进一步提升方法的适用性与泛化表现。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering is a basic foundation of many recommendation algorithms. It works by analyzing a user's history and then suggesting items similar to those in that history. This method typically relies on user data to compute item similarity, data that is widely available to commercial systems but is usually sparse or inaccessible for independent systems and research, especially across multiple media domains. In this work, we propose a method to generalize similarity information between items using supervised learning. As input to our model, we use semantic and temporal features extracted from Amazon product reviews, which are publicly available. As the training target, we use item (product) co-occurrence in the reviewing history of Amazon customers. We evaluate the quality of the predicted similarities across two media domains: books and movies. Experimental results demonstrate the robustness of our methodology, yielding positive outcomes in zero-shot cross-domain transfer tasks. Furthermore, feature attribution analysis reveals a structural shift in how feature groups are utilized across setups: intra-domain models rely heavily on linguistic semantics, whereas cross-domain environments pivot toward behavioral popularity cues. Incorporating even limited target-domain context during training bridges this domain sparsity gap, significantly enhancing framework generalization.

</details>


### [181] [IntentGR: Enhancing Intent for End-to-End Personalized Generative Retrieval in Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22788)
*Gefan Ye,Yanjing Wu,Yinfu Feng,Zhiqiang Wang,Liang Li,Jun Xiao,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出意图增强型端到端生成式检索框架，通过在检索流程多个阶段强化查询语义，使生成结果更贴合用户当前搜索意图，并提升整体检索效果，尤其改善长尾查询表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化生成式检索需要兼顾用户的即时搜索意图与长期偏好。现有端到端方法虽能联合优化语义标识学习与序列生成，却往往只把查询作为普通输入特征，未充分利用查询来指导标识学习和生成过程。因此，生成结果容易偏向高频语义标识模式，而偏离当前查询意图。

Method: 提出意图增强型端到端框架，并从四个环节强化查询语义：查询感知的语义行为表示，将查询相关的行为证据纳入输入；查询—语义对齐，在优化阶段加强查询与语义标识的联系；长尾查询增强，改善训练分布中长尾查询的覆盖；查询条件注意力调制，在解码阶段根据查询调整注意力。

Result: 在公开的个性化搜索基准和大型工业电商数据集上，该方法相较最强基线的前十项召回率分别提升百分之八点一一和百分之十一点九六，并在长尾查询上取得显著收益。

Conclusion: 研究表明，为端到端生成式检索补充查询相关证据，并在标识优化、训练数据分布和解码等环节有效整合这些证据，有助于构建更能理解搜索意图的检索系统。

Abstract: Personalized generative retrieval in search should jointly satisfy the user's immediate search intent and long-term preference. Recent end-to-end approaches, primarily explored in recommendation, unify item tokenization and sequence generation within a single model, allowing semantic identifiers (SIDs) to be optimized toward the downstream generation objective. However, these methods often treat the query merely as an input feature, without explicitly leveraging it to guide either identifier learning or sequence generation. This leaves intent guidance weak in both the tokenization and the generation stage, causing generated semantic identifiers to drift toward high-frequency token patterns rather than the current search intent. In this work, we find that incorporating additional query-related evidence into the framework of end-to-end generative retrieval is important. Based on this finding, we propose IntentGR, an intent-enhanced end-to-end framework that strengthens query semantics at four complementary stages of the retrieval pipeline. Query-Aware Semantic Behavior Representation (QBR) introduces query-level behavioral evidence as a new input signal; Query-Semantic Alignment (QSA), Long-Tail Query Enrichment (LQE), and Query-conditioned Attention Modulation (QAM) then serve as integration mechanisms at the optimization, training-distribution, and decoding stages to fully exploit this evidence. Experiments on the public AOL4PS benchmark and AE, a large-scale industrial e-commerce dataset, show that IntentGR improves Recall@10 by 8.11% and 11.96% over the strongest baseline, with strong gains on long-tail queries. These results suggest that enriched query-related input evidence, together with effective integration mechanisms, is beneficial for building intent-aware end-to-end generative search systems.

</details>


### [182] [Better Rankers, Worse Retrievers: The Hidden Cost of RL Post-Training in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22838)
*Yuheng Chen,Fan Zhang,Yixuan Xu,Chao Yang,Yang Xiang,Can Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 强化学习后训练虽能提升推荐列表前部的准确性，却会损害数百个候选项范围内的召回率；在五个数据集上，加入列表外锚定损失后，模型保留前部收益并恢复原有召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型通常在监督微调后进行强化学习后训练，既有评估多关注前十项，但实际应用还需向后续排序器提供数百个候选项。论文指出，强化学习可能造成被忽视的代价：模型更擅长排序，却更难找全候选，因此需要系统研究这一召回损失及其成因。

Method: 作者在五个数据集上测量强化学习后训练对不同候选列表长度下召回率的影响，并通过受控实验追踪其原因。由于每位用户通常只记录一个后续点击商品，强化学习会把模型采样列表中的其他候选项赋予负优势，等同于将它们视为错误。作者据此提出“列表外锚定”：增加一项损失，使模型保留监督微调模型对采样列表之外商品的排序关系。

Result: 在五个数据集上，强化学习后训练均提升小规模列表的命中率，却降低数百个候选项范围内的召回率。实验表明，负向惩罚采样候选项是召回下降的原因之一：这些候选项往往是模型认为合理的替代项，也可能是历史相似用户的真实后续选择。加入列表外锚定后，模型保留列表前部的提升，并将召回率恢复至监督微调模型的水平；该方法在其他强化学习配置下也有效。

Conclusion: 强化学习后训练可能让生成式推荐模型成为更好的排序器、却成为更差的候选检索器。其根源在于稀疏的单一正例监督使合理替代项遭到错误惩罚。列表外锚定是一种简单有效的修正，能够兼顾前部准确性与整体候选召回率。

Abstract: Generative recommenders decode the semantic ID of a user's next item with a language model, and post-training them with reinforcement learning (RL) after supervised fine-tuning (SFT) has become standard practice. The gains are mostly reported on the first ten results, yet in deployment such models hand hundreds of candidates to a downstream ranker or reward model. We show that RL post-training has a hidden cost: it improves accuracy at the top of the list (hit@K for small K) but, on all five datasets we study, lowers the recall of the candidate list (hit@K for K in the hundreds). The model becomes a better ranker and a worse retriever. Controlled experiments trace the loss to the negative advantages that RL assigns to the candidates it samples, the rollout. The root cause is that only one next item is logged per user, so RL treats every other candidate as wrong. Yet these candidates are the model's most plausible alternatives, and often the next items of other users with similar histories. Penalising them pushes down exactly what similar users choose next, and these items drop out of those users' candidate lists. This diagnosis points directly to a remedy: out-of-rollout anchoring, a single additional loss term that preserves the SFT model's ordering of the items outside the rollout. It keeps the gain at the top and restores recall to the SFT level on all five datasets, and it is effective under other RL configurations as well. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of this recall loss: we measure it, identify its cause, and show that, once the cause is known, a simple remedy suffices.

</details>


### [183] [Music Similarity Learning in Sparse Latin Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22846)
*Gabrielly Xavier dos Santos,Lucas Dias Cler,Olga Goussevskaia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量、可解释的方法，利用公开元数据预测音乐项目之间的相似度，并在拉丁美洲音乐的零样本评估中验证其对稀疏数据和区域差异的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多音乐相似度系统依赖充足的项目共现数据、音频信号处理或较大的计算资源；当面对共现数据稀少的地区音乐时，现有方法可能受到区域差异影响。本文旨在以更低成本、更易解释的方式解决这一问题。

Method: 构建混合回归模型，将基于内容的过滤与经典的基于项目的协同过滤相结合，并使用公开元数据估计项目间的音乐相似度。通过对拉丁美洲音乐进行零样本评估，检验模型对未见且数据稀疏项目对的泛化能力。

Result: 摘要报告称，该方法能够准确预测未见且共现数据稀疏的项目对相似度，表现出应对区域差异的稳健性；同时无需音频信号处理，也不需要深度多模态方法所需的计算资源。摘要未提供具体数值或对比实验细节。

Conclusion: 基于公开元数据的混合回归方法有望以轻量、可解释的方式扩展音乐相似度估计，并改善在区域性、稀疏数据场景中的泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work we propose a lightweight, interpretable approach that estimates item-item music similarity from publicly available metadata. Our hybrid regression model combines content-based filtering with the classic item-based collaborative filtering. We test generalization via a zero-shot evaluation on Latin American music, a genre for which co-occurrence data is scarce. Accurately predicting similarity for these unseen, sparse pairs shows that our approach is robust to the regional gap affecting many existing systems, without the audio signal processing or the computational budget that deep multimodal approaches would require.

</details>


### [184] [CARE: Conversational Affective Response Retrieval for Internet Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23005)
*Jiajun Li,Leihan Zhang,Teng Zhang,Le Zhang,Qiang Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出结合情绪信息的两阶段对话表情包检索方法，在按话题留出的评测中优于微调后的图文检索基线；但结果尚不能证明其提升源于情绪建模，也不能直接代表用户感知的社交共鸣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线交流中的表情包回复是否合适，不仅取决于与对话语境的语义相关性，也取决于情绪表达。现有检索方法对情感因素的利用有限，因此论文探索如何将情绪信息融入检索，并分析其与用户互动反馈的关系。

Method: 研究使用豆瓣和因姆弗利普平台的两个语言特定数据集，共包含98,506条表情包记录和5,583个讨论话题。以同一讨论串中的表情包构造弱相关标签，并将点赞作为隐式排序反馈；同时提取情感、面部表情及跨模态一致性特征。提出两阶段框架：第一阶段在图文对比学习检索器中通过门控融合加入自由形式的情绪描述，并利用多个正例训练；第二阶段使用基于文本嵌入模型的重排器，结合查询与图像的交互特征及情绪表示，并依据互动反馈进行排序训练。

Result: 在按话题留出的评测中，该方法的十位命中率在豆瓣数据集上为24.03%，在因姆弗利普数据集上为49.33%；微调后的图文检索基线分别为23.16%和41.07%。结果显示，完整的两阶段系统更善于找回与讨论串相关联的表情包。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将情绪信息纳入两阶段检索系统具有应用潜力，但当前实验只能支持系统整体在特定数据与评测协议下的效果，不能单独确认情绪建模带来的贡献，也不能据此断言检索结果符合人类对社交共鸣的感知。后续应采用人工相关性判断，并通过受控对比实验分离各组件的作用。

Abstract: Conversational meme retrieval supports response selection in online communication, where relevance depends on both contextual meaning and affective expression. We study this task using two language-specific collections from Douban and Imgflip, containing 98,506 meme records across 5,583 discussion topics. Thread membership supplies weak relevance labels, while likes provide implicit ranking feedback. Automatically extracted sentiment, facial-expression indicators, and cross-modal consistency features support an exploratory analysis of engagement. We propose CARE, a Conversational Affective Response Retrieval framework that combines an emotion-enhanced CLIP retriever with a Jina-based reranker. The retriever incorporates free-form emotion descriptors through gated fusion and learns from multiple positive examples per query. The reranker combines query--image interactions with emotion representations and is trained using engagement-based ranking supervision. Under the reported topic-held-out protocol, CARE achieves Hit@10 of 24.03\% on Douban and 49.33\% on Imgflip, compared with 23.16\% and 41.07\%, respectively, for fine-tuned CLIP. These results support the utility of the complete two-stage system for recovering thread-associated memes. They do not independently establish perceived social resonance or isolate the contribution of affect from reranker training. The study provides a basis for further evaluation of affect-aware retrieval with human relevance judgments and controlled comparisons.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [DeltaHarness: Self-Evolving Harnesses for Resource-Adaptive Tool Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21688)
*Lecheng Yan,Jianze Lin,Xicheng Han,Xiantao Xu,Wenxi Li,Chenyang Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过实际执行测量来调整智能体工具执行框架的方法；初步实验显示，该方法在若干任务中降低了完成交付的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的任务与资源描述，可能因运行环境不同而使有效执行方案的延迟排序发生逆转。仅凭静态描述难以判断哪种方案更快，因此需要在目标环境中进行实际执行与测量。

Method: 提出德尔塔执行框架，一种从冷启动测量出发、通过执行结果指导策略搜索的方法。其循环机制利用观测结果调整运行时调度、修订物理执行计划，并选择后续实验；另设独立确认机制，决定是否替换现有策略。

Result: 在固定调度条件下，修订后的物化策略在一个视频编辑任务的三组全新样本对上，将完整交付延迟降低了百分之三十。对于留出任务，在一个随机种子下，演化策略较最佳固定方案降低百分之八点二；在另一随机种子下，较初始方案降低百分之四点六。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对目标环境进行执行测量，并据此迭代调整工具执行框架，有望改善任务延迟。不过，摘要报告的结果来自有限任务与实验条件，尚不足以证明其对更广泛场景的普遍效果。

Abstract: Tool-using agents rely on execution harnesses to organize and schedule tool operations. The same task and resource description can conceal runtime conditions that reverse the latency ordering of valid execution plans. Resolving this uncertainty requires firsthand execution in the target setting. We introduce DeltaHarness, an execution-guided policy-search method that adapts agent harnesses from cold-start measurements. Its QHI loop uses the observed response to update runtime dispatch, revise physical plans, and select experiments for later proposals. An independent confirmation panel controls policy replacement. With fixed dispatch, a revised materialization policy reduces complete-delivery latency by 30.0% on one video-editing task across three fresh pairs. On held-out tasks, the evolved policies reduce latency by 8.2% relative to the best fixed plan in one seed and by 4.6% relative to the initial proposal in another.

</details>


### [186] [AdaFlowLite: Scalable and Non-blocking Inference on Asynchronous Mobile Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21691)
*Sicong Liu,Fengmin Wu,Yuan Gao,Bin Guo,Zimu Zhou,Hongkai Wen,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向移动端异步多模态数据的机会式推理方法，利用结构化模态亲和度分析和轻量级跨模态补全，在降低延迟与能耗的同时提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上的不同传感器数据会因数据规模和网络状况而异步到达。等待较慢模态会增加推理延迟，过早推理则可能降低准确率；移动环境中模态类型和数量不断变化，使现有主要关注模态一致性与互补性的方案难以有效控制模态亲和度，也难以适应开放环境。

Method: 提出一种异步多模态机会式推理框架，允许部分数据到达后立即开始推理。框架通过基于层次分析的归一化矩阵，刻画移动场景中的结构化跨模态亲和度，以适应不同类型和数量的模态；并采用轻量级多模态视窗变换器，实时、灵活地补全缺失模态，适配不同输入模态和下游任务，且无需重新训练。该方法还可用于预处理异步数据，以提升大语言模型的表现。

Result: 实验显示，该方法相较现有方案最高可将推理延迟降低百分之八十点四、准确率提升百分之六十二点一，并将能耗降低近百分之五十。论文还报告其能够改善大语言模型处理异步数据时的表现。

Conclusion: 该研究将模态亲和度从定性考虑推进为适用于移动开放环境的结构化计算机制，并结合在线跨模态补全实现异步数据的低延迟推理。结果表明，此方法有望兼顾准确率、响应速度与能效，但摘要未提供具体任务、数据集及对比设置，因而这些性能提升的适用范围仍需结合全文评估。

Abstract: The rise of mobile devices equipped with numerous sensors, such as LiDAR and cameras, has driven the adoption of multi-modal deep intelligence for distributed sensing tasks, such as smart cabins and driving assistance. However, the arrival time of mobile sensory data vary due to modality size and network dynamics, which can lead to delays (if waiting for slow data) or accuracy decline (if inference proceeds without waiting). Moreover, the diversity and dynamic nature of mobile systems exacerbate this challenge. In response, we present a shift to \textit{opportunistic} inference for asynchronous distributed multi-modal data, enabling inference as soon as partial data arrives. While existing methods focus on optimizing modality consistency and complementarity, known as modal affinity, they lack a \textit{computational} approach to control this affinity in open-world mobile environments. %
\sysname pioneers the formulation of structured cross-modality affinity in mobile contexts using a hierarchical analysis-based normalized matrix. This approach accommodates the diversity and dynamics of modalities, generalizing across different types and numbers of inputs. Employing an multi-modal lightweight Swin Transformer (MMLST), \sysname facilitates real-time and flexible data imputation, adapting to various modalities and downstream tasks without retraining. Experiments show that \sysname significantly reduces inference latency by up to 80.4\% and enhances accuracy by up to 62.1\%, while achieving nearly a 50\% reduction in energy consumption, outperforming status quo approaches. Also, this method can enhance LLM performance to preprocess asynchronous data.

</details>


### [187] [TIBECG: Temporal Information Bottleneck for Lead-Specific Demographic Privacy in ECG Waveform Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21694)
*Tsai-Min Chen,Jun Seita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究评估了一种时序信息瓶颈方法，尝试在保留心电诊断效用的同时减少波形中的年龄与性别信息泄漏；效果因导联而异，尚不足以证明其适合消费级部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电波形除诊断信息外，也可能泄露年龄、性别等人口统计信息。若诊断模型运行于专有云端服务，设备端必须上传波形才能获得诊断结果，因此有必要研究能否在传输前降低隐私泄漏，同时尽量保留诊断价值。

Method: 作者在医院十二导联心电记录中分别提取单个导联，评估时序信息瓶颈处理器。该处理器有约230万参数，通过改变潜在表示长度控制信息压缩，并保持相同的九分数一致性目标；保护器拟合时不使用年龄或性别标签。研究涵盖6386份记录、十折评估、12个导联和21种潜在长度，并与常规变换及不同采样率的直接降采样进行比较。

Result: 回顾性选择出的处理设置，在各导联上均保留了至少95%的未处理信号宏平均F1诊断表现。其年龄或性别区间增益在所有导联上均超过最佳可比较的常规变换，中位优势为22.08个百分点。直接降采样通常更能抑制人口统计信息推断，但会造成更大的诊断效用损失；该方法更侧重保留诊断效用。不同导联的选择稳定性有所不同，且在潜在长度固定时，较低的潜在表示库尔贝克—莱布勒散度不能可靠预测残余性别信息泄漏。

Conclusion: 结果支持按导联评估心电波形处理方法，并显示该方法可能在诊断效用与人口统计隐私之间实现较好的折衷。但研究并未验证其适用于消费级部署，也未证明其能重建专家可读的心电波形；因此不能据此认为云端诊断所需的波形传输问题已得到解决。

Abstract: Transmitting an electrocardiogram (ECG) waveform can expose demographic information beyond an intended diagnostic task. We evaluate TIBECG, an architectural temporal information bottleneck, on individual leads extracted from hospital twelve-lead recordings. A 2,302,337-parameter processor varies its latent length while retaining the same nine-score consistency objective, without age or sex labels in protector fitting. Across 6,386 recordings, ten folds, 12 leads and 21 lengths, retrospective selections retained at least 95% of each lead's clean macro-F1. Their weaker age/sex interval gain exceeded the best eligible conventional transformation on all leads, by a median 22.08 percentage points. The implemented sampling-rate comparison shows that direct downsampling usually suppresses demographic inference more strongly but at a larger loss of diagnostic utility, whereas TIBECG favors utility preservation. Selection stability also varies by lead. Lower latent Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence does not reliably rank residual sex leakage at a fixed length. These findings support lead-specific evaluation of waveform processing, not validated consumer deployment or expert-readable reconstruction. When the waveform-input diagnostic classifier is hosted as part of a proprietary cloud service rather than deployed on the device, waveform transmission is required to obtain its scores.

</details>


### [188] [Layerwise Learning-Rate Scaling in Vision Networks: Effective-Rate Identification, Power-Mean Aggregation, and Budget Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21696)
*Chandini Vysyaraju,Raghuvir Duvvuri,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，从头训练视觉网络时，分层学习率策略的速率效应可用一个由各层学习率倍率计算出的有效学习率概括；据此无需逐一训练候选方案，也能更好地筛选策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层学习率缩放已广泛用于预训练模型微调，但在从头训练视觉网络时，通常必须实际训练每种候选方案，才能评估效果。论文旨在找出能直接预测这类策略表现的简洁指标，并厘清其效果是否源于优化进展，以及这种效果如何随训练预算变化。

Method: 作者在六种视觉网络架构中进行匹配对照，考察分层学习率倍率与测试准确率、训练损失之间的关系，并检验提高基础学习率及延长训练预算的影响。研究还比较不同形式的有效学习率汇总指标，以架构内表现差异评估其解释力；最后利用该指标对候选方案排序，并与逐一训练筛选的结果比较。

Result: 匹配对照中，测试准确率的变化与训练损失变化高度对应，相关系数为负零点九九六，支持短训练预算下的速率效应主要来自优化进展。将未经调优的基础学习率提高十倍后，六种架构中的速率匹配对照均向有利方向变化；一项未参与拟合的已发表差异化学习率方案，优于统一学习率对照的架构数也从六种中的两种增至五种。在所评估的策略范围内，参数加权的零点三五次幂平均值解释了百分之四十二点六的架构内差异，高于算术平均值的百分之三十六点四。依据该标量排序，可将选中方案的平均百分位排名从第二十六位提升至第十位，并将其相对最佳方案的平均准确率差距从三点七零个百分点缩小至一点一二个百分点。速率相关效应会随训练预算增加而减弱；在三种架构中的两种里，整体策略效应落入预先设定的实际等效范围。另有一项与分组方式相关的效应可持续至较长训练预算，但方向和幅度取决于架构。

Conclusion: 在本文考察的从头训练视觉网络策略范围内，有效学习率可作为分层学习率速率效应的实用摘要指标，帮助在不训练所有候选方案的情况下进行筛选。速率效应在短预算下与优化进展密切相关，但会随训练时间延长而减弱；分组方式的影响则可能持续存在，且具有架构依赖性。

Abstract: Layerwise learning-rate scaling assigns different learning rates to different parts of a network. Although established in fine-tuning pretrained models, its effects in vision networks trained from scratch are commonly assessed by training each candidate recipe. We show that, in this setting, the rate-related component of a layerwise strategy is well summarized by an effective learning rate computable directly from its multiplier profile. Across matched-control comparisons, changes in test accuracy closely track changes in training loss (r = -0.996), which supports an optimization-progress explanation of the short-budget rate effect. Increasing the untuned base learning rate tenfold shifts the rate-matched contrast upward in all six tested architectures (p = 0.016) and increases the number of architectures in which a published discriminative schedule, excluded from model fitting, outperforms the uniform control from two to five of six (p = 0.031). Within the evaluated strategy space, a parameter-weighted power mean with exponent 0.35 (95% confidence interval [0.20, 0.50]) accounts for 42.6% of within-architecture variance, compared with 36.4% for the arithmetic mean. Ranking candidates by this scalar without training improves the selected candidate's mean percentile rank from the 26th to the 10th and reduces its mean accuracy shortfall relative to the best candidate from 3.70 to 1.12 points. The rate-related effect decays as the training budget increases; relative to tuned baselines, the overall strategy effect falls within the prespecified practical-equivalence margin in two of three architectures. A separate grouping-related contrast can persist at long budgets, although its direction and magnitude depend on the architecture.

</details>


### [189] [FACE: Flow-Guided Angular Channel Estimation Framework for mmWave MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21698)
*Samitha Gunarathne,Eslam Eldeeb,Nurul Huda Mahmood,Matti Latva-aho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于流匹配的毫米波多输入多输出信道估计框架，利用角域稀疏性和生成式先验，在减少导频与推理开销的同时提升估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波多输入多输出系统的信道状态信息获取受到信道维度高、导频观测有限及测量噪声影响，准确估计具有挑战性。传统估计方法难以充分利用信道结构；扩散式生成估计虽能引入数据先验，但采样步骤较多、计算开销较高。

Method: 提出流引导角域信道估计框架。该方法利用毫米波信道在角域的稀疏性，并将通过流匹配学习的连续概率传输过程作为生成式信道先验，以辅助从有限且含噪的观测中恢复信道。

Result: 数值实验表明，该框架在多种信道与系统配置下，估计精度持续优于传统估计器及扩散式生成估计器；使用更少导频时仍能保持较高精度，且平均可达速率接近完美信道状态信息基准。相较扩散式方法，它所需采样步骤显著更少，推理时间和计算开销也更低，同时精度更优或相当。

Conclusion: 流匹配可作为有效的生成式信道先验，支持高精度、低导频需求且计算高效的毫米波多输入多输出信道估计。该研究展示了流匹配在无线信道估计中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Acquiring accurate channel state information (CSI) is essential for millimeter-wave (mmWave) multi-input multi-output (MIMO) systems. However, it remains challenging due to high-dimensional channels, limited pilot observations, and measurement noise. To address these challenges, we propose flow-guided angular channel estimation (FACE), a novel channel estimation framework based on flow matching, an emerging generative modeling paradigm that learns continuous transport dynamics between probability distributions. FACE exploits the inherent angular-domain sparsity of mmWave channels and incorporates the learned flow matching model as a generative channel prior. Numerical results show that FACE consistently achieves higher channel estimation accuracy than both conventional estimators and diffusion-based generative estimator across diverse channel and system configurations. Moreover, FACE maintains high estimation accuracy with fewer pilot observations and achieves an average achievable rate close to the perfect-CSI benchmark. Compared with diffusion-based estimation, FACE requires substantially fewer sampling steps while achieving superior or comparable estimation accuracy, reducing inference time and computational cost. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates the potential of flow matching for accurate, pilot-efficient, and computationally efficient mmWave MIMO channel estimation.

</details>


### [190] [Hybrid Quantum Selection of Learning Rates in Language-Model Training: Termination Criteria and an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21701)
*Wei-Che Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文探索在混合经典—量子训练框架中，为不同层组联合选择学习率；实验展示了闭环量子辅助搜索的可行性，但基准准确率仍处于五个统一学习率随机种子的波动范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调时的学习率会显著影响语言模型表现，但不同层组学习率的最佳联合配置通常未知。论文旨在研究量子搜索能否在训练过程中自适应地寻找更优的学习率安排。

Method: 论文先将杜尔—霍耶尔最小值搜索与布布什蒂—巴拉德—哈里斯—塔普搜索结合，利用固定训练检查点上测得的配置分数构造查表式预言机，并比较固定预算与基于置信度的停止策略。随后提出交互引导的调度预言机：通过经典单步损失测量估计层组间的主效应、两两交互和三方交互，在量子叠加态中计算联合调度分数，并标记优于当前基线的方案以进行振幅放大。候选方案经经典验证后才被接受；每个选定方案用于接下来的二百步经典训练。

Result: 在十五亿参数语言模型的闭环微调实验中，所选学习率调度方案的基准准确率落在五个统一学习率随机种子所呈现的范围内。结果表明，该方法可以在训练过程中完成量子辅助的自适应学习率选择，但摘要未显示其准确率超越这一基准波动范围。

Conclusion: 论文给出了利用量子叠加支持层组联合学习率搜索的可运行示例，并验证了经典测量、量子搜索与经典训练组成闭环的可行性。当前结果主要证明方法能够运行，尚未表明其带来明确的基准性能提升；此外，初始基线采用统一学习率，而以经典自适应方法的调度方案作为基线进行联合细化仍留待后续研究。

Abstract: Learning-rate choices strongly affect language-model fine-tuning, but the best joint profile across layer groups is generally unknown. This paper studies quantum minimum finding for layer-group learning-rate selection within a hybrid classical-quantum training framework. It combines Durr-Hoyer minimum finding with BBHT search and compares fixed-budget and confidence-based stopping policies using a lookup oracle built from measured profile scores at fixed training checkpoints. The paper then introduces an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle that scores and marks joint schedules in superposition without a precomputed score table. Classical one-step loss measurements supply the main, pairwise and three-way interaction effects between layer groups. The circuit adds the effects for each schedule, compares the resulting score with the current baseline score, and marks improving schedules for amplitude amplification. Measured candidates are checked classically before acceptance. Uniform-rate training supplies the initial baseline; using schedules from classical adaptive methods as baselines for joint refinement is left for future work. In closed-loop fine-tuning of a 1.5-billion-parameter language model, each selected schedule drives the next 200 classical training steps. Benchmark accuracies lie within the range observed across five uniform-rate seeds. The results give a working example of how quantum superposition can support adaptive learning-rate selection during training; the remaining quantum limitations are discussed in the paper.

</details>


### [191] [Margin Dynamics for Large Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21766)
*Xingzi Xu,Mehmet Saygin Seyfioglu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文建立成对偏好对齐方法的理论分析框架，刻画偏好与非偏好回答之间的概率质量如何变化，并据此解释不同方法的训练行为、标签噪声影响及平滑设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中，研究者选择大语言模型对齐方法往往依赖反复试验，缺少能够预测训练行为的理论依据。论文希望揭示不同成对偏好方法如何改变模型对偏好回答与非偏好回答的相对概率，并以此指导方法选择和设计。

Method: 将每种成对偏好方法视为概率单纯形上的向量场，并把分析投影到单个偏好—非偏好回答对，得到一维的边际差分常微分方程及其闭式解。论文据此分析直接偏好优化、隐式偏好优化和简化偏好优化的边际变化、标签噪声效应及梯度集中现象；同时推导出，简化偏好优化加入任意正标签平滑后，会形成有限边际吸引点，位置为 Δ*=β⁻¹log((1−ε)/ε)。

Result: 理论分析表明：直接偏好优化的边际会持续增大，在梯度流极限下按对数速度增长；隐式偏好优化的边际则收敛至有限值 1/(2τ)。标签噪声会使直接偏好优化从边际无界增长转为有界增长，而对隐式偏好优化主要产生定量影响。非对称加权下的梯度集中程度则关系到大规模训练的可行性。研究者在涵盖四项基准的五类模型上验证了前三项预测；并在三种八十亿至九十亿参数模型上验证平滑设计预测：理论推导的配置在三种模型的指令遵循评测平均分上均优于所比较方法。标准简化偏好优化不具有有限边际吸引点。

Conclusion: 该研究为成对偏好对齐方法提供了可解析的理论框架，可用于理解训练动态、评估标签噪声的影响，并指导平滑参数设计。实验支持其理论预测；理论推导出的有限边际吸引点也展现了实际可用性，但论文强调其目标是验证方法设计原则，而非宣称提出新的最先进损失函数。

Abstract: Practitioners choose among LLM alignment methods largely by trial and error. We provide a theoretical foundation: each pairwise method defines a vector field on the probability simplex that governs how the model redistributes mass between preferred and dispreferred completions. Projecting onto a single preferred/dispreferred pair reduces this to a one-dimensional margin ODE with a closed-form solution. These solutions reveal that DPO margins grow without bound (logarithmically as $O(\log t)$ in the gradient-flow limit) while IPO margins converge to a finite $1/(2τ)$; that label noise induces a qualitative phase transition in DPO (unbounded $\to$ bounded) but affects IPO only quantitatively; and that gradient concentration under asymmetric weighting governs trainability at scale. We confirm all three predictions across five model families spanning four benchmarks. The closed-form solutions also enable principled method design: we prove that adding any label smoothing $\varepsilon > 0$ to SimPO creates a finite margin attractor at $Δ^* = β^{-1}\log((1-\varepsilon)/\varepsilon)$, while $\varepsilon = 0$ (standard SimPO) has no attractor. We test this across three models (8B-9B) and confirm the prediction: the theory-derived configuration (TPO-S, $\varepsilon = 0.05$) is competitive with the strongest baselines, attaining the highest mean IFEval score among the compared methods on all three, validating that the predicted finite attractor is a usable operating point rather than proposing a new state-of-the-art loss.

</details>


### [192] [Learning Calibrated and Samplable Predictive Distributions of Surface-Ocean $Δp$CO$_2$ Using Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21769)
*Gabriele Accarino,Mohammad M. Amirian,Thea H. Heimdal,Amanda R. Fay,Galen A. McKinley,Viviana Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究以条件扩散模型估计海气二氧化碳分压差的完整预测分布，在观测层面提供校准良好的不确定性，同时基本保持点预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋吸收了近三成的人为二氧化碳排放，但海表观测在时空上稀疏，使海洋碳汇难以准确量化。现有机器学习方法多只给出单一预测值，再以残差、模型集成或预设分布估算不确定性，难以直接刻画每条观测对应的完整预测分布。

Method: 研究使用树扩散模型，一种结合梯度提升树评分函数的条件扩散模型，以 13 个环境、空间和时间预测因子为条件，估计每条观测的海气二氧化碳分压差预测分布。模型在留出的全球海洋表层二氧化碳观测数据集上进行评估，并与多种不确定性量化方法及确定性梯度提升树基线比较。

Result: 该模型的连续秩概率评分为 8.229 微大气压，在所比较的不确定性量化方法中最低；百分之六十八和百分之九十五名义区间的实际覆盖率分别为 0.686 和 0.930，接近目标值。点预测表现与确定性基线相近：均方根误差为 18.4 微大气压，对照基线为 17.7；决定系数为 0.77，对照基线为 0.79。

Conclusion: 条件扩散方法能够在基本保留点预测能力的同时，为海气二氧化碳分压差提供经过良好校准的观测级不确定性，其生成样本也可用于概率分析。不过，要将方法扩展到具有空间连贯性的场重建，还需显式建模网格单元间的依赖关系，并在空间留出数据上进行评估。

Abstract: The ocean absorbs almost 30% of anthropogenic CO$_2$ emissions, but quantifying this sink requires estimating surface-ocean CO$_2$ from observations that are sparse in space and time. Most existing machine-learning estimates of this field are deterministic, giving a single value per grid point, with uncertainty approximated post hoc via residuals, ensembles, or prescribed distributions. We instead learn a conditional predictive distribution for each observation: using Treeffuser, a conditional diffusion model with gradient-boosted-tree score functions, we estimate the full predictive distribution of $Δp$CO$_2$ (the sea-air $p$CO$_2$ difference) from 13 environmental, spatial, and temporal predictors. On a held-out SOCAT test set, Treeffuser attains the lowest continuous ranked probability score among the uncertainty-quantification methods evaluated (8.229 $μ$atm) and the coverage closest to the nominal 68% / 95% levels (0.686 / 0.930), while retaining point-prediction skill comparable to a deterministic XGBoost baseline (RMSE 18.4 vs. 17.7 $μ$atm; $R^2$ 0.77 vs. 0.79). These results show that conditional diffusion can provide well-calibrated, observation-level uncertainties while largely preserving point-prediction skill. Because Treeffuser is generative, its samples can support probabilistic analyses of $Δp$CO$_2$; extending this to spatially coherent field reconstruction will require modeling dependence across grid cells and evaluation on spatially held-out data.

</details>


### [193] [Regularized Score Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21791)
*Hao Bai,Chenlu Ye,Xuanchang Zhang,Aviral Kumar,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出正则化分数中心化方法，通过反向库尔贝克—莱布勒散度约束学习策略与生成数据的行为策略之间的差异，提升异步强化学习的稳定性与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习能够提高训练吞吐量，但训练数据可能来自滞后的策略，且训练与推理间的数值差异也会导致学习不稳定。分数中心化可消除奖励基线对期望更新的影响、缓和极端梯度，但当学习策略偏离数据生成策略较远时仍可能失效，因此需要限制策略间的偏差。

Method: 提出正则化分数中心化，在分数中心化目标中加入反向库尔贝克—莱布勒散度惩罚，使学习策略的下一词元分布接近行为策略的对应分布。该方法可与截断重要性采样结合，也可单独使用；理论分析表明，散度正则化能够改善收敛保证。

Result: 在数学推理及多类智能体任务上评估了稠密模型和混合专家模型。结合截断重要性采样时，该方法在所有智能体基准上显著提高样本效率，并能以相同数量的采样回答获得更高训练奖励。最终评测中，它在六项智能体任务上均取得最佳表现：在应用世界任务上提升百分之十四点三，在婴儿人工智能任务上的得分达到最强基线的九点九倍。

Conclusion: 通过约束学习策略与行为策略之间的分布差异，正则化分数中心化缓解了异步、离策略训练中的策略失配问题，并在理论收敛性与多项智能体任务表现上展现出优势。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning increases training throughput, but data from stale policies and numerical mismatch between training and inference can destabilize learning. To stabilize off-policy learning, score centering (SC) removes the effect of reward baselines on the expected update. Our gradient diagnostics show less extreme gradients with SC than with importance sampling (IS), but SC can still fail when the learner moves far from the behavior policy that generated the data. To limit this policy mismatch, we introduce Regularized Score Centering (RSC), which adds a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) penalty to encourage the learner's next-token distribution to stay close to the behavior policy's next-token distribution. RSC works with or without truncated importance sampling (TIS), which caps token importance weights. Our theoretical analysis supports this approach, showing that KL regularization improves convergence guarantees. We evaluate dense and mixture-of-experts models on mathematical reasoning and agentic tasks, including online shopping, tool use, games, and fully asynchronous software engineering. RSC with TIS substantially improves sample efficiency across all agentic benchmarks, attaining higher training rewards with the same number of sampled responses. In final evaluations, it achieves top performance across all six agentic tasks, improving by 14.3% on AppWorld and reaching 9.9 times the strongest baseline's score on BabyAI.

</details>


### [194] [Urban Representation Learning for Identifying Potential Disparities in Crime Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21825)
*Jorge Valverde-Rebaza,Carlos Paez De La Cruz,Pedro Pérez-Collado,Daniel Sánchez-Cataño,Eashley Martínez-Vergara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种自监督城市表征学习方法，通过比较相似区域的犯罪报案模式，筛查值得进一步核查的异常区域；该方法并不直接判定犯罪漏报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放犯罪数据记录的是已报案事件，而非犯罪活动的完整分布，因此报案差异可能扭曲后续空间分析。研究旨在仅利用带地理位置的犯罪记录，识别相对于特征相似区域而言异常的报案模式。

Method: 分别构建按小时、按星期和静态的报案画像，在地理自监督机制下学习各尺度区域表征，并融合到统一潜在空间。随后检索表征相似的同伴区域，以邻域画像差异度衡量每个区域与同伴报案画像之间的不一致程度。

Result: 在墨西哥城超过一百八十万条犯罪记录、汇总为一千零五十八个六边形区域的数据上，所提表征的平均轮廓系数为零点四零五二，高于对比模型的零点二八六三。不同邻域规模下的差异度排序高度稳定，斯皮尔曼相关系数为零点九七二零至零点九九五一；多数高差异候选区域在不同配置下持续出现。检索到的同伴区域也显著超出直接地理邻近范围。部分高差异区域呈现有组织的空间模式，且其事件数量与同伴预期存在差别。

Conclusion: 邻域画像差异度可作为内部审计与调查优先级排序工具，帮助发现值得进一步核查的区域。结果表明，学习到的表征捕捉了超越局部地理邻近的结构；但高差异仅代表报案模式与同伴不一致，不能直接作为犯罪漏报的证据。

Abstract: Open crime data support urban safety analysis but reflect reported incidents rather than the complete distribution of criminal activity, potentially introducing spatial distortions in downstream analysis. We propose a self-supervised urban representation learning framework for identifying unusual reporting patterns relative to representation-similar peers using only georeferenced crime records. The method constructs complementary hourly, weekly, and static reporting profiles, learns scale-specific representations under geographic self-supervision, and integrates them into a unified latent space. Neighbor Profile Divergence (NPD) is then used to quantify disagreement between each region's reporting profile and those of its retrieved peers. Experiments on more than 1.8 million crime records from Mexico City, aggregated into 1,058 H3 regions, show that the proposed representation achieves a mean Silhouette score of 0.4052, compared with 0.2863 for Hex2Vec. NPD rankings remain highly stable across neighborhood sizes, with Spearman correlations from 0.9720 to 0.9951, while most high-divergence candidates persist across configurations. The retrieved peers also extend substantially beyond immediate geographic neighborhoods, indicating that the learned representation captures structure beyond local spatial proximity. High-divergence regions exhibit spatially organized patterns and, in several cases, differences in incident volume relative to peer expectations. These results support NPD as an internal auditing and prioritization mechanism for identifying regions that warrant further investigation, rather than as direct evidence of underreporting.

</details>


### [195] [VisHead-Spec: Head-Routed Visual Attention for Multimodal Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21839)
*Xin Guo,Arushi Rai,Xiang Zhou,Qiang Zhang,Siyu Lin,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉头部推测解码，通过让少量草稿模型注意力头读取高层图像特征、其余头仅处理文本，在基本维持草稿质量的同时提升高并发吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码可在保持目标模型输出不变的前提下加速视觉语言模型，但并发量升高、批次变大后，系统更受计算能力限制，速度收益随之下降。图像提示词较长，使草稿模型预测时的计算负担尤为显著。现有块扩散式草稿模型虽能一次预测多个词元，但高并发场景下的加速效果仍有限。研究进一步发现，草稿模型并不需要所有注意力头都读取图像信息，且有用的图像特征主要来自目标模型的高层。

Method: 提出视觉头部推测解码：固定选取少量草稿模型的键值头，使其读取目标模型高层的全部图像特征；其余键值头则限制为只读取文本特征。该设计利用跨模态注意力的稀疏性，减少草稿预测中的图像计算开销。

Result: 在通义千问第三代视觉语言模型（八十亿参数）及多模态推测解码评测中，所提八头配置让四十个键值头中的八个读取图像特征，接受词元长度相较块扩散草稿基线仅降低百分之零点三以内。批次大小为六十四时，相对自回归解码的吞吐量提升百分之十六，高于基线的百分之九点五；批次大小为一时，加速效果与基线相近。

Conclusion: 通过将图像信息访问集中到少量关键注意力头，并仅使用目标模型高层图像特征，可以在几乎不损害草稿接受效果的情况下提高高并发视觉语言模型服务的吞吐量。该方法尤其适合传统推测解码收益因计算受限而减弱的高并发场景。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models(VLMs) while preserving the target model's outputs, but its speedup diminishes under high concurrency serving as the system becomes more compute bounded as the batch size increases. This problem is pronounced for multimodal speculative decoding as the long range of the image tokens in the prompt can significantly increase the computational cost during draft token prediction. The block-diffusion drafters (e.g. DFlash, DSpark) can effectively speed up the draft model efficiency by predicting multiple draft tokens in one forward pass, but their speedup for VLMs under high concurrency is still modest (<9%). In order to understand this gap, we examine the information flow between different modalities from target model to draft model and identify the sparsity pattern of visual attention between the target and draft models: only small set of key-value heads in the draft model need direct visual feature attention in the context and only visual features from top level layers in the target model need to be attended in the context. We therefore propose VisHead-Spec, which selects a small, fixed set of key-value (KV) heads to attend to all image features from top level layers in the target model and restricts the remaining heads to text features. On Qwen3-VL-8B and MMSpec, VisHead-Spec-8 lets 8 of the drafter's 40 KV heads read the image while keeping accepted length within 0.3% of the block-diffusion draft model baseline (i.e. DSpark). At batch size 64, its throughput gain over autoregressive decoding increases to 16.0%, compared with 9.5% for DSpark; at batch size 1, its speedup remains comparable to DSpark.

</details>


### [196] [Inventa: Decentralized Self-Improving Agents for Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21847)
*Prithwish Jana,Nikos Kanakaris,Purak Jain,Anoop Deoras,Sahika Genc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种名为“因维塔”的自动化科学发现框架，通过自主研究单元、动态调整探索策略和经验证的共享知识，提升科学构想的多样性、可行性与搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增加大语言模型智能体的数量或迭代次数，并不必然带来更好的科学发现：智能体可能趋向相似构想、传播未经证实的说法，也可能反复使用已无收益的搜索策略。因此，需要一种能够持续调整探索过程、抑制错误知识传播并促进有效经验共享的机制。

Method: 框架由多个自主研究单元组成。每个单元通过“变异器”智能体生成并演化一系列候选构想，由本地编排器定期调整变异器的运行方案；当进展停滞时，编排器更换模型或重新启动构想演化。验证器评估候选构想，并将已验证的改进、有用组成部分及约束违规记录到共享知识中；该共享知识是不同研究单元之间唯一的信息通道。

Result: 在覆盖文献检索、构想生成、研究计划撰写和科学规律发现的五项基准测试中，该框架均取得领先表现。在科学规律发现基准上得分为百分之七十三点六，高于对比方法的百分之五十六点二；在构想基准上，每篇论文发现二十六个新颖构想，高于另一智能体方法的十八点六个，同时减少百分之八十二的模型调用。

Conclusion: 摘要显示，将候选构想的验证、受控知识共享与适应性搜索策略结合，有望提升自动化科学发现的效果与效率。该框架在多类研究任务和基准上表现突出，尤其体现出以较少模型调用发现更多新颖构想的潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can generate research ideas and improve scientific discovery through iterative experimentation. However, increasing the number of agents or iterations does not automatically translate to producing more diverse or feasible ideas. Agents may converge on similar candidates, propagate unsupported claims through shared memory components and repeatedly apply search strategies that no longer yield any improvements.
  We introduce Inventa, a framework for automated scientific discovery that employs autonomous research units and verified shared knowledge. Each unit creates a lineage of candidate ideas using a mutator agent. A local orchestrator agent periodically adapts the mutator's harness. When progress stalls, the orchestrator agent replaces its model or restarts the idea evolution process. Verifiers evaluate each candidate idea and record verified improvements, useful components and constraint violations in a shared knowledge, the only channel between units. We evaluate Inventa on five benchmarks spanning the research cycle, covering retrieval (ScholarCatalyst), ideation (IdeaBench), proposal writing (IdeaScientist), and scientific law discovery (EurekaBench, SciLaws-Bench). It leads all five, achieving 73.6% on EurekaBench versus 56.2% for CORAL. On IdeaBench, it discovers 26.0 novel ideas per paper, compared with 18.6 for ResearchAgent, with 82% fewer model calls.

</details>


### [197] [When Does Discrete Ricci Curvature Help? An Edge-Level Analysis of Curvature-Enhanced GCNs for PPI Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21849)
*Clara Joswig,Elham Gholamzadeh,Ghazal Mostofi,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，曲率增强图卷积网络虽在四个蛋白质互作数据集上提升总体预测表现，但优势主要来自低度边上的预测；其对瓶颈边的表现反而在多数数据集上下降，更符合增强簇间分离的解释，而非缓解过度挤压。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲率增强图卷积网络利用最优传输理论中的奥利维耶—里奇曲率为消息传递中的边赋权，原本旨在缓解结构瓶颈造成的过度挤压。论文质疑其总体性能提升是否确实源于这一机制，并指出仅比较整体指标不足以揭示模型在哪类边上获益或受损。

Method: 研究在四个真实蛋白质互作数据集上，将曲率增强图卷积网络与标准图卷积网络进行逐边比较，同时考察曲率加权函数、聚类有效性指标、边距离比，以及四种网络深度下的消融结果。为检验替代解释，研究还依据图拓扑定义社区，而非沿用分类标签。

Result: 曲率增强模型在四个数据集上的整体曲线下面积、精确率—召回率曲线下面积和调和平均指标均有所提升，但增益几乎集中于低度边。三个数据集中，模型在低曲率瓶颈边上的表现出现下降，与缓解过度挤压的预期相反。其他分析则支持另一种解释：曲率加权增强了不同簇之间的区分，使簇内预测改善，却以簇间（瓶颈）预测为代价。

Conclusion: 整体指标的提高并不能证明曲率增强网络缓解了过度挤压；其优势更可能来自强化簇结构分离。实际选用时，应根据任务侧重决定：若更重视簇内预测，可考虑曲率增强模型；若簇间或瓶颈边预测至关重要，则应谨慎评估，并将标准图卷积网络作为对照。

Abstract: Curvature-enhanced graph convolutional networks (CGCN) incorporate Ollivier-Ricci curvature (ORC), grounded in optimal transport theory, into message passing, motivated by the claim that curvature-weighted edges alleviate "over-squashing" at structural bottlenecks. But does CGCN's improvement actually come from mitigating over-squashing? We investigate this on four real-world protein-protein interaction (PPI) datasets, comparing CGCN against a standard GCN at the level of individual edges rather than aggregate metrics alone. While CGCN improves aggregate AUC, AUPR, and F1 across all four datasets, this gain is concentrated almost entirely on low-degree edges, while performance on low-curvature (bottleneck) edges systematically degrades in three of four datasets, the opposite of what the over-squashing motivation predicts. Examining the learned edge-weight function directly, together with clustering-validity metrics, edge-distance ratios, and a layer-depth ablation across four depths, we find evidence for an alternative account: curvature-weighting sharpens the separation between clusters, which improves intra-cluster predictions at the cost of inter-cluster (bottleneck) ones. We test this account against topologically defined communities rather than the classification labels used elsewhere. Additionally, we offer practical guidance on when to prefer CGCN over standard GCN, depending on whether intra- or inter-cluster predictions matter most.

</details>


### [198] [The Hidden Cost of MoE Diffusion Decoding: Profiling and Fixing the Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21852)
*Harshit Samrat,Ramakrishna Paramahamsa Kompella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，扩散语言模型量化后，解码瓶颈会从专家矩阵计算转移到全画布词元的采样与停止处理；通过融合或消除这部分冗余开销，并结合低比特专家计算，可在准确率不变或持平时实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型每轮去噪都要处理整张词元画布的全部词表分数，因此每个输出词元涉及的分数处理量约随去噪步数成比例增长。权重量化降低了模型主体的计算成本后，原本容易被忽视的采样、停止判断等流程可能成为主要耗时。论文旨在识别这一瓶颈，并探索不损害解码结果或任务准确率的优化方法。

Method: 作者在单用户解码场景下分析一款二百六十亿参数的扩散混合专家模型，并将其部署于带宽受限的边缘加速器。针对分数处理流程提出两级优化：严格无损级别确保输出与参考解码器逐字节一致；准确率持平级别则采用融合的单遍熵计算内核和基于极值采样的随机采样方法。随后将这些优化与四位权重、十六位激活的专家内核结合，并在另一款独立构建的扩散混合专家模型和一款稠密扩散模型上验证。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成等基准上，完整优化方案相较于半精度基线实现二点四至二点七倍端到端加速，同时保持基准准确率。在另一款扩散混合专家模型上，分数处理流程占解码时间的百分之二十八点八，融合低比特方案在准确率持平时达到三点三倍加速。相比之下，该流程在稠密扩散模型中仅占百分之三点六，说明这一瓶颈主要出现在量化后主体计算成本较低的混合专家扩散模型中。

Conclusion: 论文指出，扩散混合专家模型的优化不能只关注专家矩阵计算：权重量化后，全画布分数处理可能成为首要解码瓶颈。通过消除冗余流程并融合采样计算，可在不降低准确率的前提下显著提升端到端速度；这一结论具有模型结构特异性，并不普遍适用于稠密扩散模型。作者同时发布了代码与量化权重。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) decode by iteratively denoising a full canvas of tokens, so every forward pass runs a per-token sampling and stopping pipeline over the entire canvas-by-vocabulary logits tensor. We profile single-user decoding of a 26B-parameter diffusion Mixture-of-Experts model on a bandwidth-bound edge accelerator (NVIDIA GB10) and report a result that is easy to miss: after weight quantisation, this logits pipeline, not the expert GEMMs, is the single largest consumer of decode time. Unlike autoregressive decoding, where sampling touches one position's logits per committed token, diffusion decoding processes S times as many logit entries per output token, where S is the number of denoising steps. We show this pipeline is largely redundant or fusable and remove it in two lossless tiers: a bit-exact tier whose output is byte-identical to the reference decoder, and an accuracy-parity tier using a fused single-pass entropy kernel and Gumbel-max sampling. Combined with a W4A16 expert kernel, the full stack reaches 2.4-2.7 times end-to-end speedup over bf16 at unchanged benchmark accuracy on GSM8K, HumanEval, and MATH, on a single 273 GB/s device. We confirm the finding on a second, independently built diffusion MoE (LLaDA2.0-mini: pipeline 28.8% of decode, fused W4A16 3.3 times at accuracy parity) and show it disappears on a dense diffusion model (3.6%). The cost is specific to MoE diffusion, where quantisation makes the backbone cheap. Code and quantised weights are released.

</details>


### [199] [tmol: a GPU-accelerated implementation of the Rosetta energy function for machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21857)
*Andrew Leaver-Fay,Kieran Didi,Jeff Flatten,Alex Ford,Guangfeng Zhou,Nathaniel Corley,Hahnbeom Park,Maxwell W. Kazman,Rafael I. Brent,Joseph Kleinhenz,Henry Solberg,Andrew M. Watkins,Brian Kuhlman,David Baker,Frank DiMaio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于图形处理器、与 PyTorch 原生集成的 tmol，使 RosettaGenFF 力场能够高效批量计算并支持可微分优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理启发的力场可弥补生成式机器学习模型在训练分布之外泛化能力不足的问题，但传统力场计算速度较慢，且其导数难以融入机器学习框架，限制了应用。

Method: 开发 tmol 图形处理器原生库，在 PyTorch 中实现先进的 RosettaGenFF 力场，支持高效的批量可微分评分和整结构精修，并将其作为生成式蛋白质设计的奖励信号。

Result: 批量计算吞吐量最高提升 362 倍；用于生成式蛋白质设计时，tmol 提升了设计界面中的氢键和水桥数量。

Conclusion: tmol 将高性能、可微分的物理力场计算融入机器学习工作流，为蛋白质结构精修及生成设计提供了更高效的工具；摘要所述结果显示其有助于改善设计界面的相互作用。

Abstract: Physics-inspired forcefields complement generative machine learning models, showing better generalization beyond the training distribution. However, these forcefields are typically slow to evaluate, and their derivatives are difficult to integrate into ML frameworks. We introduce tmol, a GPU-native PyTorch library implementing the state-of-the-art RosettaGenFF forcefield. tmol supports efficient batched differentiable scoring and whole-structure refinement with up to 362-fold throughput increase (batched). Used as a reward in generative protein design, tmol increases hydrogen bonds and water bridges at designed interfaces.

</details>


### [200] [PromptPack: Training-Free Augmentation of Diffusion Editors for Intent-Aligned Spatial and Semantic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21865)
*Sophia Zalewski,Mustafa Munir,Enting Liu,Anjul Patney,David Dynerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重新训练的扩散图像编辑方案，通过明确指定可编辑区域及各区域对应的文本指令，提升编辑意图的落实程度，并保护指定内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式编辑流程对用户意图中的空间范围描述不足；修补式编辑虽能限定编辑区域，却难以说明不同区域分别应采用哪些编辑概念。因此，编辑结果可能出现内容放错位置或误改受保护区域的问题。

Method: 该方法为扩散编辑器提供两类显式编辑引导：一是将像素划分为“保留”“允许”和“修改”区域，规定编辑权限；二是将空间区域对应到不同文本提示，规定各处应生成的概念。引导信息可由用户手动指定；针对源图像中的对象编辑，也可借助文本定位的分割掩码自动生成。方法在不重新训练模型的情况下，将区域提示嵌入编辑器固定长度的文本序列，并利用引导信息限制注意力、控制流更新及恢复受保护的源图像像素。

Result: 作者将该方法集成到三种扩散编辑器中，并在图像编辑基准测试上验证，结果显示其能改善目标概念的区域放置，并更好地保留受保护内容。用户成对比较研究中，该方法相较于流编辑基线获得百分之八十七的偏好率。

Conclusion: 通过把编辑范围和区域—提示对应关系明确化，该方法为扩散图像编辑提供了实用且无需训练的意图控制接口。摘要报告的实验结果表明，它在多种编辑器上均能改善空间对齐和内容保留。

Abstract: Many image-editing tasks require confining changes to intended spatial regions while exactly preserving user-designated source content. Instruction-driven editing pipelines often leave such user-intent underspecified, while inpainting editors condition generation on explicitly defined edit support but not on the spatial assignment of individual edit concepts. PromptPack addresses both limitations by equipping diffusion editors with two explicit 'Edit Guides': an edit-role partition that labels each pixel as 'Keep,' 'Permit,' or 'Modify' to specify where edits may occur, and a regional-prompt assignment that maps spatial regions to text prompts to specify which concepts apply where. These guides may be manually specified or, for source-object edits, automatically derived from a text-grounded SAM 3 mask. Without retraining, PromptPack injects independently embedded regional prompts into the editor's fixed-length text sequence and uses the Edit Guides to spatially mask attention, gate flow updates, and restore protected source pixels. We integrate PromptPack into FlowEdit FLUX, FlowEdit SD3, and FLUX Kontext and demonstrate improved requested-concept placement and protected-content preservation across all three integrations on PIE-Bench++. In a pairwise user study, PromptPack achieves an 87% preference rate over FlowEdit FLUX, establishing PromptPack as a practical, training-free interface for intent-aligned diffusion editing.

</details>


### [201] [Adaptive Memory for Online Reinforcement Learning under Sparse Model Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21879)
*Sreejeet Maity,Sukanya Modak,Leonardo F. Toso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究环境在多次部署间发生稀疏变化时的折扣强化学习，提出按状态—动作对自适应选择历史数据的算法；在给定条件下，算法只需利用最近约为一除以变化比例的部署数据，并达到约为变化比例与每次部署样本量之比的平方根的长期平均性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次部署对应一个固定的马尔可夫决策过程，但奖励函数和转移概率可能在部署之间改变。从头学习会浪费过去仍然有效的数据，而汇总全部历史数据又可能因已变化的条目而产生偏差，因此需要在利用历史信息与避免陈旧数据之间取得平衡。

Method: 对每个状态—动作对分别估计奖励和下一状态分布。算法从当前部署的数据开始，按时间由近及远逐次加入旧部署数据；每加入一段历史，就检验不同历史范围的估计是否与单一共同估计相符。对奖励和转移分布分别进行检验，并采用最后一个通过检验的历史范围作为估计依据。随后基于拟合得到的马尔可夫决策过程计算控制策略。

Result: 在状态数与变化比例满足零小于状态数乘以变化比例且不超过二的条件下，算法只需使用最近数量级为一除以变化比例的部署数据。对于有限次部署，论文刻画了最优回报与学习者策略回报之间的平均差距；长期平均差距至多为带对数因子的变化比例与每次部署观测数之比的平方根量级。

Conclusion: 针对部署间稀疏变化，按状态—动作对自适应筛选历史数据能够兼顾数据复用与变化适应。该方法在特定条件下只依赖有限长度的近期历史，并给出随每次部署观测数增加而降低、随环境变化程度增大而提高的长期性能保证。

Abstract: We study the discounted reinforcement learning (RL) problem in a setting where the underlying environment changes between deployments. Each deployment provides $H$ observations from a fixed MDP. The reward function and transition kernel are fixed within each deployment. Across deployments, at most an $ε>0$ fraction of the reward and transition kernel entries may change. In this setting, learning each model to deploy from scratch may discard useful observations from past deployments, whereas pooling all past observations can bias the estimates of entries that have already changed. We then propose an algorithm that for each state and action pair, selects how much history to reuse to estimate the reward and probabilities of the next state. We start with observations from the current deployment and add earlier deployment observations one at a time, the most recent first. After each addition, we check whether a single average reward agrees with the estimates from all histories considered until that point. We apply the same check to the distribution of next states. For each estimate, we leverage the last history that passes these aforementioned checks. The optimal control policy is then computed on top of the fitted MDP. We characterize the average gap between the optimal return and the return of the learner's policies over a finite number of deployments. We demonstrate that when $0< |S|ε\leq 2$, with $|S|$ being the cardinality of the state space, our algorithm needs only the observations from the most recent $O(1/ε)$ deployments, and achieves a long-run average gap of at most $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{ε/H})$.

</details>


### [202] [When the tail is earned by its companions: measuring label dependence in long-tailed multi-label classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22859)
*Christoffer Löffler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，尾部标签的高召回可能依赖其常与之共现的其他标签；仅看宏平均指标无法揭示这种依赖，并提出训练前的风险界限及针对性的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有标签往往伴随高频标签出现，模型可能借助这些伴随标签的线索作出预测，却未真正学会识别该稀有标签。现有诊断通常需要先训练模型，而宏平均指标也无法区分标签出现时有无伴随标签的召回差异，因此难以判断语料是否容易出现这种问题，以及汇总指标掩盖了多少风险。

Method: 作者提出标签内估计量，衡量某标签在有伴随标签与无伴随标签时的召回差异；并给出判读规则：只有伴随依赖下降同时伴随无伴随标签时的召回上升，才算真正改善。此外，作者利用训练标签的共现信息构造训练前统计量，用于界定报告召回可能高于无伴随标签时召回的幅度，并比较不同训练目标、标签相关机制与模型架构。

Result: 在涵盖二十三个语料库、七种训练目标和五类标签相关机制的实验中，六个可定义该估计量的语料库，其无伴随标签时的召回均低于总体表现。按无伴随标签时的召回计算，尾部真阳性中最多有百分之十八点四属于宏平均指标所计入、但可能无法保留的部分；这一差距在所有语料库中均未超过训练前界限。长尾训练目标均未降低这种依赖，而模型架构会影响依赖程度；利用其他标签预测结果计算尾部得分的模型尤其依赖伴随标签。

Conclusion: 评估尾部标签模型时，整体召回和宏平均指标不足以证明模型真正掌握了稀有标签。应单独检查标签在缺少共现伴随项时的召回，并在训练前利用标签共现结构评估潜在风险。实验还表明，降低这种依赖不能仅靠更换长尾训练目标，架构选择同样关键。

Abstract: A rare label seldom occurs alone, and a classifier can recover it from the frequent labels it co-occurs with, its companions, without having learned the label itself. Existing diagnostics of this need a trained model: none says before training whether a corpus admits the failure, or what the standard aggregate hides. Macro-F1, like any measure built from per-label confusion counts, is unchanged by how a label's recall divides between occurrences with and without a companion. We introduce a within-label estimand of that division, a reading rule under which a fall in dependence counts only if companion-free recall rises, and a statistic of training labels that bounds, before training, how far reported recall can exceed companion-free recall. Across 23 corpora, 7 training objectives and five families of label-correlation mechanisms, companion-free recall falls short on all six corpora where the estimand is defined.
