Up to 18.4% of the tail true positives macro-F1 credits would vanish at the companion-free rate, within the training-label bound on every corpus. No long-tail objective reduces the dependence, but architecture moves it. Models whose tail score is computed from other labels' predictions are strongly dependent.

</details>


### [203] [Cancellation-Free Constrained SquareCB for Safe Controller Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21880)
*Kartik Pandit,Sourav Ganguly,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种带有截断成本权重的上下文 bandit 算法，在保证较低遗憾的同时减少约束违反；在无人机和建筑控制任务中，其违反量显著低于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常用由历史约束残差更新的拉格朗日乘子给成本加权，因而可能让某些轮次的约束松弛抵消其他轮次的超限。论文希望避免这种跨轮次抵消，并在每个上下文下都能控制预算违反。

Method: 在可实现性和带裕度 δ 的斯莱特条件下，采用方形置信界算法，并对成本设置截断权重：若估计成本不超过预算，权重为零；若超过预算，则只赋予足以使其回到预算的权重。该方法也可用于要求每个上下文分别满足预算的情形，即使上下文由对手选择。

Result: 设回归预言机的误差为 Regₛq(T)，算法的遗憾上界为 O(√(KT·Regₛq(T))/δ)，不发生跨轮次抵消的累计违反上界为 O(√(KT·Regₛq(T)))。在安全控制无人机和建筑控制基准任务中，违反量比原始—对偶方法和虚拟队列基线低 6 至 9 倍，遗憾则更低或相当。

Conclusion: 截断成本权重能直接针对当前超出预算的情况采取措施，避免用其他轮次的松弛掩盖违反。理论分析与控制任务实验均表明，该方法可在维持竞争性遗憾的同时，更有效地控制约束违反。

Abstract: We study constrained contextual bandits, in which a learner chooses one of $K$ actions in each round to minimize loss while keeping the expected cost of its play below a budget. Existing reductions to online regression weight the cost by a Lagrange multiplier learned from past constraint residuals, so slack in some rounds can offset violations in others. We instead run SquareCB proposed in \citep{foster2020beyond} with a rectified weight on the cost: it is zero when the estimated cost is within the budget, and otherwise just large enough to bring it back to the budget. Under realizability and Slater's condition with margin $δ$, the algorithm attains regret $O(\sqrt{KT\,\mathrm{Reg}_{\mathsf{Sq}}(T)}/δ)$ and cancellation-free violation $\sum_t(V_c^{p_t}-b)^+=O(\sqrt{KT\,\mathrm{Reg}_{\mathsf{Sq}}(T)})$, where $\mathrm{Reg}_{\mathsf{Sq}}$ is the error of the regression oracle. The same bounds hold when the budget must be met in every context, potentially adversarially chosen contexts. In controller selection for a safe-control-gym quadrotor and a BOPTEST building, the violation is 6 to 9 times lower than that of primal-dual and virtual-queue baselines, with a lower or comparable regret.

</details>


### [204] [WM-Quant: Rollout-Aware Post-Training Quantization for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21882)
*William Hammer,Andrew Heuer,Nikhil Krishna,Matthew Grenier,Ramtin Zand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专为预测式嵌入世界模型设计的训练后量化框架，通过保护关键计算路径并针对完整规划过程校准，缓解低精度量化造成的误差累积和动作排序失准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型参数量通常不大，但基于模型预测控制的动作规划需要进行数千次推理，因此计算开销仍然显著。训练后量化有望降低开销，但量化误差会在自回归预测的多步展开中逐步累积，使量化模型的动作排序接近随机，进而损害规划效果。

Method: 该框架包含三项设计：一是提高编码器前段的数值精度，以保护潜在状态和目标信息；二是保留控制路径的精度，使预测器能够区分细微动作差异；三是采用面向多步展开的尺度校准，联合校准编码器及各预测步骤的激活范围，而非只针对单次预测进行校准。

Result: 在多种世界模型、环境和训练后量化方法上的实验表明，该框架能显著提升低位宽量化下的规划成功率；在权重和激活均采用四位表示时，性能接近全精度模型。

Conclusion: 世界模型量化不能只关注单步预测精度，还必须兼顾动作区分能力及多步误差传播。通过保护关键路径并按完整展开过程校准，该方法能够以较低数值精度降低规划计算成本，同时较好地维持规划性能。

Abstract: We present WM-Quant, to our knowledge, the first post-training quantization (PTQ) framework designed specifically for JEPA-style world models. Although world models typically feature relatively few parameters, action planning algorithms often require thousands of inferences, which can quickly become computationally expensive. PTQ is a natural way to reduce this cost. However, we show that in world models using autoregressive model predictive control (MPC) rollouts, quantization error can compound with each predictor step, resulting in action rankings from the quantized model becoming near-random. WM-Quant addresses this with three components that build on existing PTQ methods: early-encoder precision, control-path preservation, and rollout-aware scaling. Early-encoder precision protects accurate latent state and goal information to mitigate long-term error propagation. Control-path preservation allows the world model predictor to distinguish between fine-grained actions, significantly improving action rankings and execution. Rollout-aware scaling jointly calibrates encoder and per-step predictor activation ranges with respect to an entire rollout rather than a single prediction, reducing the cumulative rollout error. Across multiple world models, environments, and PTQ methods, WM-Quant substantially improves planning success at low bit-widths, approaching full-precision performance at W4A4.

</details>


### [205] [World-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Pollution-Aware Bird Movement Modeling Using a Multi-Instance DQN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21895)
*Shivin Mangal,Divyansh Chawla,Anshu Garg,Isshaan Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以空气质量指数作为环境反馈的多实例强化学习模拟，用于学习鸟类在污染条件下的自适应导航策略；深度价值网络在稳定性、适应性和累计表现方面优于所比较的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强化学习方法依赖人为偏好或合成奖励，难以体现真实生态影响。论文希望直接利用环境结果作为训练信号，使鸟类导航策略能根据污染状况作出反应，并探索可扩展的生态决策建模方式。

Method: 研究构建印度二维网格环境，整合预先计算的空气质量指数矩阵与地理行政边界，并结合塞诺坎托鸟鸣数据库中的鸟类鸣叫元数据。系统将空气质量指数作为环境反馈，引导多个智能体学习导航策略；为降低开销，以结构化空气质量映射替代训练和执行期间反复调用实时接口。实验比较蒙特卡洛方法、表格型强化学习和价值迭代等基线，以及深度价值网络。

Result: 摘要报告称，深度价值网络在不同环境条件下表现出更强的稳定性、适应性和累计表现，整体优于所比较的基线方法。摘要未提供具体数值、实验规模或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将真实环境指标用作强化学习反馈信号的可行框架，为污染约束下的生态导航决策提供了可扩展的模拟基础。不过，摘要所述结果仍需结合详细实验数据、环境验证及对真实鸟类行为的评估来判断其实际生态适用性。

Abstract: Birds are modeled as environmentally sensitive agents whose movement reflects real-world air quality conditions, where AQI serves as a measurable world feedback signal representing pollution-driven consequences. Instead of relying on human preference or synthetic rewards, the system uses environmental outcomes as the primary training signal, enabling policy learning grounded in ecological impact. We propose a multi-instance reinforcement learning simulation in which agents navigate a 2D grid representation of India integrated with a precomputed AQI matrix and geographic state boundaries. The framework combines bird call metadata from the Xeno-canto dataset, spatial discretization, and real environmental data to learn adaptive navigation strategies under pollution constraints. To ensure scalability and efficiency, repeated real-time API queries are replaced by a structured AQI mapping that enables fast lookup during training and execution. Multiple baselines, including Monte Carlo, Q-Learning, and Value Iteration, are evaluated for comparison. Deep Q-Network demonstrates stronger stability, adaptability, and cumulative performance across varying environmental conditions, establishing a scalable foundation for world-feedback driven ecological decision modeling.

</details>


### [206] [A Temporal Algebraic Framework for Data Stream Evaluation Under Delayed Labels and Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21914)
*Ronaldo C. Prati,Elaine Ribeiro de Faria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出适用于数据流的分类评估框架，可处理类别比例变化、标签延迟与选择性反馈，并区分先验分布变化和概念漂移。实验显示，基于先验无关指标的检测器在标签比例变化下不会额外触发误报；延迟标签校正对召回率的恢复较有效，但对其他指标效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流中的标签可能延迟到达或只对部分样本提供，类别比例也会随时间改变，短时间窗口还可能缺少稀有类别。常规评估容易把类别比例变化误判为分类边界退化；若标签延迟或由模型选择性获取，评估结果还会产生偏差。因此需要能适应动态数据、反馈不完整并区分不同变化来源的评估方法。

Method: 扩展基于混淆矩阵的代数框架，在时间维度上采用指数衰减或滑动窗口加权，统一计算总体（微平均）和逐类（宏平均）指标；每步更新成本为类别数平方量级，且总体指标始终是逐类指标的加权组合。对尚未获得标签或未被标注的样本，使用模型校准后的类别概率暂作计数，并以概率校准误差刻画临时估计偏差；对没有已核验样本的类别则不报告指标。为区分先验分布变化与概念漂移，监控召回率、特异度等不受类别先验影响的坐标，并通过一阶雅可比近似分解两种变化对指标的影响。

Result: 在合成数据流和四个真实数据集上的实验中，基于先验无关坐标且按类别保留记忆的检测器，在标签比例变化时没有增加误报；相比之下，雅可比分解告警器和错误率检测器会产生额外误报。延迟标签校正在部分真实场景中恢复了召回率，但对精确率、特异度和Ｆ一值的改善不完整。研究还使用形式化证明工具核验了二十五条性质。

Conclusion: 该框架为标签延迟、选择性反馈和类别比例漂移并存的数据流提供了统一的加权评估与监测方案。先验无关指标有助于避免把类别比例变化误报为概念漂移；不过，延迟标签带来的偏差只能部分校正，尤其是精确率、特异度和Ｆ一值仍需谨慎解释。

Abstract: In data streams, labels often arrive late or only for some instances, class prevalence drifts, and rare classes vanish from short windows. Standard evaluation then confuses a degraded decision boundary with a change in prevalence, and is biased when labels are delayed or selected by the model. We extend an algebraic framework that obtains pooled (micro) and per-class (macro) measures from one confusion matrix to streams, weighting the counts in time by exponential fading or a sliding window; the update costs O(m^2) per step, whatever the stream length, and each pooled measure stays a weighted combination of the per-class ones. Delayed and selective feedback enter as an observation mask: until its label arrives, an instance is counted with the model's calibrated class probabilities, and the bias of this interim estimate is driven by their calibration error. Audited conventions report no value for a class without verified instances. We separate prior shift from concept drift by monitoring prior-free coordinates (recall and specificity) and attribute a change in a measure to the two sources with a first-order Jacobian decomposition. On synthetic streams and four real datasets, a detector on these coordinates with a memory per class raises no excess false alarms under label shift, unlike the Jacobian alarm and error-rate detectors. The correction for delayed labels recovers recall in some real settings and is only partial for precision, specificity and F1. Twenty-five statements are checked in Lean 4, and the code is in the aggmetrics package.

</details>


### [207] [Lost in a Few Heads: Diagnosing and Repairing Block Selection in Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21917)
*Wenhe Zhang,Boxing Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏注意力的长文本检索损失，主要不是固定预算不足，而是少数注意力头的选择器漏掉了关键内容。针对这些头恢复全注意力或改进评分器，可在保留大部分速度收益的同时显著提升检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力通常为每个查询分配固定数量的键块，但在长文本、多关键项检索任务中，准确率仍可能明显低于全注意力。论文试图定位损失来源：究竟是预算太小，还是选择器未能选中承载答案的关键内容。

Method: 研究者先用精确注意力质量选择作为同等预算下的参照，判断选择器是否漏选关键内容；再通过强制提供正确答案的校准探测，逐头测量两项指标：选择器舍弃的关键内容注意力，以及修正该头的选择后，答案对数概率沿着从原选择器到全注意力的变化路径所恢复的比例。依据这些指标识别盲区注意力头，并比较两种修复方式：在这些头启用全注意力，或使用四位量化评分器且保持原预算。研究还提出按校准效果设定阈值的统一筛选规则，并在多种稀疏注意力机制上验证。

Result: 多个模型系列在多关键项检索中的损失，集中于少数盲区注意力头；这些头虽关注关键内容，但对键的均值池化摘要会稀释其中唯一关键的词元。在一个八十亿参数模型的六万四千长度测试中，修复盲区头后准确率从百分之五十四点一升至百分之九十七点五，接近所有头均使用全注意力的水平。另一模型中，只对二百八十八个键值头中的十八个启用全注意力，就使准确率从百分之二十九点八升至百分之九十七点七。生产推理引擎测试显示，在三万二千至十二万八千长度下，只修复这些头仍保留稀疏注意力所节省首词元生成时间的百分之八十四至百分之八十九。类似盲区头也出现在多种机制中；而另一种机制则存在较多此类头。

Conclusion: 长上下文稀疏注意力的检索退化，往往由少数头的选择器失误造成，而非整体预算耗尽。逐头诊断并只对关键头进行修复，可以在无需训练的情况下大幅弥补准确率损失，同时保留大部分推理速度优势。

Abstract: Sparse attention models read a few blocks of keys for every query at a fixed budget and can lose much of their long-context retrieval accuracy at that budget. We ask where this loss is made. Using exact attention mass selection at the shipped budget as an oracle, we show that the selector under-ranks the needle rather than running out of budget. We find attention heads that miss the needle by running a calibration probe that teacher-forces the answer and reads two quantities in every head. The first is the needle attention that the selector forfeits, and the second is the share of the answer's log-probability that fixing the head's selection restores, integrated along the path from the shipped selector to full attention. Four checkpoints of the InfLLM-V2 family and NOSA-8B lose multi-key retrieval in the same three blind heads, which attend to the needle while a mean-pooled key summary dilutes its single decisive token. Full attention in those heads or a 4-bit scorer that keeps the shipped budget recovers most of the loss without training. On MiniCPM4.1-8B at 64K, either fix lifts multi-key retrieval accuracy from 54.1 to 97.5%, level with full attention in every head. In production serving engines, full attention in those heads keeps 84 to 89% of the time to first token (TTFT) that sparse attention saves at 32K to 128K context length. Beyond the InfLLM-V2 selector lineage, one unified rule flags attention heads above a threshold swept on each checkpoint until they close most of the gap to full attention on calibration prompts. A similar small set of blind attention heads carry the loss for the SeerAttention-R gates, NSA, and DashAttention, whereas many such heads exist in MoBA. On Qwen3-8B with the SeerAttention-R gates, full attention in 18 of 288 key-value heads lifts multi-key retrieval accuracy from 29.8 to 97.7%.

</details>


### [208] [KVUse: Reusable-KV User Memory for Efficient LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21920)
*Xin Guo,Qiang Zhang,Xiang Zhou,Arushi Rai,Siyu Lin,Yifan Wu,Xiang Shen,Lei Huang,Qunshu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将用户画像直接保存为可复用键值缓存的方法，减少推荐排序中的重复预填充计算，在基本不损失排序质量的同时显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的推荐系统通常以文本形式保存用户画像。每次排序调用都要将画像插入提示词，并在预填充阶段重新计算其键值缓存；由于排序请求频繁、提示词较长，这一重复计算成为主要服务开销。传统前缀缓存难以解决问题，因为画像前面的文本会随调用变化。

Method: 提出键值缓存复用方案：通过自蒸馏训练轻量低秩适配写入器，使画像更新过程在生成画像时同步产出可复用的键值缓存。后续调用可在新的位置和上下文中读取该缓存，无须重新计算画像文本对应的缓存。此外，设计专用缓存调度器，在当前批次计算期间将下一批所需缓存从主机内存传至图形处理器，以计算过程掩蔽传输开销。

Result: 在四个指令推荐数据集及一款八十亿参数指令模型上的实验显示，经过连续多轮记忆更新后，该方法的排序质量与完整预填充相比没有统计显著差异。在负载饱和时，下游评分路径吞吐量提升四至四点五倍，画像更新路径吞吐量提升一点四至一点九倍。

Conclusion: 将用户画像从文本转为可复用键值缓存，可避免排序请求反复执行画像预填充计算。该方法在维持排序质量的同时提高了服务效率，适用于重复调用且预填充开销占主导的记忆增强推荐系统。

Abstract: Memory-augmented recommenders built on large language models (LLMs) maintain a natural-language profile of each user. These profiles are updated by a user profile update path that revises the profile as new interactions arrive and are consumed by downstream task paths like ranking. Because these user profiles are stored as text in the user memory, every ranking LLM call that consumes them must splice them in the prompt and calculate the KV cache of them during the prefill phase. Given these ranking LLM calls are prefill heavy (long prompt but short decoded output) and repeatedly called, these repeated prefill of user profile text dominates the serving. Prefix caching cannot remove it, because these user profiles are preceded by different text in different calls. We present KVUse, which stores the user profiles as reusable key-value (KV) caches instead of text. KVUse trains lightweight LoRA writers by self-distillation, so that the user profile update call produces the reusable KV of the user profile while decoding it, and the LLM can read these reusable KV at new positions and behind new context without recomputation. KVUse also has a customized KV scheduler that uploads the stored reusable KV of the next batch while the current batch is computed, hiding the KV transfer from host memory behind GPU computation. On four InstructRec datasets with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, KVUse shows no statistically significant difference in ranking quality from full prefill over continuous memory update rounds. Under saturated load, KVUse raises the throughput of the downstream scoring path by 4.0x-4.5x and of the profile update path by 1.4x-1.9x.

</details>


### [209] [Physics-informed neural networks for spacecraft trajectory optimization with exact boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21933)
*Samuel Schönherr,Günther Waxenegger-Wilfing,Sascha Ranftl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过函数变换将轨迹的初末边界条件直接嵌入神经网络表示，使轨迹优化无需边界惩罚项，并改善优化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规物理信息神经网络将初始与终端状态作为加权损失项处理，形成多目标优化；结果容易受权重选择影响，导致收敛不佳、精度下降。研究旨在更稳定地满足轨迹边界约束。

Method: 将位置表示为时间的神经网络函数，并以推力最小化为目标。利用轨迹优化边界维度较低这一特点，构造满足边界条件的函数变换，将约束直接嵌入解的表示中，相当于对可行解空间重新参数化，从而把原约束问题转化为仅针对可行轨迹的无约束单目标优化。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够显著改善优化的收敛表现和解的精度。摘要未提供具体数值或与各基线方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 将边界条件通过结构化变换精确地嵌入神经网络，可避免对边界惩罚权重的依赖，并提升轨迹优化的稳定性与准确性；该思路尤其适用于边界结构简单、维度较低的轨迹问题。

Abstract: We use neural networks to optimize trajectories by modeling the position as a function of time and minimizing the thrust with respect to the network parameters. This corresponds to an unsupervised training procedure in which physical laws are incorporated directly into the optimization objective. In standard physics-informed neural networks, initial and terminal states are enforced through additional weighted loss terms, resulting in a multi-objective optimization problem that is highly sensitive to the choice of loss weights and can lead to poor convergence and reduced accuracy. To address this challenge, we construct a simple transformation, exploiting the mathematical structure of trajectory optimization problems, to enforce boundary conditions exactly a-priori. This transformation can be interpreted as a reparameterization of the solution space that embeds the boundary conditions directly into the function. This converts the original constrained optimization problem into an unconstrained one over admissible trajectories. I.e. the transformation eliminates the boundary condition penalty terms and reduces the problem to a single-objective optimization. Crucially, trajectory optimization comes with a low-dimensional boundary, which allows particularly simple and stable constructions of distance functions compared to general domains in partial differential equations. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach meaningfully improves convergence behavior and solution accuracy.

</details>


### [210] [Higher-Order Interaction Reconstruction via Integral Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21935)
*Xin Mao,Zhen Xu,Shiyi Yang,Sukwon Yun,Yunmei Liu,Ren Wang,Tianlong Chen,Can Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于积分形式的物理信息神经网络，用于在噪声和观测稀疏条件下重建高阶相互作用，并通过理论分析和多类系统实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶相互作用重建有助于理解生态、社会和生物网络，但该逆问题受到组合结构复杂、动力学非线性强，以及观测含噪且稀疏等因素制约。基于导数的物理残差还可能放大噪声并引发数值不稳定，因此需要更稳健的重建方法。

Method: 提出积分物理信息神经网络，在时间区间上采用积分形式约束动力学，而非惩罚基于导数的物理残差，以抑制高频噪声并减轻导数放大造成的不稳定。通过连续参数化相互作用张量，使高阶结构能够借助梯度优化进行识别；同时分析方法的误差界，并提出该界在满足激励条件的积分窗口下不受导数放大影响。

Result: 在生态动力学、社会传播和神经元同步系统上的实验表明，该方法在含噪和稀疏观测下仍能准确、稳健地重建高阶相互作用，并优于论文所比较的现有先进方法。摘要未提供具体数值指标。

Conclusion: 积分形式为高阶相互作用重建提供了对噪声更稳健的物理约束方式。结合连续参数化和理论误差分析，该框架有望缓解稀疏、含噪观测下的重建难题，并适用于多类复杂网络动力学系统。

Abstract: Reconstructing higher-order interactions is a fundamental challenge in complex networked systems, with applications in ecological, social, and biological networks. This inverse problem is inherently difficult due to the combinatorial complexity of higher-order structures, the strong nonlinearity of the underlying dynamics, and the prevalence of noisy and sparse observations. In this article, we propose a novel integral physics-informed neural network (IntegralPINN) framework for higher-order interaction reconstruction. Instead of { penalizing derivative-based physics residuals,} we adopt an integral formulation over temporal intervals, which naturally suppresses high-frequency noise and mitigates derivative-induced instability. We further introduce a continuous parameterization of interaction tensors, enabling tractable identification of higher-order structures via gradient-based optimization. We provide theoretical analysis establishing a noise-robust error bound that is independent of derivative amplification when the integration window satisfies an excitation condition. Extensive experiments on ecological dynamics, social contagion, and neuronal synchronization systems demonstrate that IntegralPINN achieves accurate and robust reconstruction under noisy and sparse observations, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [211] [A Log-Timescale Perturbation Bound for Selective State Space Models at Every Sequence Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21952)
*Jack Humphries,Daniel Midgley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以对数尺度衡量时间尺度变化的稳定性分析方法，证明选择性扫描单元的扰动界对任意序列长度均成立、随输入变化趋零，且无需假设时间尺度有正的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曼巴模型用于限价订单簿等流式数据时，实际序列往往远长于训练序列，因此需要对任意序列长度都有效的隐藏状态稳定性保证。已有分析以加法方式度量时间尺度变化；若时间尺度没有正下界，误差界会随序列长度线性增长。

Method: 改为在对数尺度上度量时间尺度的变化。关键观察是，每一步的衰减因子变化至多与该步输入权重成固定比例，因此逐步误差不会不断累积。基于此，论文证明：从相同初始状态出发的两次选择性扫描运行，其隐藏状态差异具有适用于任意序列长度的上界；该上界随输入扰动幅度趋于零，且不要求时间尺度有正下界。论文还构造了小扰动情形下的例子，表明该界距最优界约不超过两倍，并在时间尺度有上界时将分析扩展到曼巴公开代码中的更新规则。

Result: 作者将定理转化为块级界，并在两个使用一日限价订单簿数据训练的小模型上进行评估。时间尺度有界时，基于既有分析的界最多只在前八步更紧；采用软加时间尺度、其可证明的下界低至十的负二十一次方时，本文的界在所有序列长度上都更紧。不过，本文的界虽然适用于块的任意输入，仍比梯度攻击找到的实际变化大五个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 对数尺度分析消除了既有稳定性界对时间尺度正下界的依赖，并提供了可覆盖任意序列长度的扰动保证，且小扰动下接近理论最优。实验显示该方法相较既有分析在长序列或极小时间尺度情形更有优势，但所得界仍较保守，与实际攻击观察到的扰动幅度相差显著。

Abstract: Mamba models deployed on streaming data, such as limit order book updates, run far past the sequence lengths they were trained on. A guarantee on how much a small change of input can move the hidden state therefore has to hold at every length. The closest existing bound we know of measures the change of the timescale additively, and it grows linearly with the sequence length unless the timescale is bounded away from zero. In this paper, we measure the change on a log scale instead. The key observation is that the decay factor of each step then moves by at most a fixed fraction of that step's input weight, so the per-step errors cannot pile up. Using this, we prove that two runs of a selective-scan cell from a common start stay within a bound that holds at every length, shrinks to zero with the size of the change and needs no lower bound on the timescale. We give a construction showing that, for small changes, this bound is within a factor of about 2 of the best possible, and we extend it to the update used in Mamba's released code when the timescale is bounded above. We then evaluate block-level bounds built from the theorem on two small models trained on one day of order book data. With a bounded timescale, a bound built on the existing analysis is tighter than ours for at most the first 8 steps. With Mamba's softplus timescale, whose certified lower bound is as small as $10^{-21}$, it is looser at every length. Our bounds hold for every input to a block, but they remain more than five orders of magnitude above the changes that gradient attacks find.

</details>


### [212] [Weight-space programming for tractor guidance controller: large language models natively write neural network weights that steer a real tractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21958)
*Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，大语言模型可直接读写神经网络权重，为自动拖拉机导航修复或编写控制器；借助少量仿真反馈，两种模型均能成功，实车表现接近纯追踪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络控制器在更换设备或执行器特性改变后，通常需要重新训练或依赖优化算法。研究探索能否让大语言模型依据自然语言要求直接修改网络权重，并像控制工程师一样分析闭环行为，从而快速适配控制器。

Method: 研究采用含二百五十三个参数的神经网络控制拖拉机沿直线与圆弧路径行驶。两种大语言模型分别在少量仿真评估下，修复为另一台拖拉机训练的控制器，或从全零权重开始编写控制器；并与进化策略、梯度下降及纯追踪方法比较，测试环境包括仿真和真实拖拉机。

Result: 两种模型均能正确读取网络并进行闭环控制分析。没有仿真反馈时，模型修复控制器仅偶尔成功；加入仿真反馈后，两者都能在单次交互中修复已有控制器，也都能从零权重编写可工作的控制器，而进化策略未能从零找到可用方案。新写出的控制器结构稀疏且呈经典级联形式；梯度下降虽精度更高，但使用了多得多的仿真。所有大语言模型控制器都能在真实拖拉机上无需人工干预地转向，误差与纯追踪处于同一量级。

Conclusion: 直接通过语言模型编程神经网络权重，有望成为适配和设计控制器的新途径，作者将其称为“权重空间工程”。结果显示，少量仿真反馈对可靠成功十分关键；该方法已展现实车可行性，但其精度、稳定性与适用范围仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Weight-space programming is a process where a large language model reads and writes the weights of a neural network directly, guided by a natural language specification, without an optimiser or gradient descent. This study applies it to automatic tractor guidance, where a 253-parameter neural network steers along paths of lines and arcs and must be adapted when the machine changes, such as a slower steering actuator. GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1 (also 5.2) edited the network for our research tractor using a small budget of simulated evaluations to repair a network trained for another tractor and write a controller into a network with zero weights. We compared them with an evolution strategy, gradient descent and pure pursuit in simulation and on the real tractor. Both models read the network and analysed the closed loop with control theory correctly, and Fable's repair without simulation succeeded only occasionally. With simulated feedback, both models repaired the trained network in single sessions and wrote a working controller from zero weights, whereas from zero the evolution strategy found none. The controllers written from zero were sparse and in a classical cascaded structure; one model edited the trained network while the other replaced it. Gradient descent reached higher accuracy with far more simulation, and all language model controllers steered the real tractor without intervention, with errors of the same order as pure pursuit. Language models can program the weights of a neural controller directly, much as control engineers design one. We call this Weight-Space Engineering.

</details>


### [213] [Solver to System: LLM Reasoning Across Representations and Execution in Scientific Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21964)
*Eliza Berman,Rohan Subramanian,Shreya Jha,Lohit Jagarapu,Romain Lopez,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用于评估大语言模型理解偏微分方程物理系统能力的数据集，并发现模型在跨表征推理和物理错误诊断方面存在不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程模拟器广泛用于描述物理系统，但大语言模型生成和理解求解器代码仍有难度。科学模拟适合作为检验科学推理的对象：同一系统可由代码、方程、自然语言和数值轨迹表示；而代码即使能够运行，也可能违背物理规律。因此，仅检查程序能否执行不足以判断模型是否真正理解系统。

Method: 研究构建了“求解器到系统”数据集，收录具有多种表征的偏微分方程求解器，并加入受控错误和词汇扰动。评测关注两类能力：判断不同表征之间是否一致，以及通过静态分析或智能体交互检测物理无效性。实验覆盖八种开放权重大语言模型，并考察代码执行对判断的影响。

Result: 模型在证据相互冲突时会系统性偏向某些表征；即便其他表征保持不变，仅改变代码中的词汇也会影响模型对它们的解读。执行代码有助于提升物理有效性判断，但较弱的智能体即使判断正确，也可能无法指出导致错误的因果缺陷。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可能给出正确的物理有效性结论，却未能一致地协调不同表征，也可能误诊错误原因。因此，评估模型的科学推理不能只看最终判断，还应检验其跨表征一致性以及对物理缺陷因果机制的识别能力。

Abstract: Simulators based on partial differential equations (PDEs) are widely used to model physical systems, but generating and interpreting PDE solver code with large language models (LLMs) remains challenging. Scientific simulation code is a useful testbed for LLM scientific reasoning because (1) one system can be represented by code, equations, natural language, and numerical trajectories and (2) code can execute successfully while implementing invalid physics. We introduce Solver2System, a dataset of PDE solvers with multiple representations, controlled corruptions, and lexical perturbations, to evaluate whether LLMs can recover the underlying physical system through (1) cross-representation consistency and (2) static and agentic detection of physical invalidity. Across eight open-weight models, LLMs systematically favor some representations when evidence conflicts, and lexical perturbations to code alter interpretation of other unchanged representations. Execution improves physical-validity judgments, but weaker agents can judge correctly without identifying the causal defect. Thus, LLMs may reach correct judgments despite inconsistent representation reconciliation or causal misdiagnosis.

</details>


### [214] [Large Language Models as Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: The Three Clocks of Depth, Training, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21983)
*Maor Farid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将非线性动力学与语言模型联系起来，提出涵盖网络层、训练过程和生成过程的“三时钟词典”，并用实验检验若干动力学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在网络层迭代、训练更新和逐词生成这三个尺度上，都会呈现固定点、分岔、同步、混沌及噪声驱动逃逸等现象。由于非线性动力学与语言模型研究长期脱节，相关规律常被重新发现或以不同术语描述。论文旨在建立两领域之间系统、可检验的概念桥梁。

Method: 作者梳理约两百篇研究，构建“三时钟词典”，将十五种非线性动力学概念对应到三个尺度，并标明每项对应关系属于理论证明、实测结果或猜想，同时并列展示机械系统与语言模型中的实例。随后推导低阶模型，将软最大函数解释为玻尔兹曼分布、梯度下降解释为非线性弹簧上的欧拉步进，并以库拉莫托模型和势阱等描述注意力同步与重复循环。最后在开放语言模型上开展实验，检验重复循环逃逸、训练稳定性及文本生成温度等现象。

Result: 实验发现，生成式预训练变换器第二版和“智慧”系列模型从重复循环中逃逸的规律符合阿伦尼乌斯定律，逻辑值差可视为势垒；势垒随循环加深后趋于饱和，因此离开循环所需温度随生成长度增加而按对数尺度下降，并随模型规模增大而上升。连贯文本对应一个较窄的温度区间，小型推理模型建议使用的温度也落在该区间。训练会增强“智慧”系列模型各层的扩张性；一个小型变换器则在多模态稳定性边缘附近训练。基于逃逸分析设计的控制器虽能减少重复循环，但尚未优于最佳固定温度。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一个连接非线性动力学与语言模型的统一框架，并以低阶模型和实验展示其解释力。现有结果有助于理解重复生成、训练稳定性和采样温度，但尚未转化为优于现有基线的控制方法。作者据此提出十二个开放问题，认为利用动力学理论解决语言模型问题仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Language models are dynamical systems. A transformer is iterated over its layers, over the steps of its training and over the tokens it generates, and on each of these three clocks it shows behavior that nonlinear dynamics has studied for a century: fixed points, bifurcations, synchronization, chaos and noise-driven escape from potential wells. The two communities rarely meet, so this behavior is often rediscovered under new names. We build a bridge between them. To describe, we condense about 200 papers into the Three-Clock Dictionary, which maps fifteen concepts of nonlinear dynamics onto each clock and marks every correspondence as proved, measured or conjectured, and we show each concept in a mechanical system and in a language model side by side. To explain, we derive reduced-order models: the softmax is a Boltzmann distribution, gradient descent an Euler step on a nonlinear spring, a loss spike a ball thrown out of a stiff valley, attention on the circle a Kuramoto model, and a repetition loop a well that deepens while it is occupied. We then test these explanations on open language models, on a laptop CPU. Escape from repetition loops in GPT-2 and Pythia follows the Arrhenius law with the logit gap as the barrier, and the barrier deepens and then saturates, so the temperature needed to leave a loop falls like $1/\ln N$ with the generation length $N$ and rises with model size. Coherent text lives in a narrow window of temperatures, which a small reasoning model shares at its recommended temperature. Training makes each Pythia layer more expansive, and a small transformer trains at a multi-mode edge of stability. The third step, solving problems of language models, is still ahead: controllers derived from the escape analysis remove loops but do not yet beat the best fixed temperature. We state twelve open problems where nonlinear dynamics could get there.

</details>


### [215] [How Much Distribution Shift Can a Clustering Withstand? Exact Split and Merge Certificates for Structured Transports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22000)
*Alisina Bayati,Srinivasa Salapaka,Shahriar Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于局部稳定性的聚类鲁棒性度量，并用半定规划精确计算结构化分布变化导致聚类分裂或合并的临界幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的待聚类人群分布可能不确定或随时间变化。分布偏移会改变已形成的簇：簇内成员可能变得过于分散，需要分裂；不同簇也可能变得足够接近，可以合并。论文旨在量化聚类对这类变化的稳健程度。

Method: 作者借鉴确定性退火中的相变分析，以局部稳定性准则严格定义簇的分裂与合并事件。针对位移受指定子空间约束的结构化分布变化，分别定义触发分裂和合并所需的最小变化幅度，即分裂半径与合并半径，并将其精确改写为半定规划问题。其中，分裂问题的半定规划仅含两个标量决策变量。

Result: 所得凸优化表述能够精确求出结构化分布变化触发各类分裂或合并阈值的最小幅度。进一步结合这些半径，作者构造了整个聚类的鲁棒性证书，用以衡量达到任一受监测阈值所需的最小结构化变化。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布不确定或随时间变化时的聚类稳健性提供了可计算的分析框架，能够分别评估聚类对分裂和合并的敏感程度，并汇总为针对完整聚类的鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: In clustering, the distribution of the population being clustered is often uncertain or changes over time, so the true distribution may shift away from the nominal distribution used to form the clustering. Once the clustering is committed and each member is assigned to a cluster, such shifts may change the distribution of each cluster. They can make a cluster too spread out for one representative at a given resolution, motivating a split, or bring distinct clusters close enough to share a representative at that resolution, motivating a merge. We make these split and merge events precise using a local stability criterion built on the phase-transition analysis in deterministic annealing. This notion of stability enables us to quantify how robust a clustering is against such events. Furthermore, motivated by applications in which uncertainty and population change are structured, we study shifts whose displacements are restricted to a prescribed subspace. The smallest structured shifts that reach the respective thresholds define the split and merge radii, for which we obtain exact semidefinite programming (SDP) reformulations. The split SDP has only two scalar decision variables. These convex reformulations enable us to combine the radii into a robustness certificate for the full clustering that gives the size of the smallest structured shift that reaches any monitored split or merge threshold.

</details>


### [216] [An AI-Based OSSE Framework to Evaluate Learned Physical Consistency: A Multi-Model Study Using FourCastNet, Aurora, Prithvi WxC, and GraphCast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22024)
*Yash Diggikar,Milton Halem,Aishwarya Raman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于人工智能天气模型的观测系统模拟实验框架，比较不同模型利用新增观测改善大气状态估计的能力；结果显示，模型响应存在显著差异，基础模型整体影响较强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测系统模拟实验是评估未来大气观测系统的重要方法，但传统评估依赖物理数值天气模型。随着人工智能天气模型兴起，尚不清楚它们能否像传统模型一样，从新增观测中获取可比的物理影响。因此，本文旨在建立人工智能模型评估框架，并检验不同架构对观测信息的响应。

Method: 研究选取四种架构不同的人工智能天气模型：四卡斯特网第二版、图形卡斯特、奥罗拉和普里特维天气基础模型。各模型进行自回归积分，模拟六十天预报；经过三十天自旋调整后，分别插入欧洲中期天气预报中心第五代再分析资料中的温度场或风场，以检验模型能否据此推断风场及其他大气变量。研究比较有无观测插入时，模型在三十天资料同化循环预报中的响应。

Result: 不同模型对相同观测的响应差异明显，两个基础模型奥罗拉和普里特维天气基础模型总体产生最强影响。除图形卡斯特表现最弱外，其余模型均能捕捉温度插入引起的温度位势高度响应；两个基础模型还保留了温度插入后五百百帕热带纬向风的正向且非平凡响应。此外，即使在同一架构内，不同观测插入实验的效果也可能有较大差异。研究还发现，图形卡斯特采用完整三十七层配置时存在架构特有的数值不稳定性：温度插入会在高层引发失控反馈；排除一百百帕及以上的气压层后，该问题可以得到纠正。

Conclusion: 人工智能天气模型并非以相同方式利用新增观测，其观测影响取决于模型架构与具体插入方案。基础模型在本实验中表现较强，但模型间差异和高层数值不稳定性表明，评估未来观测系统时必须针对不同模型开展专门测试，并审慎处理垂直层配置。

Abstract: Observing System Simulation Experiments (OSSEs) are a central methodology for evaluating future atmospheric observing systems. We present the first AI-based OSSE framework to test whether different AI weather models can reproduce comparable physical impact from new observations that traditional physics based models generate. We evaluate four distinct architectural AI models on the Charney conjecture that incomplete historical observations can infer the present state of the atmospheric. We considner- FourCastNet V2, GraphCast, Aurora and Prithvi WxC and integrate each autoregressively over a 60-day forecast horizon. After a 30-day spin-up, we insert ERA5 temperature fields to infer winds and related atmospheric fields. Similar OSSE experiments are conducted for wind insertion. We measure the performance of each model's response to observational information on a 30-day data assimilation cycle forecast with and without data insertion. Distinct architectural AI weather models respond differently to the same OSSE observations. Foundation models (i.e. Prithvi WxC and Aurora), produce the strongest impact. For example, while GraphCast is the weakest, all three of the four models capture the temperature-to-geopotential height response. The two foundational models retain a positive, non-trivial, tropical zonal wind at 500 hPa response to temperature insertion. Various data insertion experiments can vary significantly even within a given architecture. We identify an architecture-specific numerical instability in GraphCast's full 37-level configuration that produces a runaway feedback concentrated at the uppermost levels with ERA5 temperature insertion correctable by excluding levels at or below 100 hPa.

</details>


### [217] [GP--RL--MPC: Uncertainty-Aware Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control with Gaussian Process Postdecision State-Value Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22038)
*Jon Li,Jay H. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种融合高斯过程强化学习与模型预测控制的方法，用学习到的价值函数补偿预测时域之外的长期成本；在连续搅拌釜反应器任务中，相比匹配基线，平均降低平方误差总和百分之八十点五二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限预测时域的模型预测控制难以考虑时域之外的长期成本；而强化学习价值函数虽可补足长期评估，却面临样本有限、观测含噪以及价值估计不确定等问题。论文旨在将两者结合，并提高数据效率、控制稳定性及对不确定性的处理能力。

Method: 将高斯过程回归学习得到的价值函数作为模型预测控制的终端惩罚项，以估计预测时域之外的成本；在乐观近似策略迭代中，利用少量含噪轨迹学习价值函数，并通过软更新缓解策略抖动。引入可调风险系数，对终端成本中的认知不确定性加权；采用后决策状态，将终端转移之后的扰动平均计算转移至离线，以处理偶然不确定性。在目标函数与可行动作一致时，单步预测时域可退化为基于价值空间的贪心强化学习。为降低数据集增大后的在线计算开销，先以精确边际似然拟合高斯过程超参数，再通过枢轴化乔列斯基分解选择诱导点，构造稀疏确定性训练条件后验。

Result: 在受外部扰动的连续搅拌釜反应器任务中，采用适当的风险系数和策略筛选后，该方法相较于其他设置匹配的基线模型预测控制，在五个随机种子上的平方误差总和平均降低百分之八十点五二；样本标准差为百分之三点四八。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将高斯过程学习的价值函数作为模型预测控制终端成本，可以有效纳入预测时域之外的长期影响，并通过不确定性加权、软策略更新及稀疏近似兼顾风险调节、控制稳定性与计算效率。实验结果显示，该方法在所测试的反应器控制问题上显著优于匹配基线；其效果仍依赖风险系数与策略筛选的合理选择。

Abstract: We propose Gaussian Process Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control (GP--RL--MPC), which uses a learned critic as the MPC terminal penalty to account for costs beyond the prediction horizon. Gaussian process regression (GPR) enables data-efficient critic learning from limited, noisy rollouts within optimistic approximate policy iteration (OAPI), with soft updates mitigating policy chattering. A tunable risk coefficient weights GP epistemic uncertainty in the terminal cost, regulating sensitivity to uncertain critic predictions. The postdecision state formulation shifts disturbance averaging offline to account for aleatoric uncertainty from the terminal transition onward. Under matching objectives and admissible actions, a one-step horizon recovers greedy value-space RL. After fitting GP hyperparameters by exact marginal likelihood, we construct a sparse deterministic training conditional (DTC) posterior using inducing points selected by pivoted Cholesky (PC), reducing online evaluation costs as the training dataset grows. In a continuous stirred-tank reactor (CSTR) subject to exogenous disturbances, GP--RL--MPC with an appropriately selected risk coefficient and policy screening reduces the sum of squared errors (SSE) by an average of 80.52% relative to an otherwise matched baseline MPC across five random seeds (sample standard deviation: 3.48%).

</details>


### [218] [DSADiff: Data Structure-Aware Diffusion for Crystal Structure Prediction via Mitigating Complexity- and Imbalance-Induced Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22050)
*Jiang Xiaobo,Dinghong Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层分析策略与数据结构感知扩散模型，以识别并缓解晶体结构预测中的生成偏差，在三个基准数据集上取得先进表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的晶体结构预测方法面对多样化材料空间时容易产生生成偏差；同时，关于不同样本为何导致性能差异的系统分析仍然不足。

Method: 首先，提出分层分析策略，识别晶体复杂度和元素组成不平衡是影响生成难度的关键因素，并分析它们与模型性能的关系。随后，提出数据结构感知扩散模型，将这些因素整合为统一的生成难度表征，用于协同调整特征建模与训练策略，使模型适应结构和难度不同的样本。

Result: 在包括碳二十四在内的三个晶体结构预测基准数据集上，该模型均提升了结构匹配率并降低了均方根误差；结构匹配率最高提升百分之九，整体表现超过已有先进方法。

Conclusion: 研究从生成偏差角度加深了对扩散式晶体结构预测模型的理解，并表明利用数据结构与生成难度信息协同优化模型，有助于提升复杂材料空间中的预测能力，为设计偏差更小的生成模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Crystal Structure Prediction (CSP) is an important step in accelerating the generation and discovery of new materials. In recent years, diffusion-based CSP methods have made significant progress. However, existing models still suffer from generation bias when facing diverse materials spaces, while systematic approaches for tracing the sources of such performance variations remain limited. To address this issue, we propose LAS (Layered Analysis Strategy), a mechanism analysis framework that identifies crystal complexity and elemental composition imbalance as two key factors affecting generation difficulty and further reveals their relationships with model generation performance. Building on these findings, we further propose DSADiff (Data Structure-Aware Diffusion), a data structure-aware diffusion model that transforms the key factors identified by LAS into a unified generation difficulty representation and uses it to jointly drive feature modeling and training strategy optimization. This enables the model to adapt its representation and generation capability to samples with different data structures and difficulty levels, thereby mitigating learning bias across samples. Experiments on three CSP benchmark datasets, including Carbon-24, demonstrate that DSADiff consistently improves both Match Rate and RMSE, with a maximum Match Rate improvement of 9%, surpassing existing state-of-the-art methods and achieving new state-of-the-art performance. This work deepens the understanding of diffusion-based CSP models from the perspective of generation bias and provides a new direction for designing less biased generative models for complex materials spaces.

</details>


### [219] [RiDEx: Reward-Informed Directional Exploration for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22052)
*Song Yan,Chenfeng Wang,Wei Zhai,Xingchen Wang,Tao Zhang,Yawen Shao,Shi Pan,Min Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出奖励知情定向探索方法，将扩散模型的局部探索集中到更可能影响奖励的方向，并利用探索结果提供学习信号；在四项图像对齐与偏好任务上取得最高峰值奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励的扩散模型后训练通常依赖高维潜在空间中的各向同性随机扰动，扰动预算可能浪费在对目标奖励影响很小的变化上，导致探索效率低。论文希望回答扩散模型应沿哪些方向探索，以及如何从探索反馈中学习。

Method: 该方法在去噪速度场中选择与奖励相关的子空间：以条件残差或可微奖励梯度提出探索方向，并在同一生成状态下施加大小相等、方向相反的扰动，通过生成结果的终端奖励差异衡量这些方向的影响。奖励差异为探索坐标提供带符号的局部监督；同时，所有生成图像均用于全局扩散模型学习，从而将探索方向选择与变化效果反馈结合起来。

Result: 在稳定扩散三点五中型模型上，该方法在四项对齐与偏好任务中均取得所评估方法里的最高峰值奖励。采用奖励梯度方向的版本，在人类偏好评分二代和偏好评分任务上，达到目标奖励的速度分别是扩散模型自然反馈优化基线的十五倍和二十九倍，按高端图形处理器运行时长衡量。对照实验还显示，在扰动幅度相同的情况下，奖励对齐方向能引发更强的奖励响应；利用测得的奖励归因信号进行学习，可提升四项任务上的曲线平均奖励。

Conclusion: 研究表明，奖励导向的定向探索能够比各向同性随机扰动更有效地利用探索预算；将方向筛选与实际奖励反馈相结合，可同时改善目标奖励的提升速度和整体学习表现。

Abstract: Reward-based diffusion post-training updates model parameters from task rewards. While existing approaches improve sampling and policy optimization, they still rely largely on isotropic random perturbations in a high-dimensional latent space for exploration. Such variation often spends the perturbation budget on changes with little influence on the target reward, making exploration inefficient. We study where diffusion models should explore and how to learn from that exploration. We introduce Reward-Informed Directional Exploration (RiDEx), which concentrates local exploration in reward-relevant subspaces of the denoising velocity field. Conditional residuals or differentiable reward gradients propose the subspace, and equal-and-opposite perturbations at the same generation state measure its terminal reward response. The reward difference supplies signed local supervision in the explored coordinates, while all generated images contribute to global DiffusionNFT learning. RiDEx thereby couples exploration-space selection with feedback about the changes it produces. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, RiDEx attains the highest peak rewards among the evaluated methods on four alignment and preference tasks. Its gradient-direction instantiation achieves 15x and 29x target-attainment speedups over DiffusionNFT on HPSv2 and PickScore, measured in H200 GPU hours. Controlled experiments confirm that reward-aligned directions elicit stronger responses at equal perturbation magnitude, and that applying their measured credit improves curve-average reward across all four tasks.

</details>


### [220] [Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22055)
*Hyunjong Ok,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在由大语言模型直接编写模型权重、而非通过梯度训练的设定下，是否仍应采用变换器架构。实验发现，模型更倾向于将事实编码为稀疏表格，并通过固定过程查询；这种结构在部分测试中更准确、参数更少，也更适合快速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变换器等常见架构主要因适合梯度下降训练、能高效利用图形处理器而被广泛采用。但若由大语言模型直接手工生成权重，这些优势未必成立，因此需要重新考察何种模型结构最适合这种编程方式。

Method: 研究者提供少量指导、十个带标签的示例和沙盒，让三种前沿大语言模型处理以选择题为主的任务，并比较两种方案：为固定架构生成权重，或自行设计架构。研究还考察预训练权重、其他基准，以及需要模型生成答案的任务。

Result: 在可自行设计架构时，三种模型都将文本事实编译成由固定查询过程访问的稀疏表格，最高测试准确率达到百分之五十四点四。大语言模型设计的模型测试准确率高于同一模型编写的已发布变换器，同时参数量约少一千八百倍。表格中的每个权重含义明确，错误答案可对应到特定行并加以修正；删除一行通常只影响另一项答案。基于此构造的联想记忆模型，在图形处理器上的速度最高可达同规模变换器的十七倍。不过，预训练大语言模型仍然更准确。

Conclusion: 在不依赖梯度训练、而由大语言模型直接编写权重时，稀疏表格式联想记忆可能比变换器更易解释、修改且推理更快。论文的主张是：权重编写方式会影响适宜的模型结构；它并未声称这种结构在准确性上普遍胜过预训练大语言模型。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. In weight-space programming, a large language model (LLM) writes target weights by hand without gradient-based training, so those reasons no longer apply. Should we keep the Transformer? Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same LLM wrote while using about 1,800x fewer parameters. Without gradients, they choose a table, in which the LLM can state what each weight means and can find and rewrite a wrong one. Each wrong answer points to one row, and removing a row changes about one other answer. From this, we propose an associative-memory-based model up to 17x faster on GPUs than a same-sized Transformer. We also test pretrained weights, other benchmarks, and tasks where models generate answers. Pretrained LLMs remain more accurate; the claim concerns which structure to build when writing weights.

</details>


### [221] [Two Counterexamples for e-means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22065)
*Zhenisbek Assylbekov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过两个反例指出，指数平均与簇重启结合的算法可能劣于标准均值聚类，且其期望损失劣势在特定条件下无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在检验一种将指数平均与簇重启结合的均值聚类算法的已发表方案，特别关注重启机制是否会损害聚类质量，以及相对于标准算法的性能保证是否可靠。

Method: 作者构造两个反例：在相同初始中心下，将该算法与标准均值聚类进行比较；并分析基于簇质量的重启对全局最优解及期望损失比值的影响，其中还考察了使用均值聚类++初始化的情形。

Result: 两个反例表明，该算法最终损失可能高于标准均值聚类。基于簇质量的重启可能破坏全局最优解；即使采用均值聚类++初始化，该算法与标准算法的期望损失之比仍可能无界。

Conclusion: 该摘要揭示了所述算法方案的潜在严重缺陷：簇重启并不必然改善聚类结果，基于簇质量的重启甚至可能造成任意大的期望损失劣势。因此，对该方案的性能主张需要谨慎看待，并应进一步分析其重启规则及理论保证。

Abstract: e-means (Bessa et al., 2026) combines exponential averaging and cluster revival. We give two counterexamples to its published recipe with higher final loss than standard k-means (Lloyd, 1982) from identical initial centers. Mass-based revival can destroy a global optimum and make the ratio of expected losses unbounded even with k-means++ seeding.

</details>


### [222] [BernBRS: Certified Backward Reachability for Deep Bernstein Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22083)
*Ibrahim Albool,Yasser Shoukry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出伯恩反向可达分析框架，为采用伯恩斯坦多项式激活函数的深度神经网络同时计算经过认证的反向可达集内、外近似，并通过针对性细分提升精度与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂神经网络系统的安全验证需要严格的数学保证，而反向可达分析虽能识别安全的初始状态，其紧致形式化界的计算却难以扩展到大型网络。已有方法在规模和精度上受到限制，因此需要兼具可靠性、精确度与效率的分析框架。

Method: 针对深度伯恩斯坦网络，框架同时计算反向可达集的认证内近似和外近似。它不采用单一凸界来概括整个输入空间，而是分析网络激活模式，并利用对松弛误差的精确分解，找出预计能最大幅度降低误差的位置进行定向细分，从而逐步改进输入空间覆盖。

Result: 论文在高维模型上展示了该框架的效果，包括半猎豹控制器的线性化闭环近似模型，以及由变换器模块蒸馏得到的伯恩斯坦学生模型。作者还实证考察了模型层面的保证与原始半猎豹系统及轻量级双向编码器分类器之间的关系。摘要未提供具体数值指标。

Conclusion: 通过利用伯恩斯坦多项式网络的数学性质，并将细分资源集中于松弛误差预计最大的区域，该方法旨在实现精确且可扩展的反向可达分析，处理现有认证方法难以应对的大型复杂网络。其保证针对所分析的模型；这些保证与原始系统或模型的关系仍需结合具体实验评估。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of complex neural network systems requires mathematically rigorous verification. While backward reachability analysis is a critical tool for identifying safe initial states, computing tight, formal bounds remains notoriously difficult to scale. Recent advancements have demonstrated that DeepBern-Nets neural networks utilizing Bernstein polynomial activation functions possess advantageous mathematical properties that enable exceptionally fast and tight forward reachability analysis. Building upon this efficiency, this paper introduces BernBRS, a highly accurate and scalable framework for the backward reachability analysis of DeepBern-Nets. BernBRS simultaneously computes certified inner and outer approximations of the Backward Reachable Set (BRS). Rather than relying on single convex bounding, our method intelligently refines a cover of the input space by analyzing activation patterns, splitting the input domain precisely where an exact decomposition of the relaxation gap predicts the most significant reduction in error. This targeted refinement enables high levels of scalability to highly complex and massive architectures that remain intractable for existing certified methods. We demonstrate the efficacy of our framework on high-dimensional models, including a linearized closed-loop approximation of the Half-Cheetah controller and a distilled Bernstein student of a Transformer block and we empirically study how these model-level guarantees relate to the original Half-Cheetah and TinyBERT classifier, respectively.

</details>


### [223] [Every Measurement, Every Direction, All at Once: Multimodal Flow Matching for Molecules and Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22098)
*Thorben Prein,Elton Pan,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli,Santiago Miret*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种多模态分子生成方法，通过随机遮蔽让同一实验中的不同测量既可作为输入，也可作为预测目标，从而提升有限数据的利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验成本高、数据有限，但单次实验往往能获得同一对象的多种关联测量。常见生成模型通常只学习单一方向的映射，未能充分利用其他测量提供的监督信号。

Method: 提出多模态流匹配变换器模型，联合处理分子的三维结构以及紫外、红外和拉曼光谱。利用随机遮蔽构造多种条件输入与预测目标组合，使单个模型能够依据任意一组可用模态，生成任意互不重叠的一组目标模态。

Result: 在分子数据集上的实验中，模型的分子图恢复率达到88.8%，结构生成表现比最强的光谱到结构基线高23.2%；在光谱生成方面也优于专门的结构到光谱模型。消融实验表明，在不同模型规模下，任意模态到任意模态的训练目标均提升了结构生成效果。模型还可处理任意子集的可用测量，并通过将生成光谱与实验参考光谱匹配来辅助分子鉴定。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将多种关联测量统一纳入任意模态生成训练，有助于从有限实验数据中提取更多监督信息。所提出的方法兼具结构与光谱生成能力，并支持灵活使用不完整测量，为科学领域的数据高效生成和分子识别提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative modeling in science is often data-limited, where experiments are expensive. However, each experiment may yield several coupled measurements of the same physical entity. Standard generative models often train only on a single direction, discarding supervision from the rest. We argue that each experiment contains more training signal than this asymmetry allows the model to exploit: random masking enables measurements to serve as either conditioning inputs or prediction targets, yielding multiple supervised denoising tasks from a single experiment. We investigate this premise with MOSAIC, a multimodal flow matching transformer over molecular 3D structure and UV, IR, and Raman spectra. MOSAIC is a single model that is capable of any-to-any generation: generating any subset of modalities from any disjoint subset of the others. On QM9S, MOSAIC achieves 88.8% molecular graph recovery, outperforming the strongest spectra-to-structure baseline by 23.2% in structure generation, while simultaneously outperforming a structure-to-spectra specialist model in spectral generation. Ablations show that the any-to-any objective improves structure generation over structure-only training across model sizes. Furthermore, MOSAIC supports arbitrary subsets of available measurements and enables molecular identification by matching generated spectra to experimental references.

</details>


### [224] [VTT: Reading Vehicle Size and Powertrain Type from Motion Trajectory Across Unseen Car Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22109)
*Yuhang Wang,Yuechen Guo,Jingxin Yang,Chuheng Wei,Suyang Chen,Abdulaziz Alhuraish,Yiyi Wang,Mingyang Ban,Jingran Sun,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出车辆属性识别数据集与基准，可依据车辆运动和控制器局域网信号，在未见车型上识别车身尺寸类别与动力系统类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹数据若补充车辆尺寸和动力系统信息，可更好地支持交通分析与仿真。然而，模型需要面对训练中未出现的车型和驾驶员，跨车型泛化仍具挑战；同时，相关研究缺少大规模数据集及系统化评测基准。

Method: 研究构建了包含九千零六十二段行程、约三千九百五十小时数据的车辆数据集，覆盖一千四百三十一辆车和一百七十四种车型，并标注基于车辆外廓尺寸划分的三类尺寸及动力系统类型。研究以六十秒片段为单位，比较四种序列模型、统计特征和由大语言模型提出的物理特征，并评估未见车型与未见驾驶员场景。训练中还采用辅助物理目标、数据增强和任务专属权重，并分析物理特征与识别结果的关系。

Result: 经过改进训练后，行程级宏平均调和均值在区分纯电动车与燃油车时达到百分之九十二点六至九十三点零，尺寸分类则由百分之六十二点九提升至百分之六十七点一。在来自额外四千零六十一名驾驶员的公开数据上，完整输入模型能在八十八种车型中正确识别八十六种车型的动力系统；尺寸准确率为百分之六十三点二，接近内部车辆级结果的百分之六十三点五。分析显示，动力系统识别与减速行为相关，尺寸识别与转向响应相关；尺寸类别边界附近更易出错，延长观察时间带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合车辆运动与车载信号可以较可靠地跨车型识别动力系统，但尺寸识别仍较困难。类别标签的划分精度以及训练车队对车型的覆盖范围，是提升跨车型泛化能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Vehicle size and powertrain information can enrich trajectory data for traffic analysis and simulation. We present VTT, a dataset and benchmark for identifying these attributes from motion and controller area network (CAN) signals across unseen car models. The dataset contains 9,062 drives, about 3,950 h, from 1,431 vehicles and 174 car models. It provides three footprint-based size classes, powertrain labels, and evaluation on unseen car models and unseen drivers. Using 60-s windows, we compare four sequence architectures, statistical features and physical features proposed by large language models. Training with auxiliary physical targets, data augmentation and task-specific weighting raises drive-level macro-F1 to 92.6-93.0% for battery-electric versus combustion vehicles and from 62.9% to 67.1% for size classification. On public logs from 4,061 additional drivers, the full-input model identifies powertrain correctly for 86 of 88 car models; size accuracy is 63.2%, close to the internal vehicle-level result of 63.5%. Physical-feature analysis links powertrain recognition to deceleration behaviour and size recognition to steering response. Errors near size-class boundaries and limited gains from longer observations highlight the importance of label resolution and training-fleet coverage for cross-model generalisation.

</details>


### [225] [TempQuant: Learning Adaptive Temperatures for Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22128)
*Mukund Athmaram,Liang Ma,Manoj Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需标注答案的采样参数校准方法，通过调整量化模型的温度和核采样阈值，使其下一个词元的概率分布更接近全精度模型，并显著改善部分量化模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常以低精度量化方式部署，但量化会改变下一个词元的概率分布，进而损害生成表现。现有模型卡通常建议量化模型沿用全精度模型的采样设置；论文指出，量化与采样温度、核采样阈值相互作用，照搬原设置可能并非最佳选择。

Method: 作者分析生成过程中的熵与词元排序变化，发现量化平均会使模型分布变得更平坦，且量化模型的表现对温度更敏感。随后提出温度量化校准方法，学习温度和核采样阈值，使量化模型的词元概率尽量接近全精度模型。该方法包含两种粒度：按请求温度学习一组全局参数，或为每个词元单独设定参数；后者依据词元排序变化情况进行调节，排序被量化改变的词元倾向于升温，排序保持的词元倾向于降温。

Result: 在三比特量化的四十亿参数模型上，全局校准将数学任务准确率从推荐采样设置下的百分之三十四提升至百分之五十一。全局参数无需答案标签，其结果接近使用带标签答案调参所得的温度。当量化造成较大性能损失时，该方法能恢复其中相当一部分；损失较小时，也能使量化模型的行为更接近全精度模型。

Conclusion: 采样设置不应被视为量化部署中的固定沿用项。通过针对量化后的概率分布校准温度和核采样阈值，可以在不依赖答案标签的情况下改善准确率并缩小模型行为差异；这为低精度模型部署提供了一种简单且有效的补偿手段。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed at low precision, yet quantization can degrade their performance. We study how quantization interacts with decoding settings such as the sampling temperature and top-p by examining how it distorts the model's next-token probabilities. Measuring entropy during generation across deployed temperatures, we find that quantization warms the model on average, and that temperature matters much more for the performance of a quantized model than for its full-precision counterpart. Because quantization and temperature both shape the next-token distribution, we investigate how much the sampling settings can undo what quantization changes, through our method TempQuant, which learns the quantized model's temperature and top-p to bring its next-token probabilities closer to the full-precision model's. We learn these settings at two granularities. The first is a single global setting per requested temperature, which finds nearly the same temperatures that tuning on labeled answers would, without any labels. Model cards typically recommend sampling a quantized model with the same settings as its full-precision counterpart, while we find that different settings can substantially improve its accuracy. On 3-bit Qwen3-4B, for example, global TempQuant raises MATH accuracy from 34% under the configuration recommended for Qwen's quantized releases to 51%. The second sets the temperature and top-p for every token, motivated by our finding that tokens whose rankings flip under quantization tend to need warming, while tokens whose rankings are preserved tend to need cooling. When quantization degrades accuracy substantially, TempQuant recovers much of it, and when degradation is lighter, it still brings the quantized model's behavior closer to the full-precision model's.

</details>


### [226] [ReMoE: An Interpretable Mixture-of-Experts Framework for Human Mobility Recovery Forecasting Across Disasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22133)
*Yilin Lyu,Kang Liu,Wei Wang,Qifan Zhang,Ling Yin,Yushi Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可解释、可迁移的灾后人类流动性恢复预测框架，在未见过的灾害事件和灾害类型上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害发生后，人类流动性通常会急剧下降，并以复杂方式恢复。准确预测恢复过程有助于及时评估灾情和配置资源。但现有方法往往针对单一事件训练，且缺乏可解释性，难以迁移到未见事件。研究发现不同灾害间存在反复出现的恢复类型，因而有望将已学到的恢复规律迁移应用。

Method: 提出一种可解释的混合专家框架：由特征引导的路由器根据区域特征，自适应组合专门学习不同恢复类型的专家，生成区域特定的恢复模式；再结合未来灾害状况的预测，对该模式进行残差修正，以纳入后续危险条件的影响。

Result: 在涵盖飓风、洪水、冬季风暴和野火等十八起灾害事件的大规模流动性数据上，该方法在训练分布内具有竞争力，并在未见事件及未见灾害类型上取得最佳表现；分布偏移越大，其优势越明显。进一步分析显示，该框架具有可解释的学习结果，并能在不确定性下保持稳健表现。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，跨灾害复用流动性恢复类型有助于提升对新事件的预测能力。所提出的框架兼具迁移性、可追溯性与稳健性，可为灾后前瞻性评估提供支持。

Abstract: Human mobility often declines sharply following disasters and subsequently exhibits complex recovery dynamics. Effective forecasting of this recovery is critical for timely assessment and resource allocation. However, existing approaches are often developed for individual events and operate as black boxes, limiting their transferability and interpretability beyond the events they were trained on. Recent studies have identified recurring post-disaster mobility recovery types across disasters, suggesting that recovery knowledge may be transferable. Motivated by this, we propose ReMoE, an interpretable mixture-of-experts framework for cross-disaster mobility recovery forecasting. ReMoE uses a feature-guided router to adaptively combine specialized experts that learn these heterogeneous yet reusable recovery types, producing a region-specific recovery pattern. It further refines this pattern through residual correction informed by forecasts of future hazard conditions. We evaluate ReMoE on a large-scale mobility dataset covering 18 disaster events spanning hurricanes, floods, winter storms, and wildfires. ReMoE is competitive in-distribution and achieves the strongest performance on unseen events and hazard types, with larger gains under greater distribution shifts. Further analyses show that ReMoE yields interpretable learning outcomes and maintains robust performance under uncertainty. Broadly, ReMoE offers a transferable and traceable forecaster that can support anticipatory post-disaster assessment.

</details>


### [227] [Synthetic Crystal Data Generation for Diffusion Pretraining in Material Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22140)
*Aamod Varma,Shuyi Jia,Victor Fung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过多种合成结构生成策略扩展扩散式预训练数据，并以机器学习原子间势加速结构弛豫；实验显示，合成数据预训练在多个材料性质预测任务上优于随机初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式图神经网络预训练通常依赖接近势能面局部极小值的晶体结构，而这类结构传统上需通过昂贵的密度泛函理论弛豫获得，限制了预训练数据的规模与多样性，也使大量晶体结构空间未被探索。论文旨在降低这一数据瓶颈。

Method: 提出合成数据生成流程，结合扰动后弛豫、生成模型采样和原型结构枚举三种策略；需要结构弛豫时，采用快速机器学习原子间势代替逐个进行密度泛函理论计算。研究还使用随机初始化的图神经网络骨干，比较坐标去噪、连续时间方差保持扩散目标和次方差保持扩散目标，并评估原型结构数据集规模扩大的效果。

Result: 在七项来源对比任务中，所有合成数据预训练设置的数值表现均优于随机初始化。原型结构数据集从两万条扩至十六万五千条，并采用固定的四百轮训练后，在多数任务上数值上达到或超过基于两万条材料数据的预训练表现。研究还纳入一项额外任务评估数据规模扩展。

Conclusion: 合成结构可作为扩散式材料预训练的有效数据来源，机器学习原子间势有望降低数据生成中的弛豫成本。不过，原型结构扩展实验同时增加了数据多样性和优化更新次数，因此其提升应理解为数据与计算共同扩展的结果，不能单独归因于数据规模。

Abstract: Diffusion-based pretraining has emerged as a powerful strategy for learning transferable representations in graph neural networks for materials property prediction without requiring quantum-mechanical labels such as energies, forces, and stresses for every pretraining structure. However, the method retains a critical dependency: it requires structures residing near local minima of a potential energy surface, which have traditionally been obtained through costly DFT relaxations of known or physically synthesized compounds. This reliance constrains both the scale and the structural diversity of available pretraining data, leaving large regions of crystal structure space unexplored. In this work, we connect diffusion-based pretraining with synthetic structure generation to reduce this bottleneck. We introduce a synthetic data generation pipeline combining three complementary strategies: perturb-and-relax, generative sampling via MatterGen, and protostructure enumeration. Where structural relaxation is required, we use fast machine-learned interatomic potentials instead of performing a new DFT relaxation for each generated structure. We further compare a coordinate-denoising control with continuous-time variance-preserving (VP) and sub-VP diffusion objectives using a randomly initialized Orb-v2 backbone. The source comparison covers seven MatBench property-prediction tasks, and an eighth task, \texttt{mp\_gap}, is included in the protostructure scaling study. Every synthetic-pretraining condition numerically outperforms random initialization on all seven source-comparison tasks. Scaling the protostructure corpus from 20k to 165k under a fixed 400-epoch schedule numerically matches or exceeds MP-20k pretraining on most tasks; because this protocol increases both data diversity and optimization updates, the result reflects joint data-and-compute scaling.

</details>


### [228] [When does deep reinforcement learning pay off in HVAC supervisory control? Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22144)
*Bingnan Zhao,Harshala Gammulle,Tharindu Fernando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过四个可复现的办公楼案例检验深度强化学习控制暖通空调系统的实际收益，并以调优规则和近似最优基准进行比较；结果显示，收益因场景而异，储能场景潜力较大，但仍明显落后于理想预知调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有深度强化学习暖通空调研究常使用较弱的对照方法，且很少给出可达到的最优水平，因此报告的节能收益难以解释。本文旨在通过可复现案例和强基准，明确深度强化学习相对于优质规则及近似最优控制的真实改进幅度。

Method: 研究以澳大利亚布里斯班的一栋办公楼为基础，构建四个案例：分时电价下的预冷、冷水机组分级与冷却水温重置、湿式冷却盘管下的冷冻水供水温度重置，以及考虑分时电价和需量费用的冷冻水蓄能。系统采用建筑能耗模型库建模，三个案例中使用软演员—评论家算法直接在导出的功能模型单元上训练智能体。各智能体与调优后的规则控制器比较，并在可行时与通过逐小时穷举搜索或动态规划、假设能够完美预知未来而得到的近似最优基准比较。

Result: 无蓄能的两个温度重置案例中，智能体达到近似最优基准，但相对优质规则的收益很小：冷却水温重置为百分之一点三，冷冻水温重置仅为百分之零点一八；后者的两种常见重置规则甚至比固定设定值更耗能。蓄能案例中，智能体优于两种规则，节能幅度分别为百分之七点八、百分之三点九和百分之六点三，并会在太阳能电力低价的午间充能；但它只实现了完美预知调度所能取得节能量的约百分之四十五，对需量峰值的抑制也有限。预冷案例中，人工调优规则优于智能体，节能幅度分别为百分之十二点零和百分之五点六。论文还报告了在协同仿真功能模型单元上训练时遇到的实际困难。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习并不必然优于经过认真调优的规则控制器，其价值取决于具体控制任务；在无蓄能的重置问题中，额外收益可能很有限，而蓄能控制虽显示出更大潜力，仍有提升空间。评估此类方法时，应同时报告最佳规则控制器与近似最优基准之间的差距，并说明训练和仿真实现中的实际限制。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) is often proposed for the supervisory control of heating, ventilation and air-conditioning (HVAC) systems. The reported gains are, however, difficult to interpret, because the baselines are often weak and the attainable optimum is seldom known. This paper examines four small, reproducible case studies based on an office building in Brisbane, Australia: pre-cooling under a time-of-use tariff; chiller staging with condenser water reset; chilled water supply temperature reset with a wet cooling coil; and chilled water storage under time-of-use prices and a demand charge. The plants were modelled with the Modelica Buildings library, and in three of the cases a soft actor-critic agent was trained directly on the exported functional mock-up unit (FMU). Each agent was compared with tuned rule-based controllers and, where practicable, with a near-optimal reference from hourly exhaustive search or dynamic programming with perfect foresight.
  In the two cases without storage the agent reached the near-optimal reference, but the margin above a well-chosen rule was small: 1.3% for condenser water reset and 0.18% for chilled water reset. In the latter case two common reset rules consumed more energy than a fixed set point. With a chilled water storage tank the margin was considerably larger. The agent outperformed both rules (-7.8%, compared with -3.9% and -6.3%) and adopted midday charging during the solar off-peak period, yet it obtained only about 45% of the saving achieved by the perfect-foresight schedule and did little to limit the demand peak. In the pre-cooling case a hand-tuned rule remained superior (-12.0% compared with -5.6%). These results suggest that studies of DRL for HVAC control should state the margin between the best rule and a near-optimal reference. Some practical difficulties encountered in training on co-simulation FMUs are also reported.

</details>


### [229] [What RL on Expert-Built Tasks Teaches Nemotron 3.5 Super](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22161)
*Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究者针对五类专业任务分别对英伟达一款大模型进行强化学习后训练；各任务适配器在相应基准上普遍优于基础模型，且错误分析显示其行为更贴合任务需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用基础模型在软件工程、复杂约束处理、长程工具使用、图表理解和企业文档推理等专业任务中仍有不足。研究旨在检验：仅用单一任务数据进行强化学习后训练，能否有效提升模型在对应任务上的表现，并通过逐例比较了解提升来自哪些行为变化。

Method: 以总参数量1200亿、激活参数量120亿的模型为基础，使用五套专家构建的任务数据集，分别进行五次独立训练。每次训练均从基础模型开始，采用秩为32的低秩适配器和一种强化学习优化方法，在同一训练平台上完成；不设置监督微调阶段。各适配器与基础模型在相同任务、评测流程和设置下比较。评测涵盖12项主要基准，其中8项由其他团队构建，4项由研究团队构建。

Result: 12项主要基准均报告了提升，幅度从指令遵循基准的4.5个百分点到文档问答基准的13.8个百分点不等。具体而言，软件工程基准从86.9%升至92.1%，终端任务基准从63.9%升至70.3%，多轮挑战基准从58.3%升至66.6%，自动化基准从14.8%升至27.5%，图表理解基准从39.4%升至50.6%；文档问答基准从10.4%升至24.2%。未使用应用程序接口工具训练的文档适配器，在自动化基准上也提升了8.0个百分点。逐例分析发现，工具使用适配器会先查找数据与规则，编程适配器会创建测试或临时文件，约束适配器更能保留先前轮次的信息，图表适配器会用代码测量图表标记，文档适配器则会读取电子表格。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，针对单一专业任务进行强化学习后训练，能够在多个相关基准上带来显著性能提升，并促使模型采取更有效的任务策略。尤其值得注意的是，部分能力提升可能迁移到训练时未直接使用相应工具的评测任务。不过，摘要未提供训练成本、统计显著性或更广泛迁移能力的细节，因此这些结果仍应结合完整实验设置审慎解读。

Abstract: We post-train NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 Super (120B total, 12B active parameters) with reinforcement learning (RL) on five expert-built task datasets, one at a time: real-world software engineering, complex constraints, long-horizon tool use, professional chart understanding and enterprise document reasoning. Each run trains a fresh rank-32 LoRA adapter with CISPO on Tinker, with no supervised stage, and each adapter is compared with the base model on the same tasks, harness and settings. On twelve headline benchmarks, eight built by other groups and four by Surge AI, the gains range from +4.5 points on IFBench to +13.8 on GDP_pdf, which rises from 10.4% to 24.2%; SWE-bench Pro V2 rises from 86.9% to 92.1%, Terminal-Bench 2.1 from 63.9% to 70.3%, MultiChallenge from 58.3% to 66.6%, AutomationBench from 14.8% to 27.5% and Chartography from 39.4% to 50.6%. The document adapter, trained without API tools, also gains +8.0 points on AutomationBench. In attempts read side by side with the base model's on tasks each adapter newly solves, the base model's failures recur in a few modes, and each adapter does more often what the base model missed: the tool-use adapter finds the data and the rules before acting, the coding adapter writes its own test or scratch files on SWE-bench Pro V2, the constraints adapter carries earlier turns into its answers, the chart adapter measures the marks in code, and the document adapter reads AutomationBench's spreadsheets.

</details>


### [230] [Towards Optimism-Pessimism Trade-off in Model-based Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22172)
*Guochen Zhou,Yijun Yang,Qiqi Duan,Qing Su,Weiming Ou,Li Shen,Shihao Ji,Yuhui Shi,Zhisong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出分层帕累托策略池方法：离线阶段构建覆盖乐观与悲观取舍的多样策略池，在线阶段根据任务情境自适应选择策略进行微调，从而提升离线到在线强化学习在不同任务及分布偏移下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线预训练与在线微调之间的分布偏移常会损害微调效果。论文发现，乐观策略更适合适应新任务，悲观策略则更适合已见任务，因此离线阶段如何平衡两者至关重要。现有单目标方法通常用一个系数表达这种取舍，而通过在线微调来选择该系数可能需要代价高昂的双层优化。

Method: 论文将乐观与悲观取舍建模为双目标问题，在离线训练阶段使用多目标软演员—评论家算法求解，并构建多样化的帕累托策略池。在线阶段则采用分层情境多臂老虎机算法，在每次交互时选择适合当前任务的策略进行微调。

Result: 实验显示，该分层帕累托策略池流程在已见和新颖在线任务上的综合表现最佳，并且在更强的分布偏移下仍保持优势。消融实验进一步分析了策略池、基于多目标算法的策略池构建方式，以及在线选择机制各自的作用。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将策略间的乐观—悲观取舍保留为离线阶段的帕累托策略池，并在在线阶段按情境自适应选择，有助于避免固定单一折衷带来的局限，提升跨任务适应能力与分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Model-based offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) enables sample-efficient adaptation by leveraging offline pre-training for online fine-tuning. However, the distribution shifts between offline and online stages often hinder fine-tuning performance. Many existing methods encode the optimism-pessimism trade-off through a single-objective formulation. Selecting its trade-off coefficient through online fine-tuning can require costly bi-level optimization. We identify the balance established during offline training as a key challenge: in our motivating experiments, optimistic policies adapt better to novel tasks, whereas pessimistic policies perform better on the same online tasks. To address this challenge, we propose a bi-objective formulation that captures this trade-off, yielding a Pareto policy pool during offline training. This policy pool enables adaptive selection during online fine-tuning across diverse online tasks. To generate the pool, we introduce Multi-Objective Soft Actor-critIC (MOSAIC), which solves bi-objective problems and constructs diverse Pareto policies. After offline training, a contextual bandit algorithm hierarchically selects the most suitable policy for fine-tuning at each online interaction step. Empirically, our pipeline, Hierarchical Pareto Policy Pool (HiP3), achieves the strongest aggregate performance across both same and novel online tasks and maintains its advantage under stronger distribution shifts. Comprehensive ablations further clarify the roles of the policy pool, MOSAIC-based pool construction, and the online selection mechanism.

</details>


### [231] [An Optimization Framework for Denoising Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22202)
*Xinyu Zhou,Jiawei Zhang,Stephen J. Wright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明，单一固定分数场上的去噪生成优化，在一定条件下能够收敛到数据流形附近，同时保留有限分辨率下的区域覆盖与生成多样性；实验也支持其稳健性及泛化价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无噪声条件的去噪模型学习单一分数，因此生成可解释为在固定对数密度景观上的梯度上升。但这一解释引出两个问题：轨迹能否到达有意义的数据区域，以及不同初始点能否保留多样化结果。

Method: 研究经验分布与总体分布上的多尺度高斯混合目标。在明确的后验分布与景观条件下，分析梯度上升轨迹的收敛；进一步为同一固定向量场建立源汇恒等式，用以证明有限分辨率下的区域覆盖。论文还构造仿射情形，考察带宽对训练样本记忆与生成多样性的影响，并将惩罚项用于引导可控生成。

Result: 理论上，轨迹几乎必然收敛至距一个维数为“ｍ”的参考流形至多为“ｍσ最小平方除以τ量级”的区域，且该流形的可达半径至少为τ。仿射构造显示，存在正带宽范围，使经验分数优化在训练分辨率下避免记忆训练样本，同时维持多样性。实验支持上述机制，并显示大步长引导下的稳健性改善及与有限数据生成泛化相符的下游收益。

Conclusion: 固定目标上的梯度优化并非必然导致样本记忆或多样性坍缩。在适当条件下，它能够兼顾流形邻近性、区域覆盖与多样化生成，并支持稳健的可控生成；这些结果为去噪生成模型的优化解释及其有限数据泛化提供了理论与实验依据。

Abstract: Noise-unconditioned denoising learns a single score, so generation can be viewed as gradient ascent on a fixed log-density landscape. This optimization interpretation leaves two questions: do trajectories approach meaningful data, and do different initializations retain diverse outcomes? We establish both phenomena for empirical and population multi-scale Gaussian-mixture objectives. Under explicit posterior and landscape conditions, ascent converges almost surely within $O(mσ_{\min}^2/τ)$ of an $m$-dimensional reference manifold with reach at least $τ$. We derive a source--sink identity for the same fixed field and use it to prove finite-resolution regional coverage. In an affine construction, we further identify a positive-bandwidth regime where even empirical-score optimization can avoid training-sample memorization while preserving diversity at the training resolution. The same fixed objective naturally supports penalty-guided controllable generation. Experiments support our predicted mechanisms, show improved robustness under large-step guidance, and demonstrate downstream gains consistent with finite-data generative generalization.

</details>


### [232] [Sample-efficient Personalization of Large Language Model Alignment with Multidimensional User Preference Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22210)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Jerry Huang,Gang Niu,Takashi Ishida,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出多维偏好引导的在线直接偏好优化方法，利用用户历史比较构建个性化偏好锥，减少低价值标注并提升有限查询预算下的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线偏好对齐需要反复获取人工比较反馈，但标注成本高、耗时长，因而样本效率至关重要。现有方法通常依据语言模型自身的不确定性选择比较样本，却可能忽略用户历史反馈已经暗示的偏好顺序，导致查询资源浪费在用户看来已显而易见的样本上。

Method: 该方法借助自动验证器，对用户历史比较进行低成本的多维度评估，并据此构建个性化偏好锥来指导响应选择。它先剔除按照估计用户偏好已被明确支配的响应，再从保留范围内选择彼此仍具竞争性、且尚未解决的偏好分歧最大的两个响应进行比较。

Result: 理论分析表明，随着学习推进，查询中用于偏好锥支配响应的比例以高概率趋于零。多个大型语言模型系列上的实验显示，在查询预算相同的条件下，该方法优于有竞争力的基线方法。

Conclusion: 论文表明，利用用户历史偏好而非仅依赖模型自身不确定性来筛选比较样本，有望提高在线偏好对齐的标注效率。该方法通过减少已确定偏好样本的重复查询，将有限反馈集中于真正尚未解决的偏好分歧。

Abstract: Preference alignment has become a central tool to align large language models (LLMs) with human values and desired behavior, and its online variant can further advance their capabilities beyond what static data alone offer by repeatedly generating and labeling new responses with human feedback. But costly, time-consuming annotations limit its scalability, making sample efficiency essential, and existing methods typically seek to improve it by expanding coverage of the response space or selecting pairs with the highest uncertainty for comparison. Although they are effective, these methods quantify uncertainty from the perspective of the LLM, which may differ from the uncertainty perceived by the user: a pair can appear highly uncertain to the LLM even when past comparisons from the user already imply a clear ordering. In this paper, we propose multidimensional preference-guided online direct preference optimization (MPG-DPO), which leverages low-cost multidimensional evaluations from automated verifiers on the user historical comparisons to construct a personalized preference cone guiding response selection for online preference alignment. Specifically, it first discards responses that are confidently dominated under the estimated user preference, then selects two responses that are competitive under the retained preference cone yet exhibit the strongest unresolved disagreement. Theoretical analysis shows that, with high probability, the fraction of queries spent on cone-dominated responses vanishes as learning progresses. Experiments across multiple LLM families show that MPG-DPO outperforms strong contenders under matched query budgets.

</details>


### [233] [Communicate the Change: Efficient Expert Parallelism via Activation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22215)
*Fengyuan Liu,Jay Gala,Siva Reddy,Dzmitry Bahdanau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可用于既有稀疏混合专家模型的轻量改造方法，通过压缩并传输相邻层隐藏状态之间的差值，减少专家并行推理中的通信量；通信量降低百分之五十八，吞吐量提升百分之二十二点六，同时基本保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家模型可在不过度增加计算量的情况下扩展参数规模，但大规模推理依赖跨设备分配专家。每个混合专家层都需要两次同步通信，分别用于分发隐藏状态和汇总专家输出。随着计算能力增长快于互连带宽，这类通信已成为扩展效率的主要瓶颈。

Method: 该方法利用变换器模块逐层增量更新残差流这一特点，将较早层的隐藏状态作为参照，不再完整传输当前隐藏状态，而是传输两者之间经过压缩的差值。系统会定期刷新参照状态，并在通信关键路径上同步传输压缩差值，以降低通信负载且不显著增加计算开销。该方法可改造已有预训练模型，所需训练词元数量约为完整预训练的八千五百分之一，并支持对传输表示进行量化。

Result: 实验显示，该方法将节点间通信量减少百分之五十八，推理吞吐量提高百分之二十二点六；在十八项基准测试中的平均性能差距控制在三点八个百分点以内。在带宽受限的以太网上，吞吐量最高可提升至原来的二点三八倍。对通信表示进行量化也未造成可测量的质量损失。

Conclusion: 通过传输压缩后的隐藏状态差值，而非完整状态，该方法能够以较小的训练和计算开销缓解混合专家推理的通信瓶颈，并可应用于既有模型。结果表明，它有助于提升带宽受限环境下的推理效率，并为跨广域网络部署分布式推理提供可能。

Abstract: Scaling has driven the success of large language models (LLM), with sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures enabling greater parameter capacity without a proportional increase in computation. At large scales, MoE models rely on expert parallelism to distribute experts across devices. Each MoE layer requires two synchronous collective communication steps during inference: dispatching hidden states to the activated experts and combining the resulting expert outputs. As compute throughput has outpaced interconnect bandwidth, these synchronous collectives have become a major bottleneck to MoE scaling and efficiency. We address this challenge with EchoMoE, a lightweight architecture modification that retrofits existing MoE models, requiring approximately 8,500x fewer training tokens than full pretraining. Motivated by the observation that transformer blocks update the residual stream incrementally, we use a hidden state from an earlier layer as a reference and communicate only the compressed difference between it and the current state, rather than communicating each hidden state in full. EchoMoE periodically refreshes these reference states and transmits only the compressed differences synchronously, reducing the traffic on latency-critical paths without significant computational overhead. EchoMoE reduces inter-node communication volume by 58% and improves throughput by 22.6%, while preserving average performance within 3.8 percentage points across 18 benchmarks. EchoMoE further improves inference throughput by up to 2.38x over bandwidth-constrained Ethernet, paving the way for wide-area distributed inference. EchoMoE also tolerates quantization of the communicated representations without measurable quality loss. We release an efficient implementation for applying EchoMoE to pretrained MoE models, together with an inference integration for vLLM.

</details>


### [234] [Learning Optimal Transport Plans Via Autoregressive Token Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22218)
*Ivan Jaen Marquez,Sotirios Panagiotis Chytas,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于自回归变换器的离散最优传输方案生成模型，利用稀疏性预测非零运输量，并探索模型能否通过预训练学习此类约束优化问题的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输求解器效果可靠，但通常需要针对每个新问题单独求解。离散最优传输具有可获得的精确解、明确的边际约束以及已知的解稀疏性，因此适合研究学习式摊销模型能否掌握其结构，并在未见过的问题上快速生成近似方案。

Method: 提出基于变换器的自回归模型，将离散最优传输重构为结构化序列生成任务。模型把稀疏运输方案表示为序列，并通过自回归标记回归依次预测非零元素，避免输出稠密耦合矩阵。模型在大规模合成问题语料上预训练，以期直接迁移到未见的分布对和不同成本度量。

Result: 在低维离散问题上，该模型能够生成准确的近似运输方案，且与逐实例训练的神经最优传输基线相比具有竞争力。预训练模型还展现了对未见分布对及异质成本度量的零样本泛化能力。不过，摘要未提供具体数值，也未声称其优于经典求解器。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，自回归变换器能够利用离散最优传输解的稀疏结构，以序列形式摊销生成近似方案。它为学习约束优化问题的结构提供了有前景的探索，但目前尚不能取代经典最优传输求解器。

Abstract: Optimal Transport (OT) provides a principled framework for finding the minimum cost coupling between probability distributions. Classical solvers remain the gold standard for this task. At the same time, OT is an ideal testbed for studying whether learning-based amortized models can internalize the structure of OT type problems. Here, ground-truth solutions are available and marginal constraints are explicitly defined. Also, exact discrete solutions have well-characterized sparsity. We describe ToR-OT, a transformer-based model that learns to predict discrete OT plans through autoregressive token regression. We reframe OT into a structured sequence generation task. ToR-OT decodes sparse transport plans as sequences instead of solving an optimization task at test time. We focus on three properties: (a) Zero-shot generalization: pretraining on a large synthetic corpus of OT instances allows a single model to generalize to unseen distribution pairs and heterogeneous cost metrics without retraining, (b) Sparse primal decoding: by exploiting the inherent sparsity in exact discrete OT solutions, the decoder predicts only nonzero entries (avoids dense coupling outputs), (c) Amortized inference: on low-dimensional discrete problems, ToR-OT gives accurate approximate plans and compares favorably with per-instance neural OT baselines. Overall, ToR-OT is not yet a replacement for classical solvers, but a step toward understanding how autoregressive transformers can learn structure of constrained optimization problems like OT.

</details>


### [235] [SOTA or Luck? The Winner's Curse in LLM Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22227)
*Sikun Xu,Raphael Thomadsen,Dennis J. Zhang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，排行榜会因基准测试分数的抽样噪声而高估高排名模型的表现，并提出自助法校正分数偏差、估计排名不确定性及划分模型层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的大语言模型排行榜影响模型评估与采用，但按含噪声的基准分数挑选领先者，会让抽样运气与真实能力混杂，导致冠军分数被高估。此类由排名选择引起的偏差也会影响排行榜其他位置，而原始排名未能展示排序的可靠程度。

Method: 作者提出一种从样本中抽取较小子样本的自助法，用于校正排名诱发的分数偏差，并估计各模型可能的排名区间、将模型归入能力层级。理论分析假设模型池固定、任务独立同分布且有界，同时允许同一任务上不同模型的表现存在任意相关性；在真实能力层级之间存在正间隔时，证明该方法可将排名偏差从随任务数按平方根倒数级衰减，改进为更小阶的预期残余偏差。作者还提出将分数校正与排名区间结合成一份排行榜评估报告，并通过样本拆分和校准模拟检验有限样本表现。

Result: 在涵盖五类基准的十四项测试中，第一名模型的分数校正幅度约为零点零九至三点八六个百分点。在软件工程基准测试的一个快照中，并列领先模型的分数下调零点九个百分点，说明表面领先可能具有统计脆弱性。该基准的模型共一百七十三个，其中十三个被归入领先层级。理论上，作者还给出了能够一致恢复真实能力层级的充分条件。

Conclusion: 排行榜不应只公布原始分数和名次，还应同时报告经过选择偏差校正的分数、可能排名区间及模型层级，使读者能够区分模型表现与抽样运气，并判断排序精度。论文也强调，基准测试设计应考虑排名选择效应与不确定性。

Abstract: Public LLM leaderboards guide model evaluation and adoption, yet the reported performance of top-ranked models can be systematically overstated. This leaderboard winner's curse arises because ranking noisy benchmark scores rewards sampling luck alongside genuine ability. The same selection effect can distort scores throughout the leaderboard, a phenomenon we call the rank-induced bias. We develop an $m$-out-of-$N$ bootstrap to correct for this bias. It assumes a fixed model pool with independent, identically distributed bounded tasks while allowing arbitrary within-task correlation among models. Under exact population tiers separated by positive gaps, we prove that the bootstrap reduces $Θ(N^{-1/2})$ ranking bias to expected residual bias $o(N^{-1/2})$. Across 14 benchmarks from 5 benchmark families, the rank-1 adjustments range from approximately 0.09 to 3.86 percentage points. In our SWE-bench Verified snapshot, the tied leaders receive an adjustment of 0.90 percentage points. Such overstatement is large enough to make the apparent winner statistically fragile. Thus, we use the same bootstrap to further quantify the intervals of plausible rank positions for each model and group models into tiers. We establish sufficient conditions for consistent recovery of population tiers. On SWE-bench Verified, this procedure places 13 of 173 models in the leading tier. We combine the score adjustment and rank intervals in the Rank Uncertainty and Leaderboard Evaluation Report (RULER). We validate its finite-sample performance using sample splitting and calibrated simulations, and advocate publishing RULER alongside raw rankings so readers can assess both reported performance and the precision of the ordering.
