RULER also provides guidance for benchmark design that accounts for selection effects and ranking uncertainty.

</details>


### [236] [What Does Weight-Space Programming Add to Diffusion Editing? Program Construction versus Score-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22228)
*Zitian Gao,Jingyi Xu,Ziqi Han,Chengkai Lei,Ye Liu,Liangliang Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究扩散模型的权重空间编程：一次性生成的程序在整体图像匹配与主体出现的联合指标上有所提升，但后续按分数改写会削弱这一优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间编程允许语言模型为另一网络构造可执行的权重更新。论文关注如何用这种方式编辑扩散模型概念，并检验程序生成与后续改写是否能可靠地改善编辑效果，同时保留原有能力。

Method: 研究者设计了一个编辑接口：程序指定训练情境、保留约束和更新方向，再由共享数值拟合器确定各部分系数。在稳定扩散一点四模型的二十四项测试编辑上，将生成程序与已发表的编辑方法、人工编写的共享面板基线，以及预算匹配的传统编辑搜索进行比较。另对阿斯特拉程序开展探测和按分数改写，并在固定的一百四十四张图像上使用五种额外评估器进行探索性检验。

Result: 预先注册的改写流程没有优于较强的人工基线。探索性比较中，一次性生成的阿斯特拉程序使新场景上的视觉语言模型联合成功率较基线提高十个百分点；该指标要求主体出现且整幅图像符合目标，而基于图文相对匹配的指标未见可检测变化。探测并改写程序后，图文相对匹配提高六点二个百分点，但联合成功率下降九点七个百分点。五种额外评估器在整图目标匹配上的点估计方向一致；主体局部变化的差异则不够明确。

Conclusion: 完整生成的程序可能包含有用的编辑行为，但按分数指导的改写未必能保留这些行为，甚至可能造成不同评估指标之间的取舍。因此，权重空间编程流程中的程序构造与程序改写应作为两个独立阶段分别评估。

Abstract: Weight-space programming (WSP) lets a language model construct executable weight updates for another network. We study diffusion concept editing through an interface in which programs define training contexts, preservation constraints and update directions, while a shared numerical fitter selects their coefficients. We compare generated programs with published editors, a shared-panel hand-written baseline and budget-matched search over conventional edits on 24 TIMED test edits for Stable Diffusion 1.4. The preregistered rewrite pipeline shows no advantage over the strong baseline. In an exploratory comparison, one-shot Astra programs improve fresh-scene VLM joint success, which requires subject presence together with whole-image target matching, by +10.0 points over the shared-panel baseline, while CLIP relative match shows no detectable change. These programs organise adjustable directions around protected text-state subspaces. Probing and rewriting the Astra programs then raises CLIP by +6.2 points while joint success changes by -9.7. On a fixed 144-image sample, five additional evaluators give point estimates in the same directions under whole-image target matching; checks of subject-local change give less decisive differences. Together, these results identify useful behaviour in complete generated programs and show that score-guided revision need not preserve it: program construction and revision should be evaluated as distinct stages of a WSP pipeline.

</details>


### [237] [Spatially local mini-batching for implicit neural representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22236)
*Minseok Choi,Swarup Dhar,Heejae Kwon,Kookjin Lee,Donsub Rim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可用空间局部小批次训练的低秩神经表示，在六类双曲守恒律问题上以较少参数取得较高精度，并优于同等规模的多种基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经表示通常需要从整个空间域抽取训练点；若一个小批次只包含局部区域，模型精度往往下降。论文希望利用自适应网格数值解中天然存在的局部结构，设计能够稳定进行局部训练且参数高效的神经代理模型。

Method: 采用低秩神经表示：各层权重由共享基矩阵与低维系数组合而成，系数由小型超网络生成。每次训练仅使用自适应网格中的一个小单元内、来自一个时间快照的点，并依照网格细化情况抽取快照和单元。作者从结构上分析，局部训练步只能通过少量系数作用于共享基；同时借助柯尔莫哥洛夫宽度论证，说明单元内隐藏状态接近低维子空间，因此局部更新不易使网络被单个单元主导。

Result: 在六个基于自适应网格求解的双曲守恒律问题上，一种局部训练方案达到百分之一点一至百分之二点三的相对二范数误差，参数量约为同宽稠密网络的十分之一。相同规模和训练预算下，超网络正弦网络、深度算子网络及傅里叶神经算子的误差为百分之十六至百分之七十八，而低秩神经表示为百分之四点九。此外，局部训练也会显著影响宽型通用超网络正弦网络的单元内低维结构。

Conclusion: 低秩结构使神经表示能够有效利用空间局部小批次训练，兼顾精度与参数效率。理论分析为其局部更新机制提供了依据，实验则显示其在所测试问题上明显优于多种同规模基线；同时，局部训练对一般网络表示结构的影响也值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Neural surrogates based on implicit neural representations (INRs) are typically trained on mini-batches spread over the whole domain, since a generic INR loses accuracy when every point of a batch lies in one small region. We show that low-rank neural representations (LRNRs), INRs whose weight matrices are products of shared bases with coefficients emitted by a small hypernetwork, can be trained with spatially local mini-batches. Each step uses the points of one time snapshot of an adaptive-mesh numerical solution inside one small cell, with snapshots and cells drawn according to the refinement of the mesh. We posit that such training is enabled by the structural property of LRNRs, as a local step reaches the shared bases only through the $r$ coefficients of each layer, and keeps one cell from rewriting the network. By a Kolmogorov-width argument, we show that when restricted to a small cell, the hidden states of LRNRs lie close to a low-dimensional subspace, which suggests the local steps only affect low-rank subspaces of the shared bases. On six hyperbolic-conservation-law problems with solutions computed using adaptive mesh refinement, one local training recipe reaches 1.1-2.3% relative $L^2$ error with a tenth of the parameters of a dense network of the same width. Finally, we provide a comparison by training a hypernetwork-SIREN, a DeepONet, and a Fourier neural operator (FNO) of the same size with the same local batches and training budget. We observe that these baselines reach 16-78% where the LRNR reaches 4.9%. Interestingly, we also observe that local training has a significant effect on the per-cell low-dimensional structure even in a wide generic hypernetwork-SIREN.

</details>


### [238] [PCGRL for Complex Platformers: Synthesizing Playable and Diverse Levels via Inflated Envelopes and Forward Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22250)
*Di Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合物理约束、课程学习与随机推理的关卡生成框架，旨在提升长序列、大规模平台跳跃关卡生成的可解性、稳定性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的程序化内容生成方法在长序列生成任务中容易遭遇探索瓶颈和扩展性不足。面对刚体规则与非线性运动约束时，传统方法尤其难以在大尺寸地图上稳定生成可通关关卡；收敛后的确定性策略还可能产生单调、缺乏多样性的路径。

Method: 框架包含三项设计：一是构建“膨胀体积包络”机制，将连续运动可行性纳入离散马尔可夫决策过程状态；二是引入中间状态转移反馈，并采用多阶段前向扩展课程学习，以缓解超长决策序列中的奖励稀疏问题，改善价值估计与长期信用分配；三是在关卡合成阶段启用高熵随机推理，并通过事后并行拒绝筛选机制约束生成结果。

Result: 摘要报告称，该方法在多尺度环境中提升了结构多样性，同时保持较高的物理可玩性；与基线方法相比，其收敛稳定性和跨尺寸扩展能力更强，并能在传统方法表现退化时继续可靠生成可解关卡。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将物理可行性建模、课程式长时序训练和受筛选的随机生成结合起来，针对性改善了强化学习关卡生成中的可解性、稳定性与多样性问题。摘要未提供具体数值，因此改进幅度仍需结合完整实验结果评估。

Abstract: Procedural Content Generation via Reinforcement Learning (PCGRL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for automated game development. However, existing PCGRL frameworks still suffer from severe exploration bottlenecks and scaling limitations when faced with long-sequence generation tasks governed by strict rigid-body rules and non-linear kinematic boundaries (e.g., platformer constraints). Consequently, traditional approaches frequently fail to maintain reliable level solvability on large-scale canvases. To address these challenges, we present a physics-aware, curriculum-guided PCGRL framework. First, we construct an "Inflated Volume Envelope" mechanism, which provides a physics-constrained representation that incorporates continuous kinematic feasibility into the discrete state space of the MDP. Second, to alleviate the sparse reward challenge induced by extremely long decision sequences, we introduce intermediate state-transition feedback and a multi-stage forward extension curriculum learning framework, enabling informative value estimation and stable credit assignment over long-horizon trajectories. Finally, to resolve the issue where deterministic policies after convergence generate highly monotonous map paths with a lack of diversity, we activate high-entropy stochastic inference during the synthesis phase, regularized by a post-hoc parallel rejection filtering mechanism. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly enhances structural diversity while maintaining high physical playability across multi-scale environments. Comparative evaluations across multi-scale canvases demonstrate that our framework maintains superior convergence stability and dimensional scalability, reliably synthesizing solvable levels where traditional PCGRL baselines degrade.

</details>


### [239] [Perception into Imagination: Executable Memory for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22294)
*Xianyao Li,Ruitong Tian,Rui Min,Fang Xu,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可执行感知记忆框架，使智能体在想象未来行动后果时持续更新显式感知记忆，并将其用于奖励预测、价值评估和策略学习；在多个视觉控制任务中取得了更好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理智能体在物体不可见或失去接触后，仍需依据先前观察或触摸到的信息行动。然而，行动会改变这些记忆事实对未来结果的意义。仅保存感知信息，或未将其与行动后果及学习信号关联，难以支持可靠的长期决策。

Method: 提出可执行感知记忆框架，在世界模型进行想象时，除神经潜在状态动态外，还同步推进显式感知状态，并以其演化后的读出辅助奖励预测、价值估计和策略学习。奖励输入与产生该奖励的状态转移对齐，并使用记录动作的开放环路推演结果监督奖励预测模块。

Result: 在十三乘十三的记忆迷宫任务中，交互一百万次后取得三十点三七的回报；相较于使用相同演化读出、但仅用于策略与价值学习的对照方法，高出七点零零，区间为三点八零至十点二一。完整方法在九乘九迷宫和微型世界任务中也改善了控制表现。对视野外、但曾经到达过的位置进行探测时，两种视觉实现均表现出更强的奖励预期。浓缩、触觉恢复和真实触觉帧实验进一步显示，记忆事实分别有助于想象策略训练、依赖运动状态的决策和保留感觉内容。

Conclusion: 研究将持久感知记忆与世界模型预测的行动后果及学习信号相连接，表明在想象过程中动态更新显式记忆能够改善控制与奖励预期。这为面向物理智能体的记忆建模和决策学习提供了一种有效框架。

Abstract: Physical agents must use what they previously saw or touched after visibility or contact is lost. Their actions change the implications of these remembered facts. We introduce executable perceptual memory (EPM), a world-model framework that advances explicit perceptual state alongside neural latent dynamics during imagination. Its evolving read-out informs reward prediction, value estimation and policy learning. We align reward inputs with the transitions that produced them and supervise the reward head on open-loop rollouts of recorded actions. On Memory Maze $13\times13$, EPM reaches 30.37 return at 1M interactions, compared with 23.37 when the same evolving read-out informs only policy and value on a common perceptual front end: a gain of 7.00 [3.80, 10.21]. The complete method also improves control on $9\times9$ and in MiniWorld. Probes of remembered, out-of-view arrivals show stronger reward anticipation in both visual implementations. Concentration, tactile recovery and real tactile frames demonstrate complementary roles of remembered facts in imagined-policy training, motion-dependent decisions and sensory-content retention. EPM connects persistent perception to the predicted consequences and learning signals of a world model for Physical AI.

</details>


### [240] [Similar Is Not Useful: Selecting In-Context Demonstrations by Closed-Loop Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22295)
*Jiachen Li,Zhigen Zhao,Shihao Li,Dongmei Chen,Long Chen,Zhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在检索增强视觉—语言—动作策略中，按闭环任务表现评估示例效用，比按视觉相似度挑选示例更有效；五条高效用示例使成功率提高约十个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依据冻结视觉表征中的画面相似度检索示范，但视觉上相似的示范未必能帮助策略成功完成任务。本文检验示范的视觉相似度是否真能预测其对策略闭环表现的贡献。

Method: 研究在固定的检索增强策略和任务基准上进行，并允许运行时替换示范池。研究者构造了六十个规模相同的示范池，通过回归分析各示范在不同池中的成员身份与闭环成功率，估计单条示范的效用。随后在与效用评分初始状态不重叠的状态上，比较高效用池、最高视觉相似度池和随机池；另评估池与测试场景的相似度、直接搜索示范池，以及相对于原始示范池的改进。

Result: 在十九项任务、七百六十组配对测试中，五条最高效用示范的成功率为百分之六十六点四，高于五条最相似示范的百分之五十五点七和随机池的百分之五十五点五；相对最相似池提高十点八个百分点，统计显著，并在十八项可比较任务中的十五项取得正向提升。该优势也出现在策略从未以示范预热的十二项任务中。视觉相似度对同一任务内成功率的预测很弱，且在场景相同的不同任务间降至随机水平。对示范池进行直接搜索，以一百次执行试验可恢复约三分之二的提升。相较原始二十条示范池，精选五条示范提高七个百分点；在完整示范池中按效用排序检索则提高四点五个百分点，后者证据未达到常用显著性标准。

Conclusion: 示范是否有用，应主要依据其对目标任务闭环成功的实证贡献，而不能仅凭画面相似度判断。基于任务表现估计示范效用能够显著改善示范选择；不过，直接评估效用需要执行试验，如何以更低成本可靠地估计效用仍是后续研究方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) policies fill their context with the demonstrations whose frames look most like the current observation under frozen DINOv2 embeddings. We ask whether visual similarity selects the demonstrations that make the policy succeed. For a frozen retrieval-augmented VLA (RICL on $π_0$-FAST, LIBERO), we make the demonstration pool swappable at run time and score each demonstration's utility by regressing the closed-loop success of 60 fixed-size pools on their membership. On initial states disjoint from those used for scoring, pools of the five highest-utility demonstrations succeed on 66.4% of 760 paired episodes across 19 tasks, pools of the five most similar on 55.7%, and random pools on 55.5%. Utility beats similarity by 10.8 points ($p=3.2\times10^{-6}$; positive on 15 of 18 untied tasks), including on 12 tasks the policy was never primed on, and similarity-selected pools do no better than random ($+0.1$ points). Within a task, a pool's visual similarity to the test scenes tracks its success at Spearman 0.10; across tasks, similarity separates tasks whose scenes differ and falls to chance when two tasks share a scene. The gain comes from on-task closed-loop evidence: best-of-ten direct search over pools recovers two-thirds of it with 100 rollouts. Against the shipped 20-demonstration pool, utility-curated pools of five lead by 7.0 points over 560 paired episodes from two evaluation runs ($p=0.006$), and utility-ordered retrieval inside the full pool leads by 4.5 points ($p=0.055$).

</details>


### [241] [VRLLM: Turning LLMs into Powerful Tabular Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22308)
*Yingtong Dou,Shubham Jain,Junpeng Wang,Hadi Abdullah,Vineeth Rakesh Mohan,Yuzhong Chen,Liang Wang,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究展示了如何将预训练的小型大语言模型适配到大规模表格分类：通过数十亿行标注数据微调，模型在多项比较中超过多种表格分类器及一款生产级深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在文本任务上表现突出，但它们能否胜任大规模表格分类仍不明确。已有研究通常发现，其表现不及树模型或专为表格数据设计的神经网络，因此需要验证紧凑型预训练模型经过充分训练和工程适配后，是否能达到工业级表格预测性能。

Method: 研究团队使用数十亿行标注表格数据，对预训练的拉玛三点二十亿参数模型进行微调，并系统介绍数据准备与训练方案。研究还通过消融实验考察设计选择和模型组件的作用，进行部署验证；同时在相同任务上评测前沿大语言模型，并分析不同模型家族及代际的预测行为。可解释性实验用于研究模型学到的特征关联。

Result: 微调后的签证研究大语言模型在该任务上超过了多种表格分类器，也超过了研究团队具有竞争力的生产级深度学习模型。可解释性分析表明，该模型学到了与具体特征相关的预测关联；组件消融结果显示，最早的两个前馈网络层有助于任务信息的形成，使其能够被线性解码。摘要未提供具体指标或数据集细节。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一套将紧凑型大语言模型用于工业表格预测的工程方案，并以实证案例说明：在大规模标注数据和适当训练设计的支持下，预训练语言模型有望在特定表格分类任务中达到乃至超过专用模型的表现。结论主要适用于论文所研究的任务与部署场景，能否推广到其他数据和任务仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on text, but whether pretrained LLMs are effective for large-scale tabular classification remains unclear, as many previous works demonstrate that LLMs' tabular data classification performance falls behind tree-based models or neural architectures designed for tabular data. At Visa Research, we managed to fine-tune a pretrained Llama 3.2 1B LLM on billions of rows of labeled tabular data and make it outperform a wide array of tabular classifiers and even our competitive production deep learning model. For ease of reference, we refer to the model as the Visa Research LLM (VRLLM). In this paper, we share how we achieved this exciting result, covering data preparation, training recipe, ablation studies of design choices and deployment validation. We also benchmark frontier LLMs on the same task and analyze their prediction behavior across model families and generations. Our interpretability experiments suggest that VRLLM learns feature-specific predictive associations, while component ablations indicate that the earliest two FFN layers contribute to the emergence of linearly decodable task information. Our paper provides an engineering recipe and a focused empirical case study on adapting compact LLMs to industrial tabular prediction tasks.

</details>


### [242] [BQ-LoRA: Binary-Quantized Low-Rank Adapters as Implicit Regularizers for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22334)
*Juyoung Park,Heejun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出二值适配器微调方法，在大幅降低适配器存储的同时，维持或提升多项基准表现，并给出理论分析与部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化大语言模型的参数高效微调通常采用四位量化基础模型搭配半精度低秩适配器，但适配器仍占用较多存储。论文探讨适配器参数是否必须使用半精度，并希望在缩减存储的同时保持模型效果与泛化能力。

Method: 提出二值量化低秩适配框架，将每个适配器参数压缩为一位，并结合逐层学习的缩放因子与缩放直通估计器训练。框架包含两种方案：范数缩放方案以保持范数的缩放方式进行二值化，并采用截断直通估计器；可学习阈值方案则学习二值化阈值。理论上，论文刻画了该方法的假设类：其元素范数有界，在典型集合上的谱范数界比最坏情形紧约一个秩因子，更新矩阵满秩且近似各向同性，并据此推导出显式的泛化误差界。部署方面，作者将二值内积运算加入批量矩阵向量乘法内核，以提升适配器服务效率。

Result: 适配器存储量减少约十三至十六倍，训练显存与原有低秩适配方法相当，单步训练时间增加不到百分之四。在昆文二点五系列模型上，各模型规模的基准平均分均高于对照方法约零点八至三点五分；在拉玛二代上虽保持竞争力，但未能全面超过最强半精度适配器基线。昆文二点五七十亿参数模型的二值适配器使四位基础模型的维基文本二困惑度仅变化零点零六，而对照低秩适配方法使其恶化零点五九。部署实验显示，同一张八十吉字节显存的加速卡可容纳的适配器数量提升十五点六倍；研究原型在偏斜的合成负载下消除了缓存未命中。

Conclusion: 研究表明，半精度适配器并非量化大模型微调的必需选择：二值适配器可显著节省推理存储，并在部分模型上取得更佳效果，同时具备可分析的范数、各向同性与泛化性质。不过，其性能优势并非在所有模型和强基线下都一致；训练显存也未因适配器二值化而降低。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of quantized large language models is dominated by QLoRA, which combines 4-bit weight quantization with FP16 low-rank adapters. We ask whether the FP16 adapter precision is necessary. Replacing it with a single binary bit per adapter parameter, combined with a learned per-layer scale $s$ and a Scaled Straight-Through Estimator, reduces adapter storage by 13-16x. We introduce two variants -- NormScale, which binarizes under a norm-preserving scale with a clipped STE, and LearnThresh, which makes the binarization threshold learnable -- unified under a framework we call BQ-LoRA. Our main theoretical contribution is a tight characterization of the BQ-LoRA hypothesis class: it has bounded entrywise norm, a typical-set spectral norm bound that is $Θ(r)$ times sharper than the worst case, a full-rank, near-isotropic update, and consequently a Rademacher complexity that yields an explicit $\tilde{O}(s_{\max}α\sqrt{d_{out}/(rn)})$ generalization bound. FP16 LoRA has no comparable a priori norm or isotropy control. On Qwen2.5 (1.5B-72B), BQ-LoRA exceeds QLoRA's benchmark averages at every scale by 0.8-3.5 points; on LLaMA-2 (7B/70B) it remains competitive at the same storage reduction but does not uniformly lead the strongest FP16-adapter baselines (LoftQ at 7B, QLoRA at 70B). On Qwen2.5-7B the binary adapter preserves the 4-bit base WikiText-2 PPL within 0.06 (9.86 vs. 9.80) while QLoRA worsens it by 0.59. The reduction applies to inference-time adapter storage and serving; training memory matches QLoRA and step time rises by under 4%. We translate the storage saving into a deployment study, BQ-SGMV, by extending Punica's SGMV kernel with a binary inner-product primitive: the same A100-80GB holds 15.6x more adapters in HBM, and our research prototype eliminates cache misses under a skewed synthetic workload.

</details>


### [243] [Scaling Laws for Vision-Language Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22351)
*Seng Pei Liew,Teppei Suzuki,Seiya Tokui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种描述视觉与语言联合预训练学习动态的缩放定律，可预测不同融合方式、数据配比、训练步数和模型规模下的验证损失，并用于权衡早期融合与后期融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉—语言模型通常采用后期融合，即在现成语言模型上继续进行图文预训练；近期也出现从预训练一开始就混合模态的早期融合。但两种范式的性能取舍尚不清楚，导致训练方案往往依赖成本高昂的试验和经验启发。

Method: 系统分析多模态预训练中的学习动态，将验证损失拆分为两部分：不同数据类型以不同速率形成的基础学习轨迹，以及模态间竞争引发的分布偏移。在此基础上推导统一缩放定律，用于预测不同数据混合比例、训练步数和模型规模下的损失表现。

Result: 实验表明，该缩放定律能够可靠预测多模态学习动态。在固定数据预算下，早期融合更能保留文本性能，后期融合则更有利于视觉性能；随着模型规模增大，整体取舍越来越倾向早期融合。

Conclusion: 早期融合与后期融合存在由数据预算和任务模态共同决定的性能权衡。论文提出的缩放定律为理解这种权衡、预测训练结果及制定融合策略提供了统一依据。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as central foundation models combining multimodal perception with text comprehension. While VLMs are traditionally built via late fusion by continued pre-training an off-the-shelf language model on multimodal (image-text) data, mixing modalities from the start of pre-training (early fusion) has recently gained traction. However, the fundamental trade-offs between these paradigms remain poorly understood, resulting in expensive and heuristic-driven training recipes. In this paper, we systematically analyze the learning dynamics of these multimodal pre-training processes, showing that validation losses during vision-language fusion can be modeled by separating the base learning trajectory, where different data types contribute at distinct rates, from a distribution shift driven by the competition between modalities. We derive a scaling law that unifiedly describes these dynamics, enabling accurate loss predictions across mixture ratios, training steps, and model sizes. Crucially, our law characterizes the trade-off between early and late fusion: under a fixed data budget, early fusion preserves text performance while late fusion favors vision performance, yielding a trade-off that increasingly tilts toward early fusion as model size scales. Extensive experiments confirm that our scaling law reliably captures multimodal learning dynamics and governs fusion decisions.

</details>


### [244] [Fair Bubble Sort: Provably Optimal Fair Ranking with Continuous Sensitive Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22352)
*Antonio Ferrara,Fabio Vitale,Atsushi Miyauchi,Francesco Bonchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出连续成对平等（CPP）来衡量连续受保护属性与排名位置之间的关联，并用公平冒泡排序（FBS）在满足公平约束的同时，尽量少改动原始排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平排序方法通常把年龄、收入等连续属性切分为类别。这种做法依赖任意阈值，会损失细粒度信息，也忽视属性本身的自然顺序。论文旨在不离散化属性的前提下实现公平排序。

Method: 论文以排名位置和连续受保护属性之间的肯德尔相关定义连续成对平等，并要求排序满足指定的公平约束，同时最小化其与初始分数排序之间的肯梅尼距离。为此，作者提出公平冒泡排序，通过有针对性的相邻交换逐步调整排名。

Result: 理论上，公平冒泡排序在未加权肯梅尼距离下可得到精确最优解；在分数加权情形下，算法提供近似保证。其运行时间取决于满足公平约束所需的交换次数。合成数据和真实数据实验显示，该方法具有良好可扩展性，并稳定优于基于离散化的基线，在公平性与准确性的权衡上取得更优结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为连续受保护属性下的公平排序提供了无需阈值化的公平性定义与高效后处理算法。结果表明，通过最少化排名调整来满足连续属性公平约束，能够兼顾理论保证与实际效果。

Abstract: Most fair-ranking methods assume categorical protected attributes, requiring continuous attributes such as age or income to be discretized into groups. This introduces arbitrary thresholds, loses fine-grained information, and ignores the natural ordering of the attribute. In this paper, we study the problem of fair ranking with continuous protected attributes without relying on thresholding. We introduce Continuous Pairwise Parity (CPP), a fairness notion based on Kendall correlation between ranking positions and the protected attribute, and seek rankings that satisfy a prescribed CPP constraint while minimizing their Kemeny distance from an initial score-based ranking. To solve this problem, we propose Fair Bubble Sort (FBS), an efficient post-processing algorithm based on targeted adjacent swaps. We prove that FBS is exactly optimal for the unweighted Kemeny distance and provide approximation guarantees for the score-weighted setting. FBS is output-sensitive, with running time determined by the number of swaps required to meet the fairness constraint. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that FBS is scalable and consistently outperforms discretization-based baselines, achieving a superior fairness-accuracy Pareto front.

</details>


### [245] [InfuseLoop: Cross-Trajectory Latent Injection for Scalable Parallel Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22426)
*Zhaofeng Sun,Lanxiang Hu,Yu-Yang Qian,Jiaqi Leng,Yuanbo Yang,Chi Zhang,Youyou Lu,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出跨轨迹潜在信息注入机制，在不改变并行训练与循环并发执行方式的前提下，缓解并行循环变换器随循环深度增加而出现的训练不稳定和收益递减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行循环变换器通过重叠循环迭代利用原本闲置的解码计算，但循环数增加时容易出现训练损失突增，模型质量提升也逐渐减弱。作者认为，循环间的信息传递受限于仅传递当前轨迹的前一状态，未能利用其他预测轨迹中已经完成且因果上可用的表示。

Method: 提出跨轨迹潜在信息注入机制：通过轻量级注意力残差，将后续预测轨迹中因果上可用的隐藏状态汇集并注入当前迭代。该方法保留按词元并行训练和循环并发执行，额外隐藏状态存储量为循环数与隐藏维度乘积的量级。

Result: 实验表明，该机制消除了训练损失突增，并使验证损失随循环数增加而持续改善，效果最多验证至八个循环。八循环时，相较于原始并行循环模型，困惑度降低百分之三点四至百分之十七点一；相较于无循环基线，降低百分之十点五至百分之三十七点三。下游平均准确率分别提升零点八四和一点九九个百分点。解码耗时仅比原始并行循环模型增加百分之零点三，比无循环基线增加百分之二十三点三，且逻辑键值缓存占用不变。

Conclusion: 研究表明，循环深度扩展的瓶颈不仅在于计算量，也在于循环间可利用的信息范围。跨轨迹注入以较小的存储和推理开销增强了迭代间的信息交流，提升了深层循环模型的训练稳定性与性能。

Abstract: Parallel Looped Transformers (PLTs) use otherwise idle decoding compute by overlapping recurrent iterations. However, vanilla PLTs scale poorly with depth: they exhibit training-loss spikes, and their quality gains shrink as more loops are added. The benefit of additional loops depends on the information available to each iteration, so we examine whether the existing computational dependencies limit effective depth scaling. We find that only the preceding in-trajectory state is passed across loop boundaries, even though completed cross-trajectory representations are already available. Based on this finding, we propose InfuseLoop, a cross-trajectory latent injection mechanism. It aggregates causally available hidden states from later prediction trajectories through lightweight attention residuals. This design preserves token-parallel training and concurrent loop execution, and requires only $O(Rd)$ additional hidden-state storage. Experiments show that InfuseLoop eliminates training-loss spikes and improves validation-loss scaling up to eight loops. At eight loops, InfuseLoop reduces perplexity by 3.4--17.1% relative to vanilla PLTs and by 10.5--37.3% relative to the non-looped baseline. It also improves average downstream accuracy by 0.84 and 1.99 percentage points over these two baselines. InfuseLoop incurs only 0.3% decoding time over vanilla PLTs and 23.3% over the non-looped baseline, while keeping the same logical KV-cache footprint as vanilla PLTs.

</details>


### [246] [Encoding, Decodability, and Use: A Five-Layer Framework for Benchmark Measurement Audits with Evidence from UAV Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22434)
*Zhirong Su,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并应用五层分析框架，指出基准准确率不能单独证明模型掌握了目标能力；在「空中意图」任务中，模型准确率更可能依赖程序记忆和静态外观线索，而非径向速度读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对一种常见推断提出质疑：模型在基准测试中得分高，是否就意味着它理解了基准所测量的能力？作者认为，这一推断混合了多个不同环节，因而需要区分模型是否观察到相关信息、是否形成了相应表征、该表征能否被解码、模型是否在任务中使用它，以及最终行为是否确实依赖它。

Method: 作者提出并在「空中意图」任务上实例化五层框架：观察、表征、解码、使用和行为，并将信息编码、可解码性与实际使用明确区分。研究检验图像中径向速度线索及任务设计的彩色特征，通过多特征读取、聚类层面的最小可检测效应分析和两组特征注入实验评估表征与解码；再考察程序记忆、静态线索以及外观和边界框轨迹对任务准确率的作用。

Result: 观察层面，20个无人机程序中有7个的观察结果不可靠；径向速度的直接视觉相关线索在尺度变化仅为0.26%时便出现性能下限。表征层面，两种任务设计的彩色特征呈现名义信号：光流径向偏差与目标变量的相关为0.257（显著性概率0.030），边界框光流散度的相关为0.203（显著性概率0.029）；但多特征读取的折均测试相关均未超过聚类层面的最小可检测效应，最大的正向全局样本外相关仅为0.080。解码层面，在多特征校正阈值下，最小可检测相关为绝对值0.599；观测到的0.080对应的检验功效为0.00。两组注入实验从两侧支持该检测效应量的条件：与目标对齐的注入达到或超过模拟检验功效时可被检测，正交注入则未被检测。使用层面，准确率与程序记忆相一致，程序身份指标为0.937、0.597和0.210，且静态线索也能提供解释。行为层面，外观条件准确率为0.6552，高于边界框轨迹条件的0.4080；聚类配对差值为0.2471（显著性概率0.0132），与准确率不依赖径向速度读取的解释相符。

Conclusion: 这项研究表明，基准准确率本身不足以证明模型具备基准所声称的能力。对「空中意图」任务而言，现有证据未显示径向速度信息在模型内部形成了足以支持可靠解码的表征，准确率则可能由程序记忆和静态外观线索支撑。五层框架为判断模型究竟观察、表征、解码并使用了何种信息提供了更细致的评估路径；同时，未检出相关信号应结合实验的检测能力解释，不能简单视作绝对不存在该信号。

Abstract: Benchmark accuracy is routinely read as evidence that a model understands the construct the benchmark measures. We propose and instantiate on AeroIntent a five-layer framework that decomposes that reading into observation, representation, decoding, use, and behaviour. The framework separates encoding, decodability, and use as distinct levels. In the instantiation, the observation layer is unreliable on 7 of the 20 Drone programs and floors the direct visual correlate of radial velocity at a 0.26\% scale change. The representation layer exposes two task-designed RGB features with nominal signals. RAFT flow radial bias reaches $r=+0.257$ ($p=0.030$), and bounding-box optical-flow divergence reaches $r=+0.203$ ($p=0.029$). No multi-feature readout gives a fold-mean test correlation above the cluster minimum detectable effect (MDE), and the largest positive global out-of-sample correlation is $r=+0.080$. The decoding layer sets the MDE at $|r|=0.599$ at the multi-feature Bonferroni threshold ($α=0.00714$, power 0.80), and the observed $r=+0.080$ has power 0.00. Two injection experiments establish the MDE's condition from both sides: aligned injections are detected at or above the simulated power, and orthogonal injections are not detected. The use layer finds accuracy consistent with program memory (program-identity 0.937/0.597/0.210) and multi-region static cues. The behaviour layer is consistent with accuracy not requiring a radial-velocity readout (appearance 0.6552 vs bounding-box trajectory 0.4080, cluster-level paired $Δ=+0.2471$, $p=0.0132$).

</details>


### [247] [Eyes in the Update? Learning Where Rewards Land in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22438)
*Lixuan He,Tianzhe Chu,Shulin Tian,Shikang Zheng,Feng Chen,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 凝视强化学习根据模型自身的跨模态表征与预测不确定性，将整条生成轨迹的奖励重新分配到更关键的位置，在不增加监督或修改模型结构的情况下提升图像与视频推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态强化学习通常把结果奖励施加于完整生成轨迹，但视觉证据在回答中的出现位置稀疏且不均匀，导致奖励难以准确分配到真正依赖视觉信息、影响决策的内容。已有方法借助外部标注、感知模块或逐步监督提供细粒度信号；本文进一步探讨能否直接从模型内部的多模态计算中推断奖励应落在哪些位置。

Method: 提出一种由表征驱动的策略优化方法：利用模型内部的跨模态几何关系和局部预测不确定性，构造潜在奖励分配场。该机制提高视觉依据充分且对决策重要的位置的更新权重，同时将权重归一化为均值为一，使每条轨迹获得的总奖励保持不变，只调整奖励在不同位置间的分配。方法不需要过程监督、额外感知模块或架构改动。

Result: 摘要报告，该方法在多个图像与视频基准上均带来性能提升，并在两类视觉语言模型上取得了一致改善；但摘要未提供具体分数或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 多模态模型自身的内部表征可用于细化轨迹级奖励的逐位置分配，从而在保留原有总奖励的同时，让策略更新更聚焦于视觉依据充分、对推理决策关键的内容。

Abstract: Multimodal post-training has moved from broad instruction tuning toward reinforcement learning for image and video reasoning, where outcome rewards can improve final-answer accuracy. Yet these rewards are assigned to complete generated trajectories, while visual evidence is used only at sparse and uneven positions inside the response. Recent work has begun to make multimodal RL more token-sensitive through grounding signals or external token-level weighting. We ask a more specific question: can the allocation of the same trajectory-level reward be inferred from the model's own multimodal computation, rather than from an auxiliary annotator, perception module, or process label? We propose Gaze-RL, a representation-driven policy optimization method that redistributes completion-level advantage through a latent reward landing field. The field is computed from internal cross-modal geometry and local predictive uncertainty, assigning larger update weight to positions that are both visually anchored and decision-bearing while remaining mean-one, so the total credit of each rollout is preserved and only its positionwise allocation changes. Gaze-RL requires no process supervision, auxiliary perception module, or architectural modification, and improves both Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5 across a broad range of image and video benchmarks.

</details>


### [248] [Storing the Gemma 4 K=V Cache Once: Exact Global-KV Memory Savings in a Paged Serving Engine with Unchanged Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23035)
*Kushagra Bainsla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法利用格玛4三百一十亿参数模型全局注意力中键和值的线性关系，将每个键值头、每个令牌的缓存从一千零二十四个数压缩为六百四十个数；在基本保持输出一致的同时，降低显存占用并提升解码性能，但会增加少量单步开销，且尚不能与默认配置的八位缓存叠加使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型报告指出，其全局注意力设计可将键值缓存最多减少百分之三十七点五，但作者检查的开放式推理引擎仍分别保存完整的键和值，未利用二者之间的结构关系。因此，论文旨在进一步减少缓存冗余，提升长上下文推理时的显存利用率和解码能力。

Method: 利用该模型中值为归一化键投影、键为值乘以固定权重这一关系，在实数运算中以一行六百四十个数精确表示原本分开存储的键和值。运行时不改动分页注意力内核，而是通过两个重叠的跨步视图，将压缩数据分别作为键缓存和值缓存读取。作者在推理引擎原型中实现并评估了这一方案。

Result: 在十六位模型上，压缩实现与对照缓存的同算术模拟结果逐位一致；与原始引擎相比，四百三十二个最终答案中有两个不同，差异处于引擎自身运行波动范围内。三万二千至六万四千令牌时，每请求的稳定解码缓存内存降低约一点三四至一点四零倍。双英伟达A100卡的预填充—解码测试中，解码每步处理令牌数在一万六千令牌提示下提升一点三八倍，在八千令牌提示下提升一点二四倍；模型对缓存块池的预测与实测相差不到百分之一。即时解码吞吐分别提升一点三零倍和一点一一倍。另一方面，在相同批量下，原型每步运行慢百分之四至百分之九；默认尺度下也尚不能与八位键值缓存叠加。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用模型注意力结构中的键值冗余，可以在不改变注意力内核的情况下显著压缩缓存，并在缓存容量受限的场景中提升有效解码吞吐。其收益受到批量、提示长度和缓存池容量影响，且当前实现仍有单步开销及量化兼容性限制；后续需优化运行时实现并验证更广泛的部署场景。

Abstract: In the global attention layers of Gemma 4 31B, the value is the normalized key projection, and the key is the value scaled by a fixed weight, with rotary position encoding on one quarter of its dimensions. The Gemma 4 report states that this design reduces the global KV cache by up to 37.5%, but the open serving engines that we checked still store a full key and a full value: 1,024 values per KV head and token. We store one 640-value row instead, exact in real arithmetic, and an unchanged paged attention kernel reads it through two overlapping strided views as its key cache and its value cache. In a vLLM prototype with the 16-bit 31B model, the outputs are bit-identical to an emulation of the same arithmetic on the stock cache, 2 of 432 final answers differ from the stock engine (within its run-to-run noise), and the steady decode KV memory per request falls by a factor of 1.34 to 1.40 at 32K to 64K tokens. On a prefill/decode pair of two A100 GPUs with NIXL transfer and CPU offload, a decode engine with a full 15 GiB pool ran 1.38 times the stock decode tokens per step for 16K-token prompts (repeated to 0.06%) and 1.24 times for 8K-token prompts; a model of the vLLM block pool predicted both ratios to within 1% (the engine was 0.7% to 0.9% higher) and matched the change between them. The eager decode throughput rose by 1.30 and 1.11 times, and on this path the compact engine gave the same output tokens as the emulation for 8 prompts at batch 1. The run-time prototype makes an eager step 4% to 9% slower at equal batch, and the method does not yet stack with an 8-bit KV cache at the default scale.

</details>


### [249] [How Many Training Labels Are Wrong? Auditing a Fitted Model through Gradient Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22449)
*Yuchen Li,Zongqi Fan,Xintong Li,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种利用单个已训练模型的逐样本梯度审查训练标签的方法，无需干净数据、留出集、阈值或额外训练；即使模型已拟合错误标签，该方法仍能较可靠地识别噪声标签并估计噪声率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的标签噪声估计方法依赖留出集或模型预测，但留出集的结果受其抽样方式影响；而模型一旦拟合错误标签，错误样本的损失会变小，预测也会与错误标签一致，使基于损失或预测的方法失效。论文希望直接从已训练模型中识别错误标签并估计其比例。

Method: 对每个样本计算其标签所诱导的梯度，将梯度各分量的大小归一化为梯度分配，并与各类别的平均分配进行比较。若把样本改标为最接近的其他类别后，其梯度分配比保留原标签时更符合相应类别的平均分配，就将该样本标记为疑似错标。被标记样本的比例作为噪声率估计。该规则不设阈值，也不需要干净数据、留出集或进一步训练。

Result: 在已拟合百分之九十九至百分之百错误标签的模型上，该方法在噪声较重的人类标注数据中区分错标与正确标签的曲线下面积达到零点九四至零点九六，优于特征空间间隔、损失、梯度范数和自影响等指标。在图像分类实验中，预测或特征间隔估计的噪声率仅为百分之零至六，而该方法读出的噪声率为真实值的百分之五十四至九十六，且至少百分之九十三的被计入样本确为错标。对短暂训练、尚未拟合错误标签的模型，噪声率估计与真实值相差不超过一点七个百分点。与置信学习相比，该方法的错标区分能力大致相当，但噪声率估计通常不如置信学习准确，精确率则更高。审查五千个样本在单块图形处理器上耗时十四至二十七秒。

Conclusion: 逐样本梯度分配能够提供模型输出之外的错标信号，在模型已记住错误标签时仍具较强识别能力，并可高效估计训练集噪声率。该方法尤其适用于缺少干净验证数据、且模型已经充分训练的标签审计场景；但其噪声率估计与现有方法相比存在精确率和准确度之间的权衡。

Abstract: How many labels in a training set are wrong, and which ones? The usual answers read a held-out set or the model's outputs. A held-out reading depends on how the held-out set was drawn, and an output-based reading fails once the model has fitted its wrong labels, because the loss of a fitted wrong label is small and the prediction agrees with it. We read the answer from the per-example gradients of a single trained model. Each example, together with its label, asks the network to change in a particular way. We normalise the magnitudes of its gradient into a gradient allocation and compare it with the mean allocation of every class. An example is flagged when relabelling it as its nearest other class makes its allocation fit better than under its own label, and the share of flagged examples is the reading of the noise rate. The rule has no threshold and needs no clean data, no held-out set and no further training. After the model has fitted 99-100% of its wrong labels, the allocation still separates wrong from right labels with an AUC of 0.94-0.96 on the noisiest human labels of CIFAR-10N, where a feature-space margin falls to 0.79-0.84 and the loss, the gradient norm and the self-influence of an example to 0.73-0.76. On such a fitted model, readings of the noise rate from predictions or feature margins fall to 0-6% at true rates of 8-58% on CIFAR-10 with ViT-S, while the audit reads 54-96% of the true rate, and at least 93% of the examples it counts carry wrong labels. On a briefly trained model, before the wrong labels are fitted, the reading lies within 1.7 percentage points of the true noise rate on CIFAR-10 from 0% to 58% noise. Confident learning, which trains four further models, separates wrong labels about as well (AUC 0.925-0.998 against 0.900-0.995) and mostly reads the noise rate more closely, at a lower precision. An audit of 5,000 examples takes 14 to 27 seconds on one GPU.

</details>


### [250] [Escaping Latent Traps by Remembering: Training-Free Episodic Kernel Repulsion for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22470)
*Yican Zhao,Bolin Yang,Ruizhe Wang,Haiyang Guo,Fuji Yang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的情景核排斥代价，通过避开智能体已访问过的潜在状态，减少规划陷阱并提高目标到达率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通常通过缩小预测状态与目标图像潜在表征之间的距离来规划，但这种距离并非沿可行路径计算的测地距离，可能在墙后、拐角和死胡同形成局部极小值。缺乏记忆的滚动重规划会使智能体反复停留或回访这些区域，难以接近目标。

Method: 提出情景核排斥方法：维护一份经过新颖性筛选的记忆，记录智能体实际访问过的潜在状态；利用候选计划与记忆状态之间的前若干个高斯核相似度构造惩罚项，并按当前基础代价重新缩放，使其在不同模型中的影响强度更可比。该方法无需训练，并可直接加入现有规划器。

Result: 在两个潜在世界模型和四项基准任务中，该方法将平均成功率分别从百分之三十一点八提升至百分之四十六点七，以及从百分之五十四点七提升至百分之六十四点零。对照实验显示，规划通常会避开已访问状态；导航中即使基础代价更高，也能减少受困和重复访问。在八种设置中的七种，其效果达到或超过将采样量增加至四倍，计算开销约为原来的零点八至一点五倍。无需调参迁移至另一种世界模型及其规划器后，在官方和长目标导航任务中分别提升六点七和十九点三个百分点；在长时程迷宫与推箱任务中，成功率分别由百分之四十四点七升至百分之百、由百分之二十一升至百分之三十二。

Conclusion: 访问记忆可为潜在空间规划提供有效的探索与避陷信号。情景核排斥在不改变模型和规划预算的情况下，改善了多种任务的成功率，并表现出跨模型迁移能力；结果表明，针对已访问状态的惩罚能够补足单纯潜在距离目标所缺乏的路径与历史信息。

Abstract: Latent world models plan towards a goal image by minimising the distance between predicted and goal latents. This distance is not geodesic: it has local minima, latent traps, behind walls, at corners and in dead ends, and memoryless receding-horizon replanning keeps the agent in them, revisiting states far from the goal. We propose Episodic Kernel Repulsion (EKR), a training-free cost term that penalises candidate plans by a top-$k$ Gaussian kernel to a novelty-gated memory of the latents the agent has actually visited, rescaled by the current base cost so that its strength is comparable across models. Across two JEPA world models and four benchmarks, EKR raises average success from 31.8% to 46.7% (LeWM) and from 54.7% to 64.0% (H-JEPA) with the same model, planner and budget. Same-memory counterfactuals show that, in most settings, EKR pushes plans away from visited states, in navigation, often at a higher base cost; stuck episodes and revisits drop accordingly. In seven of eight settings it matches or exceeds $4\times$ samples at $0.8$--$1.5\times$ compute. Without tuning, it transfers to DINO-WM with CEM: $+6.7$ and $+19.3$ points on official and longer-goal Wall tasks, and from 44.7% to 100.0% and from 21.0% to 32.0% on long-horizon PointMaze and Push-T.

</details>


### [251] [Right Labels, Wrong Reasons: Signed Feature Attribution for Multi-Label Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22515)
*Kenton White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多标签分类器的全局解释方法，通过统一的费舍尔线性判别模型，为每个标签生成带方向的特征重要性排序；实验显示该方法解释忠实度高，且能揭示不同分类器可能依据不同特征作出相同预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器即使预测正确，也可能依赖不可靠或分散的特征组合。不同模型家族可能使用不同特征，且没有单一特征占主导，因此仅看预测结果难以判断模型是否真正依据合理线索。论文旨在提供一种统一、可比较的多标签全局解释，并识别可能“预测正确但理由不当”的标签。

Method: 提出费舍尔线性判别解释器：直接对完整的二元标签矩阵拟合单一多标签线性判别模型，在同一特征坐标系中为各标签生成带正负方向的特征归因列表。该方法通过跨标签汇总协方差来估计判别方向，并与逐标签估计方案比较；同时考察使用不同数量特征时，解释对黑箱预测的复现程度及其复杂度。

Result: 在所评测的数据集和分类器家族中，该方法始终能以较高忠实度复现黑箱预测。跨标签汇总协方差在多数数据集上优于逐标签方案，尤其在标签数量多、正例稀少时优势更明显。随着纳入特征数量增加，忠实度与复杂度之间呈单调提升关系；使用适量特征后，其样本内忠实度可达到或超过二元相关性基线方法。判别权重向量的大小还能为标签可分性提供界限，提示哪些标签可能存在预测正确但依据不当的风险。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个统一且可调节复杂度的多标签全局解释框架，能够兼顾预测忠实度与特征简洁性。实验还表明，不同分类器虽大体同意预测结果，却可能依赖不同特征子集；因此，预测一致并不意味着决策依据一致。

Abstract: When a multi-label classifier assigns the right labels, it may do so for the wrong reasons. Diffuse feature combinations may vary across classifier families and lack a dominant feature. This paper proposes FLEX (Fisher LDA Explainer), a global surrogate that fits a single direct multi-label Fisher LDA over the full binary label matrix, producing signed feature attribution lists for each label in a single coherent coordinate system. Across the evaluated benchmarks and classifier families, FLEX reproduces the black box's predictions with consistently high fidelity. Pooling covariance across labels outperforms a per-label alternative on most datasets, with the advantage growing largest where labels are numerous and positive examples are scarce. FLEX's fidelity-complexity Pareto curve is monotonically increasing, giving practitioners a predictable path to higher-fidelity explanations as more features are used, and its in-sample fidelity matches or leads Binary Relevance alternatives once a modest feature budget is allowed. The magnitude of each discriminant weight vector bounds its label's separability, flagging labels that may be predicted correctly for the wrong reasons. The evaluated classifiers largely agree on which predictions to make, but reach those decisions through different feature subsets when no single feature dominates.

</details>


### [252] [Large-scale Neuron Community Discovery Reveals Neuron-Sparse Behaviors in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23112)
*Igor Shilov,Thomas Fel,Tal Haklay,Lucius Bushnaq,Owen Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出神经元社区发现方法，从大语言模型中识别跨提示反复使用、并对特定计算具有因果作用的神经元群体；在大规模实验中发现的社区可支持语法、复制、加法等多种功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明，单次前向计算往往能在事后精简为少量神经元，但尚不清楚这些神经元群是否会在不同输入间重复出现，能否构成稳定且有明确功能的计算单元。论文旨在发现可复用、可解释并具有因果作用的神经元群体。

Method: 提出无监督的神经元社区发现方法：先利用基于梯度的归因识别与计算相关的神经元，再将跨提示反复共同归因的神经元连接成图，并通过社区检测寻找群体。研究在拉玛三点一八十亿参数模型的千万级提示上应用该方法，并在保留的提示上进行三类因果检验：移除社区以检验其必要性；在指定层子集中仅保留社区，以检验其充分性；移除相关层以检验层的必要性。

Result: 方法发现了数千个候选社区。通过因果检验的社区涉及语法一致、词元概率校准、固定短语、上下文内复制、加法和乘法等功能。若每个社区最多包含一百二十八个神经元，在保留提示中，百分之十点三的提示至少有一层的计算贡献可由稀疏神经元群解释；要求涉及至少四层时覆盖率为百分之八点七，至少十二层时为百分之三点三。

Conclusion: 实验表明，模型中少量已有神经元能够组成跨提示复用的计算组件，并且可通过大规模无监督方法加以发现。这为理解神经网络内部的模块化计算提供了证据，但结果所称的解释范围是特定提示和层上的贡献，并不意味着所有模型计算都已被这些社区解释。

Abstract: What reusable units of computation can we discover within a language model's neurons? Prior work has shown that individual forward passes can often be sparsified post-hoc to small groups of neurons, but it has been unclear whether these groups form coherent computational entities reused across inputs. We introduce neuron community discovery, an unsupervised method for finding neuron groups that recur across prompts and are causally necessary and sufficient, within the layers they span, for common computations. The method combines gradient-based attribution, a graph connecting neurons that are repeatedly attributed together, and community detection. Applied to Llama-3.1-8B using ten million prompts, it discovers thousands of candidate communities. We evaluate them on held-out prompts through three causal tests: community necessity, sufficiency within a layer subset, and layer necessity. These tests identify groups whose removal disrupts predictions and whose retention preserves predictions despite ablating the remaining MLP neurons in the tested layers. Discovered communities support functions like grammatical agreement, token-probability calibration, fixed phrases, in-context copying, addition, and multiplication. With communities capped at 128 neurons across all layers, 10.3% of held-out prompts have at least one layer's contribution explained on a neuron-sparse basis. Coverage remains 8.7% when requiring at least four layers and 3.3% when requiring at least twelve. These results show that small groups of existing neurons can provide reusable computational components across prompts, and that such groups can be discovered at scale.

</details>


### [253] [Testing World Models on Actions They Have Never Seen: Held-Out Plans, Forecast Accuracy and Price Rules in Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22537)
*Karthik Iyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出检验行动条件世界模型能否可靠预测未见行动的方法，并发现常规预测精度未必能反映模型对新行动的预测能力；加入价格规则和少量随机测试方案，可在部分场景提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的重要用途是回答“如果采取某项行动，世界会如何变化”。但模型对历史中未出现过的行动是否可信，不能仅凭常规预测表现判断。作者因此希望建立一种可用于不同领域的检验框架，直接评估模型对未见行动的预测，并比较复杂世界模型与简单模型。

Method: 研究设计了将部分行动方案从训练数据中预先留出的测试，并以已知结果评估预测；同时检验常规预测误差能否预示未见方案上的误差，也将同一测试应用于简单模型。作者在零售定价与促销场景分析了2,853个拟合模型，并在生成的模拟环境、多个真实零售数据集及药物剂量模拟环境中进行验证。研究还测试了行动效果预训练、潜在表征一致性检查、计划日期适配、记录常规价格及价格规则、加入少量随机测试方案等做法。

Result: 常规预测精度在不同类型模型之间与未见方案误差存在中等相关，但对同一世界模型的不同版本预测力较弱；在商家倾向于高需求周制定计划时，相关关系甚至反转，最低为负0.69。针对行动效果的训练改造未降低新方案误差。记录常规价格和三种价格规则能改善部分测试结果；若对数线性模型的价格曲线形式符合真实规律，它表现最佳，但当真实曲线超出规则设定时，带规则的世界模型在40个模拟世界中的36个优于带规则的对数线性模型，且去掉循环单元后表现不变。真实数据中，规则降低了对极深降价方案的误差，但对持续性价格变化没有改善。加入少量随机测试方案能小幅帮助所有模型。药物剂量模拟中，累积药量信息使世界模型误差从2.86降至1.91，但模型仍只预测到真实收益的0.12至0.32。

Conclusion: 评估世界模型时，应直接测试其对训练中未见行动的预测，而不应把常规预测精度当作可靠替代指标。简单模型、领域规则和少量随机测试方案都可能有价值；复杂模型是否占优，取决于真实行动效果能否被规则表达。该检验框架可推广到任何需要预测历史之外行动后果的领域。

Abstract: A world model is trusted to answer "what if": how its world responds to an action it has never seen. We give a test of that trust for any action-conditioned world model: actions held out of training by design and scored against a known answer; whether forecast accuracy predicts accuracy on those actions; and the same structure given to simpler models. We run it in retail, where the action is a price and promotion plan, across 2,853 fitted models. First, forecast accuracy is a fair guide between kinds of model (rank correlation with error on new plans 0.32 to 0.66) and a poor guide between versions of one world model: where merchants plan into high-demand weeks the link reverses, down to -0.69. Second, training the world model to learn the effect of actions (JEPA pretraining, a latent consistency check, adaptation at the plan date) leaves its error on new plans unchanged. Third, in generated worlds the recorded regular price and three price rules improve it. Given the same rules, a log-linear model is best when its curve can match the truth; in a world whose curve lies outside the rules, the world model with the rules is ahead of the log-linear model with the rules in 36 of 40 worlds, and does as well without its recurrent cell. On real data (Breakfast at the Frat, The Complete Journey and M5) the rules lower the world model's error on pure price cuts deeper than any it saw (0.63 to 0.56 on the Frat; 0.45 to 0.44, within chance, on The Complete Journey), but not on lasting price moves. Fourth, a small share of random test plans helps every model a little. In a generated dosing world, a stress test in a second domain, the first finding repeats, and the built-up drug level lowers the world model's error (2.86 to 1.91) though it still predicts only 0.12 to 0.32 of the true gain. The test carries to any domain where a world model is asked about actions outside its history.

</details>


### [254] [The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23127)
*Basavaraj R M,Harshal Chandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建二十万条图文问答压力测试数据，研究检索文本与图像证据冲突时视觉语言模型如何权衡两者，并发现不同模型存在明显的文本偏重或图像偏重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强视觉语言模型可能同时受到错误检索文本和受损图像的影响。以往评测多关注性能下降，较少系统分析模型如何整合两种证据，也难以区分模态偏差与概念先验的作用。

Method: 作者构建了包含二十万条样本的数据集，基于图像问答素材设置检索噪声、视觉模糊与低资源裁剪，以及带反事实翻转对的跨模态冲突。依据理想贝叶斯证据组合推导可检验预测，形式化定义文本过度加权的“知识惯性”和图像过度加权的“感官捕获”；通过校正测量误差的回归估计证据权重，并验证其能恢复已知权重。反事实翻转分解用于区分模态偏差与概念先验，另提出“接地幻觉分数”衡量正确答案中有多少并非由图像证据支撑。研究在固定随机种子的二千条样本上评测四种开源模型，并测试一种无需训练的一致性检查。

Result: 四种模型在干净输入上的准确率为百分之九十二点五至百分之九十四点二，但在不利条件下降至百分之四十一点三至百分之五十九点九。两种拉瓦模型偏重文本，文本与图像权重比分别为一点二二九和一点三二二；国际视觉三代偏重图像，权重比为零点六八零。检索文档提供错误信息时，各模型原本答对的样本中，有百分之三十四点八至百分之七十一点四会放弃正确答案。一致性检查能在误导性文档下以降低覆盖率换取准确率，但图像严重退化时，直接退回图像判断会失效。

Conclusion: 模型对检索文本与图像的权衡存在显著差异，且干净输入上的高准确率并不保证答案真正以图像为依据。所提出的数据集、权重估计与诊断指标为评估跨模态证据整合提供了系统方法；一致性检查虽有助于应对错误检索，但面对严重视觉退化仍有局限。作者公开了逐样本输出、代码及可复算所有报告数值的审计材料。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) answer from two sources of evidence that can each fail: the retrieved text can be wrong or noisy, and the image can be degraded or contradicted. We introduce GIB-200K, 200,000 image-question items built from COCO and Visual Genome, each expanded into controlled stress conditions: hard-negative retrieval noise (1-20 documents), visual ambiguity (Gaussian and motion blur, low-resource crops) and cross-modal conflict with counterfactual flip pairs. Instead of only measuring degradation, we ask how models combine the two modalities. From ideal Bayesian cue combination we derive a testable prediction that yields formal definitions of Knowledge Inertia (text over-weighted relative to the model's own image evidence) and Sensory Capture (the reverse), and we estimate the weights with a measurement-error-corrected regression whose recovery of known weights we verify. A counterfactual flip decomposition separates modality bias from concept priors, and a Grounding Illusion Score measures how much clean accuracy is not grounded in the image. We evaluate 4 open models on one frozen, seeded evaluation split of 2,000 GIB-200K items. All models are accurate on clean inputs (92.5%-94.2%) but adverse accuracy falls to 41.3%-59.9%. The two LLaVA models over-weight the text (Knowledge Inertia, w_T/w_I = 1.229 and 1.322), whereas InternVL3 favours the image (w_T/w_I = 0.680); between 34.8% and 71.4% of the clean answers that are correct are abandoned when the document lies. A training-free consistency check that compares the answer with and without retrieval trades coverage for accuracy under misleading documents, while falling back to the image fails when the image is heavily degraded. All per-instance outputs, code, and an audit that recomputes every reported open-model number are released.

</details>


### [255] [Physically Admissible Generative Flow Matching for Sparse Flow Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22549)
*Yuhao Wang,Yuanxi Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可行子空间一致性框架，使流场生成过程的每个中间状态都满足离散守恒约束，并提升稀疏观测重建的准确性与不确定性可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、局部观测重建完整流体速度场是严重病态的逆问题：大部分空间未被观测，同时重建结果必须严格满足离散守恒规律，并提供可信的不确定性估计。现有生成方法通常只在最终输出施加物理约束，或使用软惩罚，因此生成轨迹中的中间状态可能违反物理约束。

Method: 提出可行子空间一致性框架，将物理可行性作为连续生成动力学的不变量。通过切空间匹配，使整个常微分方程生成轨迹保持在离散守恒流形上；通过质量正交坐标保持动能度量。稀疏重建则在冻结的可行先验潜空间中进行摊销后验传输，使每个后验样本无需逐样本投影，也能满足不可压缩性与边界条件。该方法在周期性科尔莫戈罗夫流中使用精确谱投影验证，并在含浸入式障碍物的圆柱尾流中采用矩阵无关的迭代投影。

Result: 在周期流实验中，该框架避免了末端投影导致的模态覆盖崩溃，模态覆盖率由零提高至零点七九，并消除了高波数谱失真。在稀疏重建实验中，相比未条件化先验，全局相对误差分别降低百分之五十和百分之四十六，传感器残差分别降低百分之六十三和百分之五十五；同时得到经校准且随空间位置变化的不确定性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将物理约束落实为生成轨迹全程保持的结构不变量，可兼顾严格物理可行性、流场重建精度、生成多样性与可信不确定性，为稀疏观测下的流体场重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing full fluid velocity fields from sparse, localized observations is a severely ill-posed inverse problem: most of the spatial domain remains unobserved, discrete conservation laws must hold strictly, and predictive uncertainty must be reliable. Existing generative models typically enforce physical constraints only at the terminal output or via soft penalties, leaving intermediate generative trajectories unconstrained. We introduce Feasible Subspace Consistency (FSC), a framework that preserves physical admissibility as an invariant of continuous generative dynamics: tangent-space matching confines the entire ODE trajectory to the discrete conservation manifold, while mass-orthonormal coordinates preserve the kinetic-energy metric. Sparse reconstruction then reduces to amortized posterior transport in the latent space of a frozen FSC prior, ensuring every posterior sample inherits incompressibility and boundary conditions without per-sample projection loops. We validate FSC on a periodic Kolmogorov flow, where exact spectral projection isolates path tangency and metric isometry, and a wall-bounded cylinder wake with immersed obstacles, realized via matrix-free iterative projection. FSC prevents the mode-coverage collapse induced by terminal projection (increasing mode coverage from $0.000$ to $0.790$) while eliminating high-wavenumber spectral distortion. In sparse reconstruction, amortized FSC reduces global relative error by $50\%$ and $46\%$, and sensor residuals by $63\%$ and $55\%$, relative to the unconditioned prior, while delivering calibrated, spatially heterogeneous uncertainty.

</details>


### [256] [Ambient LLMs: Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-tuning via Temperature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23229)
*Giannis Daras,Marios Papachristou,Adam Klivans,Antonio Torralba,Constantinos Daskalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“环境式监督微调”，利用预训练模型的软预测约束微调模型，在多个任务上优于标准监督微调，平均相对提升最高达百分之二十一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、多样化的大规模后训练数据难以收集，监督微调数据通常远少于预训练数据，容易造成过拟合，并使模型遗忘数据分布中的低频内容。论文旨在借助预训练模型保留这些知识，缓解微调导致的能力退化。

Method: 冻结预训练模型作为带噪教师，要求微调模型在较高温度下生成的条件概率分布，与教师模型对应的高温分布大致一致。温度用于调节对预训练模型的信任程度：温度趋于无穷时，该约束退化为标准监督微调；温度为一时，则对应知识蒸馏。

Result: 在领域适配、指令微调和推理任务上，该方法均优于标准监督微调，平均相对提升最高达百分之二十一。

Conclusion: 环境式监督微调通过在粗粒度上保留预训练模型的分布信息，为小规模后训练数据提供有效补充，有助于降低过拟合和遗忘，并提升多类任务的微调效果。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale, diverse, and high-quality post-training datasets for autoregressive language models is challenging. Consequently, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is typically applied to datasets significantly smaller than those used for pre-training, which leads to overfitting and the catastrophic forgetting of the tails of the data distribution. To prevent this collapse, we introduce Ambient SFT, a principled framework for leveraging imperfect predictions to augment the fine-tuning set. In our method, a "frozen" version of the pre-trained model serves as a noisy teacher that the fine-tuned model must match at a coarse level. In particular, we require that the high-temperature versions of the conditional distributions produced by the post-training model should match the high-temperature versions of the pre-training ones. The temperature hyperparameter controls the level of trust in the pre-trained model, with the limit $T\to \infty$ reverting back to regular SFT and $T=1$ to knowledge distillation. We demonstrate that our approach outperforms standard SFT across domain adaptation, instruction-tuning, and reasoning, leading for up to $21\%$ average relative improvement to standard SFT.

</details>


### [257] [ReMaRL: Learning to Unmask and Remask in Frozen Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22576)
*Egor Shibaev,Danila Biktimirov,Ivan Klimov,Artem Makoian,Viacheslav Meshchaninov,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量级强化学习策略，联合决定扩散语言模型中哪些词元应解掩码、保留或重新掩码，从而提升数学与代码任务的最终答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型并行生成词元，解掩码顺序会影响答案准确性。现有方法虽能学习解掩码策略或通过重新掩码修正早期预测，但启发式规则与监督式错误检测器都没有直接针对最终答案正确性优化这些决策。

Method: 提出重新掩码强化学习策略。该策略约含三十万个参数，在扩散模型保持冻结的情况下，利用最终答案奖励和组相对策略优化训练，使其学习对词元进行解掩码、保留或重新掩码的决策。

Result: 在拉达八十亿指令模型上，所提方法在测试的推理预算范围内，于数学任务的四至三十二次模型调用、代码任务的八至十六次模型调用中，取得了参与比较的解掩码与重新掩码策略中的最高分数。

Conclusion: 直接依据最终答案奖励优化重新掩码决策，有望有效提升扩散语言模型的生成质量。该方法参数量较小，且无需更新基础模型；不过，摘要仅报告特定模型、任务和推理预算下的比较结果。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models generate tokens in parallel, and the order in which tokens are unmasked affects final-answer accuracy. Several methods learn unmasking policies from task rewards, but these policies cannot remask earlier predictions. Remasking lets the model correct those predictions using newly generated context. Heuristic rules guide both unmasking and remasking, while supervised remaskers learn to detect token errors. Neither approach directly optimizes these decisions for final-answer correctness. To address this gap, we introduce ReMaRL (Remasking with Reinforcement Learning), a lightweight policy that learns which tokens to unmask, retain, or remask. We train this roughly 0.3M-parameter policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) from final-answer rewards while keeping the diffusion model frozen. With LLaDA-8B-Instruct, ReMaRL achieves the highest scores among the compared unmasking and remasking policies within the tested inference budgets of 4-32 model calls on math and 8-16 calls on code.

</details>


### [258] [Lost in the Funnel: Selection-Induced Miscalibration in Ranking and Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22595)
*Aditya Somani,Rahul Ragesh,Vikas Joshi,Amit Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，模型边界处校准良好的分数，经过多阶段筛选和决策后可能失准；提出面向决策环节的再校准方法，在生产测试中显著降低决策时校准误差并提升拍卖效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代排序与决策系统会将模型分数用于筛选、重排、业务规则和拍卖等多个环节，但校准通常只在分数生成处检查。选择机制可能改变分数所对应的结果概率，使边界处的良好校准无法延续到实际决策时。论文旨在区分模型自身误差与选择效应，并厘清完美校准的边界分数在后续环节可能出现的问题。

Method: 论文建立逐边界诊断框架，利用残差关系分析校准在各环节的变化，并以规则评判的相关性作为目标，避免点击率等受展示方式影响的指标。基于行业案例中发现的选择效应，作者提出漏斗感知再校准：在分数实际驱动决策的边界处，结合选择策略进行事后再校准。研究还通过双区域线上随机对照测试和改造后的公开数据集检验方法及其作用机制。

Result: 行业案例显示，决策时校准恶化可由选择效应而非模型误差导致。线上测试中，该方法将决策时的期望校准误差降低了百分之六十至八十，并提升拍卖效率；改造后的两个公开数据集进一步为相关机制提供了证据。

Conclusion: 多阶段系统不能仅凭模型输出边界的校准结果判断决策时是否可靠，必须在分数被实际使用的环节重新诊断。将选择策略纳入边界级再校准，有助于恢复决策时的概率可靠性，并改善下游效率。论文还提出了多阶段评分系统校准的实践原则，并计划公开改造数据集及其生成流程。

Abstract: Modern ranking and decision systems combine components with divergent objectives, and scores calibrated at the model boundary are used as calibrated probabilities throughout the funnel, across filtering, reranking, business-rule enforcement, and auctions. Yet calibration is typically validated only where the score is produced, so its breakdown where the score is used is routinely overlooked. We ask when calibration survives this selection, how inherited error differs from selection effects, and what can fail for a perfectly calibrated score at a component boundary. We study these questions through a boundary-wise diagnostic framework of residual identities. We target rubric-judged relevance rather than presentation-dependent metrics such as CTR, so calibration is measurable at every funnel layer of interest. We first present an industry-wide case study that applies our diagnosis to settings where calibration deteriorates at decision time, tracing the degradation to selection rather than model error. This diagnosis motivates Funnel-Aware Recalibration (FAR): post-hoc, selection-policy-aware recalibration at the boundary where scores drive decisions. In a live two-region production A/B test, FAR cuts decision-time expected calibration error (ECE) by 60-80% and improves auction efficiency. We further adapt two public datasets to simulate industry settings, providing additional evidence for the mechanism. We plan to release the adapted datasets and their generation pipeline to support further research. Finally, we distill these findings into practical operating principles for calibrating multi-stage scoring systems.

</details>


### [259] [DLS: Dual-Logic Graph Neural Networks with Chaos-Fuzzy Residuals for Robust Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22607)
*Mohammad Mehdi Keikha,Abolfazl Nadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向图链接预测的归纳式框架，通过混沌特征注入与模糊逻辑门控，缓解过平滑并提升异配图上的预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递神经网络在多层递归聚合中容易抑制高频信息，导致节点表示趋同，即过平滑。该问题在异配图中尤为突出，因为相邻节点未必具有相似特征。论文旨在提升节点表示的区分能力，同时避免引入过多噪声。

Method: 提出双逻辑采样框架，采用动态残差处理流程。其一，混沌注入模块利用逻辑斯蒂映射对特征表示进行多样化，借助其对初始条件的敏感性，区分拓扑相近但语义不同的节点。其二，模糊逻辑门控机制通过软阈值筛除混沌动态带来的不确定噪声，并保留置信度较高的信号。

Result: 摘要称，该方法在多种基准数据集上实现了较好的特征区分性与鲁棒性，在异配图任务中显著优于先进基线方法，并在同配图上保持了有竞争力的效率。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或数值，无法进一步量化比较幅度。

Conclusion: 该方法尝试以受控的不稳定性突破传统消息传递模型的过平滑限制，并通过门控机制平衡特征多样性与抗噪能力。摘要所述结果显示其对异配图链接预测具有潜力；其实际优势仍需结合完整实验、消融分析及混沌注入的稳定性评估加以验证。

Abstract: Link prediction is a fundamental task in graph mining, critical for applications ranging from biological interaction discovery to recommender systems. While Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) have achieved state-of-the-art results, they are fundamentally limited by the inadvertent suppression of high-frequency signals, as the recursive aggregation that stabilizes structural learning inherently leads to over-smoothing, rendering node representations indistinguishable in deep layers. This limitation is particularly severe in heterophilous graphs, where structural proximity does not imply feature similarity. In this paper, we propose DLS (Dual-Logic SAGE), a novel inductive framework designed to break the over-smoothing barrier via a "controlled instability" mechanism. Unlike conventional methods relying on static structural heuristics, DLS introduces a dynamic residual pipeline. First, a Chaotic Injection Module utilizes the Logistic Map to diversify the feature manifold, exploiting sensitivity to initial conditions to separate topologically similar but semantically distinct nodes. Second, to mitigate the aleatoric uncertainty introduced by chaotic dynamics, a Fuzzy-Logic Gating Mechanism applies soft-thresholding to filter noise and retain only high-confidence signals. Experimental results on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DLS effectively balances feature discrimination with robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in heterophilous settings while maintaining competitive efficiency on standard homophilous graphs.

</details>


### [260] [The Price of Hard Coverage Constraints in Online Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22646)
*Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画了在线预测中持续满足累计失覆盖预算所需付出的最小效率代价：不同评估方式下，该代价分别随轮数呈对数、立方根和平方根增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测虽提供分布无关的覆盖保证，但在逐轮部署时，失覆盖次数仍可能大幅波动。若要求累计失覆盖数在整个部署期间始终不超过预设预算，通常需要扩大预测集；此前尚不清楚这种可靠性要求必然带来多少额外代价。

Method: 研究逐轮生成预测集的在线问题，以超过总体最优预测集大小的累计部分作为效率代价。在关于覆盖率与预测集大小关系的适当正则条件下，推导满足硬性累计失覆盖约束的在线方法所必须承担的最低代价，并证明仅利用过去观测到的分数、无需知道分数分布，也能达到相应增长率。

Result: 即使数据独立同分布、分数分布已知且总体最优预测集完全已知，额外代价仍不可避免。轮数为Ｔ时，若评估预先指定时点的期望代价，最低量级为对数Ｔ；若评估截至Ｔ轮内各预先指定时点的最大期望代价，量级为Ｔ的三分之一次方；若评估事后挑选时点所对应的最坏实际代价的期望，量级为Ｔ的平方根。

Conclusion: 连续可靠性并非没有成本：即便拥有完美的统计知识，硬性控制累计失覆盖仍要求扩大预测集。论文既给出了这种效率损失的基本下界，也说明无需预知分数分布即可达到相应量级。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification, but in sequential deployment, standard coverage guarantees can still allow substantial fluctuations in the number of miscoverages over time. We study an online setting in which prediction sets are produced one round at a time and the cumulative number of miscoverages must stay within a prescribed budget throughout deployment.Such a hard coverage guarantee can be enforced by enlarging prediction sets, but how much additional prediction-set size is fundamentally necessary remains unclear. We characterize this unavoidable efficiency cost over $T$ prediction rounds. Remarkably, the cost persists even with i.i.d.\ data, a known score distribution, and perfect knowledge of the population-optimal prediction set. Under suitable regularity conditions relating coverage and prediction-set size, we determine the minimum cumulative excess prediction-set size required by any online method satisfying the hard coverage constraint. This minimum cost is $Θ(\log T)$ for expected cost at a fixed time known in advance, $Θ(T^{1/3})$ for the largest expected cost over all fixed times up to $T$, and $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ for the expected worst realized cost when the evaluation time is chosen in hindsight. We further show that these rates can be attained using past scores, without knowing the score distribution. Thus, even perfect statistical knowledge does not make sequential reliability free.

</details>


### [261] [AlignedHPO: Efficient Surrogate-Guided Loss Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22658)
*Ivan Karpukhin,Andrey Savchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“对齐式超参数优化”框架，利用可微评估指标代理和梯度对齐，高效调整损失函数中的超参数；实验显示其性能具有竞争力，超梯度计算速度接近提升十二倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习损失函数包含权重、尺度和间隔等多个超参数，通常依赖人工调整或昂贵的黑盒搜索。尽管这些超参数具有可微性，现有方法仍未充分利用梯度信息，且优化目标可能与实际任务评估指标不一致。

Method: 该框架以可微评估指标的代理目标引导超参数优化，并通过梯度对齐，使调整方向贴近任务评估标准。它使用雅可比—向量积降低超梯度计算的时间与内存开销；同时通过归一化对齐缓解梯度幅值不确定性，并以梯度平滑减轻随机波动。

Result: 在广义焦点损失和弧面角度损失上的实验表明，该方法能够适应长尾分类与开放集人脸识别任务。与隐式微分方法相比，其性能具有竞争力或有所提升，超梯度计算速度接近提升十二倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合任务指标代理、梯度对齐与高效超梯度计算，可以有效优化可微损失函数的超参数，为替代人工调参和高成本黑盒搜索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern deep learning losses involve multiple hyperparameters, such as weights, scales, and margins, typically tuned manually or through expensive black-box optimization despite their differentiability. We propose AlignedHPO, an efficient framework for tuning differentiable loss hyperparameters using gradient alignment and surrogate validation objectives. Our method employs Jacobian-vector products to reduce hypergradient computation time and memory, while differentiable metric surrogates guide optimization toward task-specific evaluation criteria. We address gradient magnitude ambiguity and stochastic instability through normalized alignment and gradient smoothing. Experiments with generalized Focal Loss and ArcFace demonstrate effective adaptation across long-tailed classification and open-set face recognition. AlignedHPO achieves competitive or improved performance compared to implicit differentiation, with nearly 12x faster hypergradient computation. These results highlight the potential of efficient surrogate-guided loss hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [262] [CULypso: A Clustered Federated Unlearning paradigm for Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22682)
*Eleftheria Chini,George Margaritis,Dimitrios Katsaros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向车联网的分簇式联邦遗忘算法，利用车辆网络的层级结构与移动特性降低遗忘开销；实验显示其综合表现优于现有遗忘方法和完整重训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据保护法规要求机器学习系统能够删除特定用户数据所产生的影响。然而，在车辆高速移动、通信条件易变且任务时效性强的车联网中执行遗忘，面临通信、计算资源与时间成本方面的挑战，相关研究仍较少。

Method: 提出一种面向分簇车联网的近似、定向、分层联邦遗忘算法。该方法利用网络拓扑和车辆移动特征组织遗忘过程，力求在减少通信与资源消耗的同时，消除指定客户端对全局模型更新的影响。

Result: 摘要报告称，该方法相较于一种现有联邦遗忘方案，在耗时、服务器存储负载和遗忘保真度方面约有百分之六十五的综合改进，并显著优于完整重训练基线。摘要未提供具体实验设置、指标定义或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 分层联邦遗忘与车联网的分簇结构相结合，有望更高效地满足车辆网络中的数据删除需求。实验结果支持该方案的潜力，但其实际适用性仍需结合具体网络条件及更详细的评估结果加以验证。

Abstract: Unlearning has emerged as an essential security and privacy-preserving solution in Machine Learning, enabling networks to support their clients' rights for data erasure, as mandated by data protection regulations. Introducing such a non-trivial task to an edge, highly mobile and time-sensitive vehicular network is an even greater, largely unexplored challenge. This article argues that a hierarchical Federated Unlearning approach could be aptly integrated into vehicular ad-hoc networks (VANETs), leveraging topology and movement to reduce communication, resource and time costs, while effectively forgetting a client's influence on the global model updates. In order to achieve this, we propose CULypso, an approximate, surgical, hierarchical unlearning algorithm tailored for clustered VANETs. Inspired by the nymph in Greek mythology, Calypso, whose name translates to 'she who conceals the knowledge', CULypso, with the capital letters referencing \textbf{C}lustered \textbf{U}n\textbf{L}earning, aims to achieve exactly what its name suggests: conceal, erase or forget in our case, knowledge it contains. Extensive experiments uphold the potency of our paradigm with it performing approximately 65\% better than FedEraser in terms of time, server storage load, and unlearning fidelity while utterly outperforming baseline full retraining.

</details>


### [263] [HADVQ: Hessian Aware Directional Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22721)
*Daniil Li,Mars Davletshin,Vadim Klyzhenko,Artem Danilishin,Valery Sandul,Daniil Ryazanov,Ekaterina Muravleva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向超低比特大语言模型的向量量化方法，通过随机预处理、极坐标分解、基于八维格子的结构化码本，以及少量敏感通道保留，减轻激进压缩造成的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特量化会严重扰动模型权重，而少数高度敏感的通道往往承受不成比例的精度损害。如何在极低存储成本下更有效地表示权重并保护关键通道，是本文关注的问题。

Method: 首先对权重进行随机预处理，使权重空间更适合量化；随后将权重转换到极坐标表示，将方向与幅度分开量化。方向量化利用随机预处理所形成的结构，并采用基于八维格子的紧凑码本。此外，借助代理海森矩阵识别极其重要的通道，并额外保留这些通道，成本仅增加百分之零点一六比特。

Result: 摘要称，实验结果显示该方法能够提升量化后的模型精度，表明几何结构利用、格子码本设计与选择性通道保留相结合，在超低比特量化中具有优势；摘要未提供具体数据或比较对象。

Conclusion: 该方法通过同时改善权重表示方式和保护少数关键通道，为大语言模型的超低比特量化提供了一条有效路径。其核心价值在于以较小额外成本缓解激进压缩带来的性能退化。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit quantization of large language models is a challenging task because aggressive compression severely distorts model parameters and disproportionately harms a small number of highly sensitive channels. We present a vector quantization method tailored for this regime. Our approach begins with random preconditioning, which regularizes the weight space and makes it more amenable to quantization. We then represent weights in polar coordinates and decouple the quantization of directions and magnitudes. To quantize directions, we leverage the structure induced by random preconditioning and design a structured codebook based on the $E_8$ lattice, yielding a compact yet expressive representation. To further reduce degradation, we detect extremely important channels using a proxy Hessian and preserve them with an additional cost of only 0.16 bits. Together, these components provide an effective quantization pipeline for LLMs in the extremely low-bit regime. Empirical results demonstrate improved accuracy, highlighting the benefit of combining geometric structure, lattice-based codebooks, and selective channel preservation.

</details>


### [264] [LoopPrune: Recurrent Pruning for Looped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22757)
*Weizhong Huang,Huaijia Kou,Yan Zhang,Fei Chao,Xiawu Zheng,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向循环扩散变换器的剪枝与恢复框架，在减少参数、显存占用和推理耗时的同时，尽量保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环扩散变换器通过重复使用共享模块，以较少参数获得更深计算能力，但重复执行注意力与多层感知机仍带来较高计算开销。现有免训练剪枝难以衡量模块在不同循环位置上的作用；依赖训练的方案则缺少对位置相关误差及其后续影响的明确约束。

Method: 提出“环剪枝”框架，包含三个关键环节：一是联合评估移除共享结构的整体影响及其在不同循环位置上的贡献差异，据此选择剪枝结构；二是汇集各循环位置的输入统计信息，重构共享权重以补偿剪枝造成的损失；三是在相同学生模型状态下比较师生模型的完整去噪预测，并匹配文本引导信号，以固定的稠密教师模型为参照更新学生模型的全部参数。

Result: 在四种开源模型、六项基准和三种剪枝范式上的实验显示，该方法具有较好效果。对环式扩散变换器B/16进行百分之三十的结构化参数剪枝后，其六项基准平均准确率达到稠密模型的百分之九十三点一四，比最佳基线高九点四九个百分点。对轻量扩散模型进行剪枝后，在三种推理部署框架中，峰值已分配显存降低百分之三十四点三六至四十四点六四，去噪速度提升一点四六至一点七四倍。

Conclusion: 该研究针对循环共享结构的特殊性设计了重要性估计、权重重构和位置级恢复机制，缓解了剪枝效率与生成质量之间的矛盾。实验表明，该方法能够在多种模型与部署环境中有效降低资源需求，并保持较强的模型性能。

Abstract: Looped diffusion Transformers reuse shared blocks to deepen computation and match larger models with fewer parameters, reducing storage and deployment burdens. Yet repeated attention and MLP computations remain costly. Pruning reduces storage and computation by removing redundant parameters or units. Training-free methods struggle to resolve recurrent contributions, while training-based methods lack explicit constraints on position-specific errors affecting later inputs and final outputs. We propose LoopPrune, a pruning and recovery framework addressing these requirements. Recurrent importance estimation combines shared-removal effects and position variation to select a shared structure; shared reconstruction pools recurrent input statistics to compensate weights. Loop-position recovery compares complete denoising predictions at identical student states and matches text guidance, updating all student parameters against a fixed dense teacher. Extensive experiments across four open-source models, six benchmarks, and three pruning paradigms demonstrate effectiveness. At 30% structured parameter pruning, Looped-DiT B/16 retains 93.14% of dense six-benchmark average accuracy, exceeding the best baseline by 9.49%. Across PyTorch, vLLM-Omni, and SGLang Diffusion, pruned SDXSv3 reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 34.36-44.64% and accelerates denoising 1.46-1.74x over dense models.

</details>


### [265] [PANO: Parallel Latent Space Neural Operator for Solving PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22782)
*Zijiang Yang,Xiaomeng Wu,Chen Kuang,Dongmei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并行潜在空间神经算子，通过多个并行压缩表示共同建模物理交互，在九项基准测试中取得先进表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变换器的神经算子通常采用单一模式将大量观测压缩为少量潜在标记。单一压缩模式可能遗漏部分物理信息，使这些信息无法参与潜在空间中的交互，进而限制模型对复杂物理过程的表达能力。

Method: 提出并行潜在空间神经算子，将观测分别映射为多个不同的压缩表示，并以共享参数在各潜在空间中并行学习物理交互，最后融合解码结果以预测目标物理场。方法还设计了耦合分配投影，用于构造双向一致的编码与解码映射；并提出成对潜在空间注意力层，通过自适应跨空间交互联合更新各空间的潜在表示。

Result: 在涵盖不同物理系统、几何形状和观测规模的九项基准测试中，该方法取得了当前先进的性能。与既有方法相比，在单侧冲压任务中，等效塑性应变预测误差至少降低百分之二十八点三；在飞机任务中，升力系数预测误差至少降低百分之二十一点四。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用多个互补的潜在空间并联合建模其间交互，可以缓解单一压缩模式造成的物理信息损失，提升神经算子求解偏微分方程的能力。实验结果支持该方法在多类物理场预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Aggregating observations into a limited number of latent tokens and learning physical interactions in the latent space has emerged as an effective approach for Transformer-based neural operators to solve Partial Differential Equation (PDE) problems involving large-scale observations. However, existing methods typically compress observations using a single aggregation pattern, making physical information not retained by that pattern invisible to the latent space and thereby constraining physical interactions within it. To address this limitation, we propose PArallel Latent Space Neural Operator (PANO), a novel neural operator for solving PDEs that formulates physical interactions among observations as an additive combination of interaction results from parallel latent spaces with shared parameters. Specifically, PANO transforms observations into multiple distinct compressed representations through independent mappings, learns physical interactions in parallel based on these representations, and finally fuses the decoded results to predict the target physical field. Within PANO, we further propose Coupled Assignment Projection (CAP) to construct bidirectionally consistent encoding and decoding mappings, and Pairwise Latent Space Attention Layer (PaLA) to jointly update latent representations of each latent space through adaptive cross-space interactions. Extensive experiments on nine benchmarks spanning diverse physical systems, geometries, and observation scales demonstrate that PANO achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, PANO reduces equivalent plastic strain prediction errors on Unilateral Stamping by 28.3% (or more) and lift-coefficient prediction errors on AirCraft by 21.4% (or more) compared with existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/ZijiangY1116/PANO.

</details>


### [266] [Virtual Double Oracle: Faster Convergence by Leveraging Strategies That Were Watching All Along](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22806)
*Ruilan Wang,Francisco Aristi Reina,Katerina Papadaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出虚拟双重预言机方法，通过历史信息为新发现的策略赋予更合适的初始权重；其折扣版本在多类博弈实验中降低了有限轮次下的可剥削性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重预言机类方法通过逐步加入最佳响应来扩展策略群体。在线方法通常在每次扩展时将新策略的权重重置为均匀先验，忽略了该策略若从学习开始便已存在时应积累的历史信息。论文关注这一尚未充分研究的重新初始化设计问题。

Method: 虚拟双重预言机利用双线性恒等关系：策略对手方历史策略序列的累计损失，可由其对时间平均对手策略的效用概括。因此，新策略加入时可据此计算它若从一开始参与乘法权重更新所应具有的权重。折扣版虚拟双重预言机则弱化历史偏好的影响，以缓解重新初始化时权重过度集中的问题。

Result: 理论上，在加入策略的累计成本至多随策略群体规模线性增长的条件下，虚拟双重预言机在不断扩展的已发现策略群体上达到可剥削性上界，量级为策略群体规模与学习轮数之比平方根的倒数。实验涵盖矩阵博弈、扑克和古夫斯皮尔，结果显示，采用合适折扣的版本可显著降低有限学习轮次下的可剥削性，并改善发现到的策略群体。

Conclusion: 策略扩展时如何初始化新策略，是影响在线求解效果的重要设计因素。利用历史信息进行虚拟初始化具有理论保证，而适度折扣还能在实践中提升有限轮次表现；理论保证则依赖策略加入成本满足线性增长条件。

Abstract: Double Oracle (DO) and PSRO approximate Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games by iteratively expanding a strategy population with best responses supplied by an oracle. Online Double Oracle (ODO) interleaves multiplicative weights updates (MWU) with population discovery and obtains an $O(\sqrt{k\ln k/T})$ rate, where $T$ is the number of learning rounds and $k$ is the larger of the two players' final population sizes. We identify the reinitialisation step as an underexplored design choice: at each expansion, MWU-based methods reset newly discovered strategies to a uniform prior. We introduce Virtual Double Oracle (VDO), which exploits a bilinear identity (a strategy's full hindsight loss history collapses to its utility against the time-averaged opponent) to initialise each new strategy with the MWU weight it would have had from the start. We prove that VDO achieves $O(\sqrt{k/T})$ exploitability over the discovered population as it grows, provided the cumulative cost of adding strategies is at most linear in $k$, a condition supported by our empirical measurements. Discounted VDO (dVDO) moderates historical preferences to reduce over-concentration at reinitialisation. Experiments across matrix games, poker, and Goofspiel show that, with a suitable discount, dVDO can substantially reduce finite-horizon exploitability relative to both VDO and uniform-reset ODO, and can improve the discovered strategy populations.

</details>


### [267] [SilverSpoon-KD: A General-Purpose Toolkit for Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22842)
*Xaver R. Davey,David Broman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一款开源知识蒸馏工具包，统一多种蒸馏范式与模型架构关系，并提供可扩展的通用分块蒸馏实现；实验显示其性能与现有工具包相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏可将教师模型的表征迁移至学生模型，以减小模型规模或降低推理成本。但现有工具包支持的蒸馏方法和应用场景有限，难以跟上研究方法的发展，也缺少统一、通用的分块蒸馏实现。

Method: 作者开发了名为“银勺知识蒸馏”的开源深度学习工具包，以单一接口支持不同蒸馏范式及模型架构关系，并实现具备可扩展特性的通用分块知识蒸馏。该工具包面向视觉、掩码语言模型和因果语言模型，覆盖同构、异构及量化蒸馏等设置。

Result: 实验将该工具包与现有工具包进行比较，评估吞吐量和显存使用等性能指标。结果表明，尽管工具包提供了便利功能、指标计算和实验追踪，其性能仍与现有工具包相当或更好。

Conclusion: 该工具包旨在降低知识蒸馏方法的实现与应用门槛，并为跨任务、跨架构的蒸馏提供统一支持。摘要报告的结果表明，它在功能覆盖较广的同时未牺牲运行性能；但具体提升幅度和适用边界尚需结合论文正文判断。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers deep learning representations from a teacher model to a student model, typically reducing model size or inference cost. However, knowledge distillation tooling has not kept pace with the breadth of research methods: existing toolkits typically support only a limited subset of techniques and use cases. We present SilverSpoon-KD, an open-source PyTorch toolkit that unifies different distillation paradigms and architectural relationships across workloads and applications behind a single API. Moreover, SilverSpoon-KD offers the first general-purpose blockwise knowledge distillation implementation with unique scalability features. We validate its versatility across vision, masked language model, and causal language models on tasks spanning homogeneous, heterogeneous, and quantization distillation across paradigms. We also evaluate SilverSpoon-KD by comparing its performance, including throughput and GPU memory usage, with existing toolkits. Despite extensive support for convenience, metric calculation, and tracking, we have found SilverSpoon-KD's performance to be on par with or better than existing toolkits.

</details>


### [268] [WATERFALL: Workflow for Adaptive Training with Evolutionary Reward Formulation and Automated Learning Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22854)
*Eleftherios Triantafyllidis,Filippos Christianos,Zhibin Li,Bernd Bickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向部分可观测环境的自动化奖励发现流程，通过生成、视觉评估和迭代优化奖励程序，在不依赖特权信息的条件下提升强化学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励、长时程依赖、记忆需求及误导性可供性使奖励设计成为强化学习的主要瓶颈。现有基础模型方法往往依赖任务描述、目标条件、专家示范或真实指标；但在部分可观测环境中，这些信息通常不可获得，任务目标与环境规律需要通过交互自行发现。

Method: 论文提出一种迭代式、基于群体的奖励发现流程：首先通过不同角色设定生成多样化的程序化奖励候选；随后依据原始视觉交互轨迹，使用由视觉语言模型评判的瑞士制进化锦标赛筛选优胜候选；最后利用历次评估记录，对候选进行变异、升级或淘汰。该流程遵守部分可观测环境的信息限制。

Result: 作者在完全可观测及逐步揭示信息的部分可观测环境中进行评估，涵盖离散与连续任务，并与稀疏奖励强化学习、内在动机及基础模型奖励合成方法比较。摘要报告称，该方法无需特权信息即可发现和优化奖励程序，并优于保留各自信息优势的基线，尤其在部分可观测环境中表现突出。消融实验还显示，视觉证据、锦标赛式筛选和迭代优化共同发挥重要作用。

Conclusion: 研究强调，真正的自主奖励发现必须严格遵守环境的可观测性约束，不能借助基准任务特有的额外信息进行奖励塑造。提出的流程为在信息受限环境中自动探索和改进奖励设计提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in sparse-reward, long-horizon and partially observable settings requiring dependency chaining, memory, and deceptive affordance disambiguation. While recent foundation-model approaches reduce manual reward engineering, most assume goal-conditioning, task descriptions, expert demonstrations, or ground-truth metrics. These assumptions are problematic in partially observable environments, where task-relevant objectives, affordances, and dynamics must be discovered through interaction rather than disclosed a priori. We formalise this problem setting by introducing WATERFALL, an iterative population-based workflow for automated reward discovery under the strict observability constraints inherent in POMDP environments. WATERFALL achieves this by (i) synthesising diverse programmatic reward candidates via persona-conditioned generation, (ii) evaluating candidate behaviour from raw visual rollouts utilising a Swiss-system evolutionary tournament judged by vision-language models to identify elites, and (iii) leveraging longitudinal assessment histories to iteratively mutate, escalate, or prune candidates. We evaluate WATERFALL across fully observable and reveal-gated partially observable environments ranging from discrete to continuous, comparing against sparse-reward RL, intrinsic-motivation and foundation-model reward-synthesis baselines. Our results indicate that WATERFALL discovers and refines reward programs without access to privileged information, outperforming baselines that retain their native information advantages, most clearly in the POMDP regime. Extensive ablations substantiate that visual evidence, tournament-based selection, and iterative refinement are jointly important. Enforcing this contract is what separates autonomous reward discovery from benchmark-privileged reward shaping.

</details>


### [269] [Path-Aligned Constraint Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22873)
*Yuhao Wang,Yuanxi Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出路径对齐约束等变框架，通过约束相容的速度场和噪声采样，在生成轨迹中精确保持物理结构约束，同时保留细尺度谱结构与预测多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场超分辨率需要满足散度为零、旋度为零或逐点归一化等结构约束。常规训练目标无法保证这些约束；软惩罚只能在预测精度与约束满足之间折衷，硬约束回归虽可消除约束误差，却容易退化为条件均值预测，从而损失细尺度谱结构。

Method: 作者提出路径对齐约束等变框架。其核心原理是：在线性路径的条件流匹配中，只要初始噪声满足线性约束，且速度场始终位于约束矩阵的零空间，整条传输轨迹就会保持约束。框架通过零空间参数化构造相容速度场，并采用满足约束的噪声采样器；无需修改损失函数或常微分方程积分器。

Result: 在三类约束问题上，该方法相较无约束基线将平均约束残差降低一个至五个数量级，达到数据生成流程本身的残差水平。同时，它能够恢复条件均值回归容易丢失的谱内容与预测多样性。
